On Self-Manifestation · Principle Chapter · The Intention Group-Form · Chapter 16 v1.0

# The Construction Form of the Intention Operating Substrate

## Section One　Purpose and the Single Task

The task of this chapter is to construct explicitly, in a real, runnable Sedimentation-Field dynamical system that already has a legal Intention structure, an operating substrate in which source drive, action, Desire-Gate switching, tuning and cross-time dynamics actually run in the field; in which the readings of Intention qualification, current action, Desire-Gate switching and cross-time dynamics used earlier by the Intention Group-Form are all obtained from real running history; and in which the interfaces that The Mathematical Form of Intention, The Mathematical Form of Intention-Action, The Mathematical Form of Intention Desire-Gate Switching and The Mathematical Form of Intention-Dynamics require the operating substrate to provide, the verdict routing of The Determination Form of Intention, and the boundary instances handed down by The Limit Form of Intention all have counterparts on one and the same substrate; and thereby to show item by item that these objects are not dangling symbols, while declaring truthfully which parts reach only interface check or structural compatibility, or are not yet modeled.

The overall construction is written:

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Spec\_IB = Augment(Spec\_ProbB, A\_I),  A\_I = A\_I^run ∪ A\_I^read

A\_I^run is the conservative running increment on the intention side: the source-drive segment, the gate-passage segment and the post-gate tuning segment of Intention are directed paths in the field and sediment through the traces left by actual traversal; the opening and closing of a source-drive session are registered as running commands; the intention-side Avail acts only on the availability of the five intention-side edges. A\_I^read is the read-only increment on the intention side: qualification readings, action readings, switching readings, legal-segment readings on the unified running certificate, the cross-time classification of source drive and action, the derivation of carrier and Probability-Dynamics, the solvability conditions of the several Mathematical Forms, verdict routing and profile comparison. The increment layer does not rewrite, replace or bypass the causal chain of the running layer and the probability layer.

Intention, Intention-Action, Intention Desire-Gate switching and Intention-Dynamics are not redefined here, and the preceding fifteen chapters are not proven anew; the meanings of all objects are taken over from the sealed upstream.

The single proposition of this chapter is: already existing Intention can actually run in the field and is not merely a formal symbol. The construction may start from a field that already has a legal Intention structure: the intention-side nodes and five edges are placed by the increment A\_I, IntentLoc is given by declaration in advance, and the Law-formation of the five intention-side edges and the formation of the two probability dimensions take place before the unified running certificate is established. Every reading after the starting point can come only from the legal running recorded by the certificate; hard-coded readings, verdicts fed in as test input and forged certificates cannot pass themselves off as actual running.

This chapter does not undertake the generation of Intention. Starting from a base Sedimentation-Field in which Intention qualification and its dedicated realization structure have not yet formed, and passing through actual stimulus, Trace-Retention, deepening, Law-formation, the formation of new paths, probability organization and the formation of the Intention duty structure, so that Intention qualification first holds and enters running: this is another construction proposition, undertaken by Chapter 21, The Construction Form of the Intention Generation Substrate. The qualification readings this chapter obtains on a field in which the dedicated Intention structure has already been placed cannot serve as evidence that Intention is generated by the base Sedimentation-Field on its own.

Two levels are distinguished here:

Formal inheritance target: Spec\_IB = Augment(Spec\_ProbB, A\_I), where Spec\_ProbB = Augment(Spec\_LDB, A\_Prob) is the formal target of The Construction Form of the Probability Operating Substrate. Its byte-level inheritance from the sealed source fldb\_v1\_12.py is still PENDING RAW SOURCE.

Reference construction: Spec\_IB^ref = Augment(F\_ref, A\_Prob^rnd, A\_I). F\_ref is the executable semantic reconstruction (fldb\_work.py) of the appendix of The Construction Form of the Law-Dynamics Operating Substrate, a sealed chapter; it reproduces that appendix's native fourteen-group acceptance, but it is not a byte-level copy of the original source. A\_Prob^rnd is the probability-side reference increment built on F\_ref (probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd.py and its v2); it is not the same code as the reference implementation in the appendix of The Construction Form of the Probability Operating Substrate. A\_I is the intention-side increment of this chapter; all of its load-bearing logic is concentrated in the single core file intent\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v14.py. The construction theorem of this chapter is borne by Spec\_IB^ref, with the strength of construction.

## Section Two　Condition-Group Declaration

### 2.1 Upstream conditions

C1 Intention qualification: N\_I := P\_I ∧ SourceDrive ∧ GateCorrespondence ∧ TuneInterface, P\_I := InIntentDomain ∧ Probability; Intent ⇔ N\_I, carrying no H in either direction (The General Form of Intention, The Necessity Form of Intention). The formal readings are P\_I^M := IntentLoc^M ∧₃ N\_PQualRead\_I^M, SourceDrive^M := SourceInternal^M ∧₃ SourceOrgBind^M ∧₃ (SrcContinuationRead^M ∧₃ SrcDomainBindRead^M), GateCorrespondence^M := GC1 ∧₃ … ∧₃ GC5, TuneInterface^M := ∀₃ \[G\_i\] TuneAvail^M(\[G\_i\]), and CoreRead\_I^M is the ∧₃ of the four (The Mathematical Form of Intention).

C2 Intention-Action: within Intent = 1, IntentAct ⇔ N\_IAct, N\_IAct := ∃w\_I \[A1 ∧ A2 ∧ A3 ∧ A4\], the same chain being borne by the same witness; on the same w\_ρ, A2 first reads CurrentProbabilityOccurrence^M and then reads ProbabilityDutyConformance^M, and is not replaced by the total reading of Probability-Action (The General Form of Intention-Action, The Mathematical Form of Intention-Action, The Necessity Form of Intention-Action).

C3 Intention Desire-Gate switching: within Intent = 1, for a fixed σ, IntentGateSwitch\_F(σ) ⇔ N\_IGS(σ), N\_IGS(σ) := ∃w\_a, w\_b, \[G\_a\], \[G\_b\] \[S1 ∧ S2 ∧ S3 ∧ S4 ∧ B\_same^IGS\]; S2 invokes only A1, A2, A3 of action (The General Form of Intention Desire-Gate Switching, The Necessity Form of Intention Desire-Gate Switching).

C4 Intention-Dynamics: W\_IDyn := Intent\_{F,t\_0} = 1; IntentDyn ⇔ N\_IDyn^γ, N\_IDyn^γ := (L1 ∧ … ∧ L6) ∧ (Src ∨ Car ∨ Prob); a 0 of L4 takes the declared segment out of the working domain; L5 takes only 1 or ⊥; the start, continuation, stop and re-onset after stopping of source drive and of action are derived from positive occurrence intervals, continuity granularity and negative coverage confirming no occurrence, and are not defined directly by the readings of adjacent units (The General Form of Intention-Dynamics, The Mathematical Form of Intention-Dynamics, The Necessity Form of Intention-Dynamics).

C5 Formal interfaces: IA-M1 to IA-M12, IGS-M1 to IGS-M10, IDyn-M1 to IDyn-M14, and PD-M1 to PD-M13 invoked by IDyn-M11; zero-type distinction; coverage and refutation paths; read-out non-interference (the several Mathematical Forms and The Mathematical Form of Probability-Dynamics).

C6 Determination: the signing rules, entry rules and misjudgment bounds of the four verdicts Det\_I, Det\_IAct, Det\_IGS, Det\_IDyn (The Determination Form of Intention).

C7 Hand-down items: the soft-limit families, the qualification hard bound, the exhaustion of the three sites, the applicability boundary of L4, the realization of compatible endpoints, whether approachable endpoints exist, and the construction probe of the L5 open item (The Limit Form of Intention).

C8 Running layer and probability layer: the causal chain of Spec\_LDB (actual running → trace → sedimentation and decay → Law-formation); the running realization of probability qualification, action and dynamics (The Construction Form of the Law-Dynamics Operating Substrate, The Construction Form of the Probability Operating Substrate).

### 2.2 Causal direction

The construction fixes the following causal direction:

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;actual running → trace → sedimentation / decay → Law-formation → probability organization → Rel\_ρ → continuation → gate passage → post-gate tuning → read-out

The source-drive segment enters before the probability organization: a source-drive pulse travels along the source-drive segment, which has formed a Law, to the entry of the probability organization; the probability organization selects a dimension for continuation; the pulse then enters the Desire-Gate along the gate-passage segment corresponding to that dimension, which has also formed a Law, and finally enters tuning along the post-gate segment, which has likewise formed a Law. Any reverse structure is prohibited: Intent, IntentAct, IntentGateSwitch, IntentDyn → field update; reader → trace or sedimentation; verdict → running. Direct writing into Intent, IntentAct, IntentGateSwitch, IntentDyn, Degree, Rel\_ρ and their component readings is prohibited.

2.3 Choices of the reference implementation

The following are all choices of the reference implementation, not the Intention ontology: the specific names and topology of nodes and edges; the two formal dimensions and the two Desire-Gate duty classes; the number of formation passes of the five intention-side edges; the judgment of Law-formation, which follows the sedimentation and repetition thresholds of F\_ref; the binding sedimentation coefficient, decay and threshold of the probability layer; the generation gate GenGate and the carrier link CarrierLink as legal operations of the probability layer; the single event as evaluation unit, with continuity granularity δ\_src = δ\_act = 1; the specific contents of the frozen duty registration, the correspondence domain and the tuning-candidate registration; test scaffolding and protocol identifiers. With another set of legal parameters or representations, the construction must still satisfy the item-by-item verification of Section Four.

## Section Three　Construction Procedure

### 3.1 Upstream interfaces and conformity

The lower-layer interfaces actually invoked in this chapter are as follows:

U1 Actual running record: every underlying pulse has an actual route and running moment and is entered in the field log; a declared event that enters the probability or intention event registration additionally carries identities such as run, branch, protocol and evidence key. Records of the two layers can be matched by actual event.  
U2 Trace is the actual route: sedimentation comes only from edges actually walked.  
U3 Sedimentation, decay and Law-formation: the Law-formation of an edge is judged only by its own sedimentary state (Deposited and Groove).  
U4 Degree is readable: the degree profile DegreeA of the bearing Law and its step-by-step comparison (rise, fall, migration, equality).  
U5 Probability qualification and action: V, D, B, R, MinSuff, Rel, Probability are computed by the probability layer from the sedimentary state; the formal dimension organization DimOrg and the common binding Bind are given by probability-layer snapshots.  
U6 Probability-Dynamics: snapshot comparison gives ActDyn, RateDyn, DimStructDyn, BindStructDyn, RateBearingStructDyn and SameRel.  
U7 Legal operations act only on the field or only register real processes; none of them writes into a derived predicate. GenGate and CarrierLink of the probability layer and SourceStart, SourceStop, Avail of the intention side are all registered through the authoritative operation interface; Avail, Freeze, Thaw, IdleStep and pulses of the running layer are executed through the legal operation interface of the unified running certificate (3.3).  
U8 Conservative extension: the read-out layer changes no subsequent state of the running layer or the probability layer.

Conformity: U1 to U4 and the running-layer part of U7 are given by fldb\_work.py, whose native fourteen-group regression passes in full (Appendix B); U5, U6 and the probability-layer part of U7 are given by probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd.py and probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v2.py, whose regressions, of 36 and 27 items respectively, pass in full; U8 is given by the differential runs of V46 and V47 in Section Four. Under the all-zero relation Rel\_ρ = \[0\]\_ρ, the probability layer registers MinSuff\_ρ = {∅}, R = 0, Probability = 0, in agreement with the all-zero-relation boundary case of The Necessity Form of Probability; zero rate of a single dimension (1, 0) is still a legal probability, checked jointly by the regressions of every layer.

### 3.2 The running layer and A\_I^run

On top of the nodes and edges of F\_ref, the field adds the intention-side nodes sI, gA, gX, tA, tX and five edges:

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;source-drive segment sI → a1; gate-passage segments c → gA, x → gX; post-gate segments gA → tA, gX → tX

Here a1 is the entry of the probability organization; the two formal dimensions A and X of the probability layer are borne by the carrier paths a1 → m1 → c and a1 → m2 → x respectively, and A has in addition a candidate carrier a1 → m2 → c (A\_alt), not wired at the start.

Formation: first A and X each receive 80 formation pulses of the probability layer, obtaining Law-bearing and common binding for both dimensions; then, within the same window, the two dimensions are refreshed alternately for 20 rounds, and in each round each of the five intention-side edges receives one actual pulse, so that they sediment into Law. No step writes into a qualification reading.

Frozen registration: the source-drive duty registration (duty identity, bearing edge, whether it lies within the system boundary, the qualification anchor it belongs to, the probability organization it binds, the continuation entry point, the direction domain it binds, whether it is a complete external selector); the Desire-Gate duty classes and their bearing nodes; the correspondence domain between directions and Desire-Gate duty classes, Corr ⊆ V\_I × Ḡ\_I, the domain being closed in the declaration; the post-gate tuning-candidate registration of each Desire-Gate duty class (duty identity, bearing edge, kind, the anchor it belongs to); the carrier-candidate registration of the probability layer; the dynamics protocol object Γ\_IDyn (version, frozen state, unit rule, δ\_src, δ\_act, carrier-candidate discovery rule, duty-identity rule, degree protocol, coverage rule, attribution rule) and its modification history. IntentLoc is given by declaration in advance; the reference construction declares it as 1.

Legal operations of the intention side: SourceStart and SourceStop open and close one source-drive session; they are only running commands and session records, do not change the field state, and do not act as the formal start and stop of source drive. Avail sets the availability of some intention-side edge and accepts only the five intention-side edges; for lower-layer edges such as probability carriers it refuses with NotIntentEdge, and both the lower-layer availability and the intention-side log remain unchanged. The availability of lower-layer edges is changed only by the running layer's own Avail operation. All three are entered in the authoritative operation log of the intention side and write into no Intention reading; the log audit requires every record to be an authoritative record registered under this run, this branch and this protocol, with its operator belonging to {SourceStart, SourceStop, Avail}.

Events: each event is the execution of one actual route on the field and is entered in the event record, which contains the event identity, evidence key, run and branch, protocol, qualification anchor, execution class, route, dimension used, the qualification snapshot before the event, the probability event record, the source-drive session identity, the duty identity, the source chain and the gate-bearing record. An event carries the source-chain segments corresponding to the level it actually reaches: a complete organizational event carries four actual chain segments Z\_src, Z\_ρ, Z\_G, Z\_T; an organizational event that stops at the gate has only three actual chain segments Z\_src, Z\_ρ, Z\_G, and Z\_T is recorded as “the actual tuning-entry chain segment does not exist” (the chain segment is empty, ActualTuneEntrySeg = 0), while the frozen tuning duty, the enumerable tuning-entry candidate domain and the post-gate path actually traversed are registered; an external gate-change event and a source-drive solo event carry no complete organizational source chain. A missing actual chain segment may not be fabricated by the read-out layer. Kinds of event:

Complete organizational event: starting from sI, a dimension is selected by the probability organization, and the event runs through that dimension's carrier, gate-passage segment and post-gate segment.  
Organizational event that stops at the gate: as above, going as far as the Desire-Gate.  
External route event: starting from sI, with the dimension specified externally; the probability layer records it as external selection.  
External gate-change event: starting from a1, without passing through the source-drive segment, going directly to some Desire-Gate.  
Source-drive solo event: walks only the source-drive segment sI → a1.

Gate-bearing record: each event registers the Desire-Gate it actually enters as the gate borne in the later state and compares it with the Desire-Gate registered by the preceding event, obtaining the records of old-gate exit and new-gate acquisition. This is a real record on the running side, written by the event and not by a reader.

### 3.3 Unified running certificate

The composite running system has only one running certificate, RuntimeAudit. It wraps all legal operations that change state: pulses, Avail, Freeze, Thaw and IdleStep of the running layer; GenGate and CarrierLink of the probability layer; SourceStart, SourceStop and Avail of the intention side; and the various kinds of event. Any call name outside the legal operations is refused. Each legal operation is recorded as one entry, containing: the operation name; the full-state digests before and after the operation; run, branch and protocol; running and sedimentation counts; the qualification readings after the operation; the duty-identity snapshot after the operation (qualification anchor, run and branch, probability running identity, source-drive duty registration, Desire-Gate duties and their bearers, correspondence relation, tuning duties and candidate registration, frozen registration of carrier candidates); the protocol snapshot after the operation (the Intention and probability protocols and their histories, the dynamics protocol object and its modification history); the probability-layer snapshot after the operation (Probability, V, Rel, DimOrg, Bind and its structure of formed bindings, MinSuff) together with the action-unit reading of the operation itself; and, if the operation produces an event, the registration of the actual event's identity, evidence key, event run, branch and protocol, route and kind. The full-state digest covers the sedimentation amount, repetition record, Law-formation-related quantities, availability and masking, degree channel and degree window of every node and every edge of the field, the log length and random-source state; the bindings and their records, generation gates, carrier links, event count, operation count and random-source state of the probability layer; and the gate-bearing state, session state, event sequence number, event count, operation count and random-source state of the intention side. The certificate is passive and does not update the state of the field or of the probability layer. A write-in that bypasses legal operations does not enter the certificate, but it makes the pre-operation digest of the next record fail to join the post-operation digest of the previous one; a bypassing write-in after the last operation makes the current actual state fail to join the last record, and can be detected without waiting for the next operation.

A declared segment γ is given by two points on the certificate, each point carrying the actual full-state digest at that time. On the same segment the following are read out:

L1: the end point is after the starting point, the actual state at the starting point is exactly the state the certificate recorded there, all records within the segment are of the same run and the same branch, and the running and sedimentation counts do not go backward.  
L2: the digests of adjacent records within the segment join end to end, the actual state at the end of the segment joins the last record, the operation of every record belongs to the legal operation set, and both authoritative operation logs, of the probability layer and of the intention side, pass audit. The 1 of L2 is obtained positively from this closed chain, and is not signed merely because no illegal operation was found.  
L3: the duty-identity snapshots at the starting point and at every point within the segment are all identical.  
L4: the qualification readings at the starting point and after every record within the segment are all present (QualEventCover); it takes 1 when all are 1, 0 when any one is 0, and ⊥ when any one reading is missing or the qualification at some point is ⊥. State within the segment changes only through these records, so the coverage of qualification readings, taken together with the closed chain of L2, covers all field states within the segment; a segment that is 1 at both ends but exits midway reads 0.  
L6: the protocol snapshots at the starting point and at every point within the segment are all identical, and every protocol and its history are in the frozen state; any after-the-fact change to continuity granularity, coverage rule or any other item reads 0.  
L5: all load-bearing evidence actually used this time is audited item by item: same run, same branch, same anchor, same protocol, with an evidence key, and evidence carrying an event identity must be an event registered by the same certificate segment. Foreign evidence or a mismatched evidence key takes ⊥ (EvidenceSplice), and a missing evidence key takes ⊥ (EvidenceOpen); neither takes 0.  
If a declared point lies beyond the range the certificate has recorded, or the end point is not after the starting point (zero-width declaration), L1 to L6 are all ⊥; this means the declaration itself is unusable, not that the trajectory is illegal.

The determination matrix, the IDyn interface, the L5 stress probe and the core's derivation of Probability-Dynamics all read the same segment on this same certificate; no second legal-segment audit is set up.

3.4 The intention-side read-only increment A\_I^read

Qualification:

IntentLoc^M reads the declaration made in advance and takes 1, 0 or ⊥; P\_I^M = IntentLoc^M ∧₃ Probability.

SourceDrive^M: SourceInternal^M reads that the source-drive duty registration lies within the system boundary, that the duty identity is this source-drive duty, and that the bearing edge has sedimented into Law; SourceOrgBind^M reads that the registered qualification anchor is the same as this anchor and that the bound probability organization is this probability organization; SrcContinuationRead^M reads that the bearing edge has sedimented into Law and actually enters the antecedent a1 of the probability organization; SrcDomainBindRead^M reads that the bound direction domain equals the current formal direction domain and that the source-drive registration is not a complete external selector. SourceOrgBear^M is the ∧₃ of the latter two, and SourceDrive^M is the ∧₃ of the three. When the registration is unreadable, each sub-item takes ⊥. Qualification reads the duty structure that has sedimented into Law; it does not read current availability or whether there is currently a source-drive pulse: current silence or current unavailability does not cancel qualification.

GateCorrespondence^M: on the frozen and closed relation domain V\_I × Ḡ\_I, CorrRead^M reads the correspondence registration, and PassRead^M reads whether there is a gate-passage edge that has sedimented into Law between the end of that dimension's carrier and some bearing node of that Desire-Gate duty class. GC1 reads that every formal direction corresponds to at least one Desire-Gate duty class; GC2 reads that every formal direction corresponds to exactly one; GC3 reads that every Desire-Gate duty class has exactly one formal direction corresponding to it; GC4 reads that every Desire-Gate duty class has a source direction; GC5 reads that every positive correspondence has legal pass-through. When the relation domain is not closed or the bearing registration is unreadable, all five items are ⊥. The reference construction takes exactly A ↔ GateRole:A, X ↔ GateRole:X; this is an implementation parameter, not the definition of GateCorrespondence.

TuneInterface^M: for each Desire-Gate duty class the frozen post-gate tuning-candidate registration is read; PostGateTuneRoleRead^M reads that the candidate's kind is tuning, that it has the same anchor as this anchor, that the bearing edge starts from a bearing node of that Desire-Gate duty class, and that the bearing edge has sedimented into Law; if some candidate is 1, the TuneAvail^M of that gate is 1; if all candidates are 0, that gate is 0; otherwise ⊥. TuneInterface^M is the ∧₃ over the gates. A candidate of the effect-output kind does not count as a tuning duty.

Intent is the ∧₃ of P\_I^M, SourceDrive^M, GateCorrespondence^M, TuneInterface^M, and ⊥ is not pressed into 0. Qualification-layer readings and current-action readings are kept strictly apart: SourceDrive^M does not stand in for A1, GateCorrespondence^M does not stand in for A3, TuneInterface^M does not stand in for A4.

Action: A1 reads whether the event route passes through the source-drive segment and agrees with this run, this anchor, this source-drive duty and its bearing edge. On the same probability event w\_ρ, A2 first reads CurrentProbabilityOccurrence^M: the event is an organizational event, of the same run, same branch and same protocol, Probability = 1 before the event, and the actual route is realized by the formal bearer of that dimension registered before the event; when this is 1 it then reads ProbabilityDutyConformance^M: that dimension belongs to some non-empty group of MinSuff\_ρ before the event (current rate realization), and the evaluation is bound to the same run and running moment (ν\_t^cur). When current occurrence is 0, A2 is 0; when it is ⊥, A2 is ⊥. A2 does not invoke the total reading of Probability-Action. A3 reads whether the event passes through the gate-passage segment of the dimension used, and whether that dimension corresponds positively to the Desire-Gate duty class registered by the event. A4 reads whether the event passes through the post-gate segment of that Desire-Gate, and whether the tuning duty registered by the event is a legal tuning candidate of that Desire-Gate duty class. All four items are obtained on the same event route, that is, by the same witness; the reading is 1 when all are 1 and there is an evidence key, 0 when any one item is 0, and ⊥ otherwise. Whether the chain exists and whether the chain domain is adjudicable are read separately: IA-M5 requires that all four chain positions have records, each chain position being either a non-empty chain segment that actually occurred or an explicit missing record whose candidate domain has been enumerated; when Z\_T is an explicit missing record, IA-M5, IA-M10 and IA-M11 can still be solved and adjudicated, and A4 obtains a structural 0 because the actual tuning-entry chain segment does not exist.

Switching: S1 reads A3 of the earlier event; S2 reads A1, A2, A3 of the later event; S3 reads whether the gates borne by the earlier and later events differ; S4 reads whether old-gate exit and new-gate acquisition in the later event belong to the bearing record of the same event; B\_same reads whether the earlier and later events are of the same run, have the same anchor and are ordered.

Cross-time classification of source drive and action: the single event is the evaluation unit, and δ\_src = δ\_act = 1 is frozen in Γ\_IDyn. For each unit: SrcOcc reads that the event is a current actual event of this run, that the route passes through the source-drive segment, and that IA-M1 to IA-M5, IA-M11 and IA-M12 are all solvable; NoSrc reads that the event is a current actual event of this run and that the recorded complete route does not pass through the source-drive segment; ActOcc reads that IA-M1 to IA-M12 are all solvable and the action reading is 1; NoAct reads that IA-M1 to IA-M12 are all solvable and the action reading is 0; for all other units neither holds. A positive occurrence interval is taken as a maximal interval of consecutive positive units. Start requires that a negative-coverage unit within the declared segment immediately precede the positive interval; when the positive interval abuts the left bound of the declared segment, Start is not evaluated within this declaration. Stop requires that a negative-coverage unit immediately follow the positive interval. Continue is each unit within the positive interval other than the first. Re-onset after stopping requires that some Stop be followed by another Start, and that the L4 of the same certificate segment be 1 (whole-segment qualification persistence); if there is a Stop and a Start but no such certificate, or the certificate's L4 is not 1, re-onset after stopping is ⊥, not 1; when there is no such sequence and every unit has been resolved as positive or negative, it is 0. The SourceStart and SourceStop commands are reported only as auxiliary records and do not enter the classification.

Carrier: the frozen carrier-candidate registration must agree with the current candidate registration; BearDim is read candidate by candidate on the world snapshot at each evaluation point; only when the same carrier bears the same formal direction at two adjacent points does degree comparison have a common domain of comparison, and in that case the degree step is read; when the bearer is replaced the degree step is NotApplicable, and no change of degree is forced. In the reference construction only the main carrier of A has a degree reading.

Probability-Dynamics: the evaluation points are exactly the points of the declared segment on the unified running certificate, and each step is one legal operation recorded by the certificate; each point records the probability-layer snapshot at that time (Probability, V, Rel, DimOrg, Bind and its structure of formed bindings, MinSuff), and each step records the action-unit reading a\_u of that operation itself (1 when it is an event and both current occurrence and duty conformance on the same w\_ρ are 1; 0 when it is not an event, or the event has no current probability occurrence; ⊥ when unreadable). Solvability conditions: PD-M1 reads that Γ\_IDyn is frozen and the certificate segment is readable; PD-M2 reads that L1 = 1 and that the load-bearing events are all current actual events of this run and this branch; PD-M3 reads that L2 = 1 and that the load-bearing events are all events registered by that certificate segment; PD-M4 reads that the probability protocol history is frozen; PD-M5 reads that every point has a snapshot and that the load-bearing events have an identity and a running moment; PD-M6 reads that Probability is obtainable at each point and before and after the load-bearing events; PD-M7 reads that the current-occurrence reading is solvable; PD-M8 reads that the evaluation kind, the evidence key and the action-unit readings of every step are complete; PD-M9 to PD-M12 rebuild the objects on the snapshots at the points and on the snapshots of the load-bearing events: the weights of Rel are non-negative and finite, the dimension sequence equals the V of the time, a non-zero class is in normalized representation, and the zero class \[0\]\_ρ is legal; DimOrg has no repetition, every formal dimension has at least one registered bearing role, and the roles agree with the candidate registration; Bind has exactly one entry for every formal dimension, with non-negative finite values; MinSuff is an antichain taken inclusion-minimal, without repeated groups, exactly {∅} for the zero class, containing no empty group for a non-zero class, and in this reference construction (where the generating factors are exactly the formal dimensions) exactly {supp(Rel)}; PD-M13 reads that the probability event and the Intention event are of the same run, same branch, same protocol and same anchor and carry an evidence key. The probability legal segment LegalSeg: 0 when L1 or L2 of the certificate segment is 0; 1 when PD-M1 to PD-M5 all hold; ⊥ otherwise. For each step, same-evidence binding is checked first (the step joins the preceding point on the certificate chain, the identities agree, and the load-bearing events all belong to the same certificate segment); then qualification contact Contact\_k = P\_k ∨₃ P\_{k+1} is derived (1 when Probability is explicitly 1 at at least one end, 0 when it is explicitly 0 at both ends, ⊥ otherwise); then Q is derived branch by branch. The step readings of the rate branch, the dimension-organization branch, the binding branch and the rate-bearing branch are each first combined with Contact\_k by ∧₃: a step that lacks qualification at both ends is not signed, even if the relation, the dimension organization or the binding changes, and reads 0; when contact is ⊥ it reads ⊥. The action branch derives StepAct from the endpoint qualification and the action readings: NA (counted as 0) when Probability at the two ends includes a 0, ⊥ when it includes a ⊥; a state point on the certificate has no action in progress, and A\_k = A\_{k+1} = 0 is a recorded reading, not a fabricated starting point; a\_u = 0 within the step is Idle (counted as 0); when a\_u = 1, A is 0 at both ends although there is action within the step: the evaluation granularity is too coarse to expose onset and end, so ⊥ is recorded and Start is not signed; the rate branch, when Rel is legal at both ends, reads SameRel and signs RateDyn; the dimension-organization branch compares V and DimOrg; the binding branch compares only the structure of formed bindings, not the amounts of sedimentation; the rate-bearing branch requires SameRel and then compares the rate-bearing organization. Each branch depends only on its own conditions: the action branch depends on PD-M6 to PD-M8, the rate branch on PD-M9, the dimension-organization branch on PD-M10, the binding branch on PD-M11, the rate-bearing branch on PD-M9 and PD-M12; if some condition fails, only the branches depending on it take ⊥. DynActual = ActDyn ∨₃ RateDyn ∨₃ QualStructDyn, where QualStructDyn is the ∨₃ of the dimension-organization branch, the binding branch and the rate-bearing branch (1 on an explicit positive witness of any legal branch, 0 when every branch is completely 0), and the total reading Read\_{N\_PDyn} = LegalSeg ∧₃ DynActual: 0 when the legal segment is 0; when the legal segment is ⊥, 1 is not signed, but it is still 0 when DynActual is completely 0.

IDyn interface: idyn\_certificate derives on the same certificate segment: M1 reads that the starting-point qualification is 1 and Γ\_IDyn is frozen; M2 takes L1; M3 takes L2; M4 takes the coverage of qualification readings within the segment and requires that the qualification at every point be readable (not ⊥); M5 takes L5; M6 takes L6; M7 reads that the unit rule and the continuity granularity are frozen; M8 and M12 take the solvability of the source-drive and action units in the derivation of the cross-time classification, and check that the events belong to the same certificate segment; M9 and M10 take the carrier derivation; M11 takes PD-M1 to PD-M13; M13 is layered coverage: the segment is readable, M3, M4, M5, M6, M8 to M12 each hold, the events belong to the same certificate segment, and the candidate domains are frozen. The coverage items come from two classes of source: those derived from running records (event membership, source chain, current probability certificate, binding, coverage of qualification readings, certificate chain), and those given by frozen finite registrations (direction domain, Desire-Gate duty-class domain, tuning-candidate domain, carrier-candidate registration, closure of the relation domain). M14 is not given by declaration; it is tested by differential runs with read-out on and off.

Solvability conditions: IA-M1 to IA-M12 and IGS-M1 to IGS-M10 read, item by item, the event records, session records, operation logs and coverage certificates; IA-M12 and IGS-M10 read the coverage certificate formed from the two classes of source above, not a bare coverage flag; if any item is missing, the reading falls back to ⊥ with that item attached as the reason.

Verdict routing: according to the entry rules, signing rules and misjudgment bounds of The Determination Form of Intention, the readings of qualification, action, switching and dynamics are routed to 1, 0, ⊥ or OutDom, with the reason attached. Every exit able to sign 1 or 0 first passes the corresponding gate: the action verdict first passes IA-M1 to IA-M12, the switching verdict first passes IGS-M1 to IGS-M10, and when the gate is not complete the verdict is ⊥ with the missing item as reason; the failure layers of action and switching recognize only an explicit 0, and when some layer is ⊥ the verdict is ⊥. The dynamics verdict and the IDyn interface reading share one legal-segment gate, deriving Read\_{LegalIDynSeg} by three-valued conjunction: ⊥ when the certificate segment is unreadable (SegmentUnreadable); when L4 = 0, routed according to the applicability boundary (QualExitOpen or the conditionalized OutDom), not taken as IntentDyn = 0; otherwise, when any of L1, L2, L3, L6 is 0, a structural 0 is signed, and 0 absorbs ⊥ (including the ⊥ of L4, which disagrees with 7.2 of The Determination Form of Intention; see V27); when there is no 0 but there is a ⊥, it is ⊥ (QualCoverOpen for L4, EvidenceSplice or EvidenceOpen for L5, and the corresponding item's undecided status for the others), signing neither 1 nor the conditionalized 0; only when all six items are 1 does it enter the positive/negative routing of the three sites. The reading of the probability site takes 0 according to LegalSeg ∧₃ DynActual: either the legal segment is 0, or every step is bound and every branch is completely 0; in a step where binding does not hold every branch is ⊥, and when the dependency condition of some branch does not hold that branch is ⊥, and in neither case can DynActual be completely 0. Whether the three sites are in motion serves as input to the dynamics verdict function and is supplied by the tests.

Reason labels: those reason labels in this reference implementation that differ from upstream reason names are all subdivisions or aliases of upstream reasons and add no new class of reason: QualCoverOpen falls under QualificationOpen of The Mathematical Form of Intention-Dynamics, and at the verdict layer is the QualExitOpen of 7.2 of The Determination Form of Intention (the qualification reading at some evaluation point is ⊥, or the coverage of qualification readings is incomplete); EvidenceOpen is EvidenceKeyOpen (evidence key missing); SegmentUnreadable falls under DeclarationInvalid (a declared point not within the certificate records, or a zero-width declaration); GateExitOpen falls under GateStateOpen of The Mathematical Form of Intention Desire-Gate Switching (the record of gate-bearing in the later state is incomplete; here, the old-gate exit record is missing). ProfileNotApplicable and the NotApplicable of the carrier degree step are comparison readings (profile not applicable and not applicable in The Mathematical Form of Intention-Dynamics), not reasons for ⊥. The set of reasons for verdicts is governed by the upstream reasons that The Determination Form of Intention takes over.

Profile comparison: the directional-type reading reads the shares of the two dimensions in Rel\_ρ; the carrier-type reading reads the degree comparison of the same bearer at adjacent evaluation points; the composite profile uses only the product order, mixed Rise and Fall is incomparable, and when a required coordinate has no common domain of comparison it is ProfileNotApplicable.

### 3.5 Order of execution

First, build the field: the running layer of F\_ref, plus the intention-side nodes and edges. Second, form qualification by actual running on the intention-side structure already placed: the formation of the two probability dimensions, and the sedimentation into Law of the five intention-side edges. Third, read out qualification. Fourth, establish the unified running certificate, and continue actual running in the same field through the certificate: the various events, session commands and legal operations. Fifth, read out on every event, every comparison sequence and every declared segment, and carry out the cross-time classification, the derivation of carrier and Probability-Dynamics, verdict routing and profile comparison.

## Section Four　Item-by-Item Verification

Each item below corresponds to a specific test in Appendix A, with results in Appendix B. Each item states its strength: construction means an instance obtained by actual running; interface check means that the routing of readings, a solvability gate or verdict logic is tested on actual or declared records, with part of the input or breakage applied by the test; structural compatibility means that the read-out kernel structurally does not read some quantity, so that the endpoint of that quantity does not affect the reading, not that the quantity is pushed to its endpoint in a system that contains it.

### 4.1 Qualification

V1 Qualification positive instance: after formation in the same run, Probability = 1; SourceInternal, SourceOrgBind, SrcContinuation, SrcDomainBind and SourceOrgBear of SourceDrive^M are all 1; GC1 to GC5 are all 1; TuneInterface^M is 1 gate by gate; these are derived from running history and frozen registration. IntentLoc^M = 1 is taken from the declaration made in advance and is not derived from running history. The four-item conjunction gives Intent = 1 (qualification, source\_drive\_full\_semantics, gate\_gc1\_gc5, tune\_per\_gate, J1). Construction, with the IntentLoc item declared.

V2 Qualification is not conditioned on a current event: after formation no event occurs, and SourceDrive^M and Intent are both 1 (source\_drive\_not\_current\_pulse, J1). Construction.

V3 Location reading open: the declaration of IntentLoc is changed to ⊥, P\_I^M and Intent are both ⊥, not 0; dynamics interface M1 does not hold (intent\_loc\_open\_preserves\_tri, IDyn-M1\_actual\_location\_open). Interface check.

V4 Probability side beyond the boundary, collapse of the dimensional base: by the running layer's own Avail operation (through the unified running certificate) the entry edge of the only wired carrier of dimension A is set unavailable, the Law-bearing judgment of A fails, V is left with one dimension, Probability = 0, the other three items persist, and Intent = 0 (J13, LIM1). Construction. This is the collapse of the dimensional base in Deg\_P,Prob, not zero rate. The intention-side Avail refuses the same edge with NotIntentEdge, the lower-layer availability is unchanged, and the intention-side log does not grow (intent\_avail\_refuses\_lower\_carrier). Construction.

V5 Probability side beyond the boundary, zero class: on the same object, the generation of both dimensions is closed by legal generation-gate operations, Rel\_ρ = \[0\]\_ρ, MinSuff\_ρ = {∅}, R = 0, Probability = 0, Intent = 0 (probability-layer regression all\_zero\_relation, qual\_exit\_covered\_L4\_zero). Construction.

V6 SourceDrive beyond the boundary: the source-drive segment is no longer refreshed, only the other edges and the two probability dimensions are refreshed, the source-drive segment loses Law-formation through decay, Probability persists, SourceDrive^M = 0, Intent = 0 (J15, LIM2). Construction.

V7 Negative instance of the direction domain of SourceDrive: the source-drive registration is changed to a complete external selector, SrcDomainBind^M = 0, SourceDrive^M = 0 (source\_domain\_external\_selector\_fails). Construction; a structural negative instance under a change of registration.

V8 Structural negative instances of GateCorrespondence: when A is given an additional positive correspondence to GateRole:X, GC2 = 0; when only A ↔ GateRole:A is left in the correspondence, GateRole:X has no source direction and GC4 = 0; in both cases GateCorrespondence^M is 0 (gate\_nonfunctional\_corr\_fails, gate\_missing\_gate\_coverage\_fails). Construction; structural negative instances under a change of registration.

V9 Structural negative instance of TuneInterface: the post-gate candidate of GateRole:A is registered as of the effect-output kind, the TuneAvail^M of that gate = 0, TuneInterface^M = 0 (effect\_output\_not\_tune). Construction; a structural negative instance under a change of registration.

V10 SourceDrive and GateCorrespondence jointly beyond the boundary: both sides lose Law-formation at once, and the failure signature consists of both (LIM17). Construction. It shows only that this composite path crosses the qualification boundary; it does not assign the boundary to either axis.

V11 Zero-rate endpoint: the generation of dimension A is closed to 0 by the generation gate, X persists, Rel\_ρ = (0, 1), V is still {A, X}, Probability = 1, Intent = 1 (LIM4). Construction.

Not constructed: a physical model of InIntentDomain (IntentLoc is given by declaration); instances in which IntentLoc^M, SourceOrgBind^M, GC1, GC3 or GC5 alone is 0; instances in which GateCorrespondence or TuneInterface alone crosses the boundary through sedimentation decay; the general degeneration family of Deg\_G beyond a finite frozen relation domain.

### 4.2 Action

V12 Complete action: on a complete organizational event, CurrentProbabilityOccurrence^M = 1, ProbabilityDutyConformance^M = 1, A1 to A4 are on the same chain, Read\_{N\_IAct}^M = 1 (action\_full, J2, IA\_positive\_read). Construction.

V13 An external route does not sign action: for an event with an externally specified dimension, A1 is 1, CurrentProbabilityOccurrence^M = 0, A2 = 0, and the action reading is 0 (external\_not\_full\_action). Construction.

V14 Missing A4 layer: in an organizational event that stops at the gate, A1, A2, A3 are 1 and A4 is 0, action is 0, and qualification is unchanged; the Z\_T of this event is an explicit missing record with no chain segment, there is no post-gate segment on the route, IA-M5, IA-M10 and IA-M11 are all 1, and the reading is 0, not falling back to ⊥ (J3, J4, gate\_stop\_no\_fabricated\_ZT, LIM5, IA\_zero\_not\_open\_when\_all\_M). Construction.

V15 Complete action under different strengths: under four random seeds, three numbers of formation passes and three pulse strengths, complete action is obtained in every case (stress act). Construction, finite grid.

### 4.3 Switching

V16 Switching positive instance: using the generation gate to make first X bear and then A bear, two complete organizational events enter gX and gA respectively, and S1 to S4 and B\_same are all 1 (gate\_switch, J5, J16, IGS\_positive\_read). Construction.

V17 Same-gate continuation is 0: the later event still enters the same Desire-Gate, S3 = 0 (J6, J17, IGS\_structural\_zero\_with\_all\_M). Construction.

V18 A missing old-gate exit record is ⊥: when the old-gate exit record of the later event is deleted, verdict routing gives ⊥ (GateExitOpen), not 0 (J18; J7 checks only that this omission yields no switching reading of 1). When the event sequence number of a same-gate continuation is altered into a foreign sequence, the IGS gate is incomplete and the switching verdict is ⊥, the 0 of S3 not being signed (J34). Interface check.

V19 Switching without complete action: the later state is an organizational event that stops at the gate, A4 = 0, and the switching reading is 1 (LIM6). Construction.

V20 Externally forced gate swap: an external gate-change event does not pass through the source-drive segment, S1, S3, S4 are 1, S2 is 0, switching is 0, and qualification persists (LIM7). Construction.

Simplification of the switching readings: S3 reads “the old gate no longer bears in the later state” by whether the gates borne as registered by the earlier and later events differ, and S4 reads the attribution of the handover by the records of old-gate exit and new-gate acquisition in the same event; in this construction each event bears only one Desire-Gate, with no joint use and no joint bearing by several gates, so within this construction domain these two readings agree with the upstream definitions, and they do not stand for gate-bearing coverage in general.

4.4 Unified running certificate and legal segment

V21 Certificate positive instance: on the declared segment of a source-drive solo run or of a complete organizational event, L1 to L6 are all 1; the same certificate segment gives IDyn-M3, M4 and PD-M3 at once, and the event identity is registered by the certificate (J8, l5\_clean, shared\_cert\_positive, PD\_M3\_same\_span\_positive, event\_identity\_ledger\_bound). Construction. When a declared point lies beyond the certificate records, all six items are ⊥ and the dynamics verdict is ⊥ (SegmentUnreadable, J29). Interface check.

V22 In-segment direct writing: within the declared segment the state of some node in the field is rewritten directly, bypassing legal operations, and then one legal event is executed; the digest chain breaks, L2 = 0, and the same certificate makes both IDyn-M3 and PD-M3 fail (J9, J20, l5\_search\_L2, bypass\_breaks\_IDynM3\_and\_PDM3, IDyn-M3\_actual\_write\_audit\_fail); after the last legal operation the sedimentation amount of some edge is rewritten (change 10^-12), and the current state fails to join the last record (trailing\_bypass\_close\_gap); direct writing itself does not advance the running and sedimentation counts (LIM10). Construction.

V23 Authoritative logs: when one record in the probability-layer log is corrupted, the same certificate makes both IDyn-M3 and PD-M3 fail (prob\_log\_corruption\_breaks\_shared\_M3); when a record whose operator is not in the legal set is forged in the intention-side log, L2 = 0, and through the same certificate IDyn-M3 fails (forged\_intent\_operator\_breaks\_L2, forged\_intent\_operator\_breaks\_IDynM3). The logs read actual records; the corruption is applied by the test. Interface check.

V24 Same anchor: changing the qualification anchor within the segment, or changing the identity of some Desire-Gate duty, and then executing a legal event gives L3 = 0 (J10, l5\_search\_L3, l5\_search\_L3\_role\_identity). Construction.

V25 Same-trajectory evidence: when load-bearing evidence comes from another run, another anchor or both, or the evidence key does not agree with the certificate registration, L5 is ⊥ (EvidenceSplice) and the other five items are 1; the dynamics verdict is ⊥, not 0 (J11, J21, l5 probe, IDyn-M5\_actual\_evidence\_bind\_fail). Interface check.

V26 Protocol freezing: changing the continuity granularity δ\_src or δ\_act of Γ\_IDyn, or the coverage rule, within the segment, and then executing a legal event gives L6 = 0 (J12, l5\_search\_L6, l5\_search\_L6\_grain, IDyn-M6\_actual\_protocol\_fail). Construction.

V27 Whole-segment persistence of qualification: on the same object and in the same run, the generation of both dimensions is first fully closed and then fully reopened through the generation gate; qualification is 1 at both ends of the segment, but the certificate records the exit in between, L4 = 0, and the other five items remain 1 (the output of the L5 probe qual\_exit in Appendix B); the starting-point qualification is taken from the starting point of the same certificate (J24, l5\_search\_L4). Construction. A segment that only closes without reopening likewise gives L4 = 0 (qual\_exit\_covered\_L4\_zero). Construction. When the qualification reading at some point of the certificate is deleted, the digest chain is still closed, M3 holds but M4 does not, and L4 is ⊥; the dynamics verdict is accordingly ⊥ (QualCoverOpen): when the three sites are in motion 1 is not signed, and when the negative conditions are assumed closed 0 is not signed either (qual\_cover\_loss\_hits\_M4\_not\_M3, IDyn-M4\_actual\_qual\_cover\_fail, J27, J28). Interface check. When the location declaration at some point within the segment is temporarily ⊥, the qualification at that point is ⊥, L4 = ⊥, L3 is still 1, and the dynamics verdict is ⊥, signing neither 1 nor the conditionalized 0; IDyn-M4 fails and the interface reading is ⊥ (J30, IDyn-M4\_open\_qual\_point\_blocks\_read). Construction. When in-segment direct writing makes L2 = 0 and at the same time deleting the qualification reading of one point makes L4 = ⊥, the dynamics verdict signs a structural 0 (L2): 0 absorbs ⊥ (J31); according to 7.2 of The Determination Form of Intention, when L4 is ⊥ Det\_IDyn should be ⊥ (QualExitOpen); in this case the present reference implementation takes the 0 of L2 first, which disagrees with that chapter, and this is listed as a to-do. Construction. When L1, L2, L3, L5, L6 of an actual legal segment are set to ⊥ one at a time, the dynamics verdict is ⊥ in each case, signing neither 1 nor the conditionalized 0; the shared legal-segment gate gives the same routing on the IDyn interface (J32, IDyn\_shared\_legal\_gate\_zero\_absorption). Interface check.

V28 All three sites at rest: the segment contains only one legal operation (the generation gate set to its current value), and L1 to L6 are all 1; on this segment the three sites are declared at rest, the current verdict is ⊥ (CarrierNegOpen, J22), and it is 0 when the negative conditions are explicitly assumed closed (J23). The legal segment is construction; the verdict is interface check. Self-comparison of the snapshot at one and the same moment gives the zero type of the three site readings (LIM9); it does not by itself constitute a legal segment. A legal segment in which pulses advance and the running count moves forward while all three sites are at rest is not constructed in this chapter.

4.5 Three sites, cross-time classification and process-type witnesses

V29 Each of the three sites going solo: after sedimentation is frozen only one source-drive solo event occurs, the probability relation is unchanged, no branch of Probability-Dynamics is signed, and the carrier degree is equal (source\_only); after sedimentation is frozen and the degree window filled, one pulse unrelated to Intention occurs on the field, the degree comparison of the bearing Law of dimension A is a fall, and the probability relation is exactly unchanged (carrier\_only, LIM8); only one legal generation-gate operation is performed, Rel\_ρ changes, RateDyn is signed, the carrier degree is equal, and there is no Intention event (prob\_only). The three give source-drive solo (1, 0, 0), carrier solo (0, 1, 0) and probability solo (0, 0, 1). Construction.

V30 Start is not signed at the left bound: two positive source-drive units begin at the left end of the declared segment, the Start of neither source drive nor action is evaluated within this declaration, the classification is empty, and “the first time a 1 is seen” is not written as Start (W0). Construction.

V31 Source drive already continuing while complete action first appears: one source-drive session is opened, with two successive source-drive solo events and then one complete organizational event; the three form one positive source-drive interval, the middle unit being a source-drive Continue; the first two have only A1, the third obtains A1 to A4 for the first time; the first two source-drive solo events have no probability event, so the solvability conditions of action do not hold and they are neither positive action units nor units confirming no action, and therefore the third does not sign the Start of action (W1). Construction.

V32 Stop and re-onset of action after a break at the gate layer: in the same session, when the gate-passage segment is set unavailable, current probability action continues while A3 and A4 break, the action reading of the middle unit is 0 and solvable, and the Stop of action is signed; after availability is restored, complete action is obtained again; all operations are executed through the unified running certificate, L4 = 1 for the same certificate segment, and re-onset after stopping is signed; qualification is 1 throughout. When the source-chain record of the middle unit is deleted, that unit is no longer a negative-coverage unit, and the Stop disappears accordingly (W2). Construction.

V33 Stop and re-onset of action after a break at the tuning layer: as above, the break being in the post-gate segment; A4 breaks while A1 to A3 continue, Stop is signed, and re-onset after stopping is signed when L4 = 1 for the same certificate segment (W3). Construction.

V34 Re-onset of source drive after stopping is derived from negative coverage: when only a SourceStop and a new SourceStart command lie between two adjacent source-drive solo events, neither the Stop of source drive nor re-onset after stopping is signed; when an external gate-change event lies between the two source-drive solo events, it is a current actual event that records a complete route not passing through the source-drive segment, and constitutes a unit confirming no source drive, so the Stop of source drive is derived, and when L4 = 1 for the same certificate segment re-onset after stopping is derived, the two stretches of source drive belonging to different sessions (W4). Construction. When the same sequence carries no certificate segment, or the generation of both dimensions within the certificate segment is first fully closed and then fully reopened so that L4 = 0, the Stop is derived all the same, and re-onset after stopping is ⊥, not 1 (W4b); when the qualification reading of one point is deleted from the certificate (qualification missing), or the location declaration at some point within the segment is temporarily ⊥ (qualification undetermined), L4 = ⊥ and re-onset after stopping is likewise ⊥ (W4c). Construction.

V35 Foreign Probability evidence: a real RateDyn that occurred on another object is taken as evidence for this object, and the reading is ⊥ (ProbBindMismatch), not 0 (W5). Interface check.

V36 Current switching is not a cross-time source-drive type: the switching reading is 1, while without cross-time material of source drive the cross-time type of source drive is SourceTransitionOpen (W6). Structural compatibility.

V37 Direction rising while the carrier falls: in the same declared step the share of dimension A rises while the degree of the bearing Law of A falls; the composite profile is incomparable and no total strength is given (W7). Construction.

V38 Profile not applicable: first the candidate carrier A\_alt is formed by actual sedimentation, then the bearing of A is moved from A\_primary to A\_alt in “make-before-break” fashion; qualification is 1 before and after; no single bearer bears A at both evaluation points, the carrier coordinate has no common domain of comparison, and the profile is ProfileNotApplicable, neither 0 nor ⊥ (W8); a carrier-link replacement of the same kind makes the common domain of comparison of degree in the carrier derivation fail, and IDyn-M10 fails (IDyn-M10\_actual\_degree\_domain\_fail). Construction.

V39 Legal replacement of bearers: with the same formation and replacement as V38, qualification is 1 before, during and after the wiring, the anchor and protocol are unchanged, and the candidate registration is frozen; no single bearer persists in bearing across both ends, so the replacement is not signed as dynamics of degree of some bearer; the change of the formal dimension organization is read by the probability site as DimStructDyn (W10). Construction.

The ways of reading the direction step, the profile and evidence binding used in W5 to W8 are written within the process-witness program; they are read-out functions of that program and do not belong to the core file.

### 4.6 Probability-Dynamics

V40 Source of the Probability-Dynamics readings: when one generation-gate operation is recorded on the certificate segment, Rel changes, the rate branch is 1, the legal segment is 1, and the total reading is 1 (D3e\_rate\_positive\_from\_recorded\_step); when a make-before-break of the carrier is performed on the certificate segment, the formal dimension organization changes and the dimension-organization branch is 1 (the legal branch of D3a). Construction. On a declared segment containing only one complete organizational event, PD-M1 to PD-M13 all hold and PD-M3 is taken from the same certificate segment, but the action branch is ⊥ because of evaluation granularity, the other branches are 0, and the total reading is ⊥, not 1 (shared\_cert\_positive, PD\_M3\_same\_span\_positive, D7b\_single\_org\_event\_read\_open). Construction. This segment does not sign 1: the Start of action is not preset by the code, and a change in the amount of sedimentation is not a change in the binding structure.

V41 Negative instances of the objects of PD-M9 to PD-M12: when the Rel before the event is changed to an unnormalized object with 2.0 for each of the two dimensions, PD-M9 fails; when an unregistered bearing role appears in DimOrg, PD-M10 fails; when Bind lacks one dimension, PD-M11 fails; when MinSuff contains a non-formal dimension, PD-M12 fails; all four make IDyn-M11 fail (IDyn-M11\_actual\_PD\_solver\_fail, PD-M10\_invalid\_dimension\_org\_rejected, PD-M11\_invalid\_bind\_org\_rejected, PD-M12\_invalid\_rate\_bear\_org\_rejected). Rel containing negative weights or non-finite values (NaN, infinity), Bind with repeated entries or negative values, MinSuff with an empty group coexisting with a non-zero relation, repeated groups, groups containing one another, or a family disagreeing with supp(Rel), and a zero class whose MinSuff is not {∅}, are all rejected (D4\_rel\_nonnegative\_finite, D5\_bind\_unique\_finite\_structure\_not\_quantity, D6\_minsuff\_zero\_class\_antichain\_complete). Any object that has all its fields but is illegal is rejected without exception. Interface check, the corruption being applied by the test.

V42 Foreign probability run and the reading gate: when the probability record of the same event is changed to another probability run, PD-M13 fails, the same-evidence binding of every step fails, Q is not read, and the probability reading is ⊥, not 0 (foreign\_probability\_bind\_rejected, PD\_read\_open\_when\_bind\_fails). When the legal segment is 1, the Rel at some point is illegal and another legal branch has a positive witness, the total reading is 1, the illegality making only the rate branch and the rate-bearing branch ⊥ (D3a\_illegal\_rate\_branch\_does\_not\_block\_legal\_positive); when the legal segment is 1, the Rel at some point is illegal and all other branches are 0, the total reading is ⊥, not 1 (D3b\_illegal\_rate\_without\_other\_positive\_not\_one); when the probability protocol history is no longer frozen so that the legal segment is ⊥, while every branch is completely 0, the total reading is 0 (D3c\_open\_legal\_and\_zero\_dyn\_is\_zero); when a bypassing write-in within the segment makes the legal segment 0, the total reading is 0 even if another branch has a positive witness (D3d\_zero\_legal\_segment\_gives\_zero); the truth tables of three-valued conjunction and disjunction are checked entry by entry (D3\_three\_valued\_tables); the action branch reads only from the points and steps recorded by the certificate: a step of a complete organizational event is ⊥, a step of an external route event is Idle, a step in which the carrier entry becomes unavailable so that Probability = 0 at the later point is NA, and no step signs a preset Start (D7\_action\_branch\_from\_recorded\_points). Interface check and construction.

### 4.7 Solvability conditions

V43 IA-M1 to IA-M12: on a complete organizational event all twelve items are 1 and the action reading is 1; setting any one item as missing gives the reading ⊥ with that item as reason; on an event with a missing A4 layer all twelve items are 1 and the reading is 0 (interface matrix); when the source-chain record of the same event is deleted, the action verdict is ⊥ (IA-M5), the 0 of A4 not being signed (J33). Interface check. IA-M12 reads the coverage certificate formed from running records and frozen registration.

V44 IGS-M1 to IGS-M10: a test of the same type; same-gate continuation is 0 when all ten items are 1 (interface matrix). Interface check. IGS-M10 reads a coverage certificate of the same type.

V45 IDyn-M1 to IDyn-M13: on an actual declared segment, the thirteen items derived by idyn\_certificate are all 1; when their actual material is broken item by item, the corresponding item fails: location reading open (M1), the running identity of one record in the certificate rewritten (M2), a bypassing write-in within the segment (M3), the qualification reading of one certificate point deleted (M4), load-bearing evidence from a foreign run (M5), the coverage rule changed within the segment (M6), the continuity granularity no longer the frozen granularity (M7), the source-chain record of an event deleted (M8), the frozen registration of carrier candidates emptied (M9), bearer succession leaving degree without a common domain of comparison (M10), an illegal relation object (M11), an illegal evaluation kind of an event (M12), and failure of any of the above branches leaving layered coverage unclosed (M13); when all thirteen items are in place and the three sites all give structural zero, the interface reading is 0, not falling back to ⊥ (idyn interface matrix). Interface check; the materials read by M2 to M4, M6 and M8 to M11 are actually derived by the reference construction. What is tested here is this chapter's derivation of the IDyn-M conditions and its three-valued routing, not an automatic determination about arbitrary implementations.

### 4.8 Read-out non-interference

V46 IDyn-M14: under the same initial state and the same operation plan, one copy repeatedly invokes readers of qualification, realization packages, probability snapshots, relations, minimal sufficient groups, degree and so on, and the other does not; after running, the dynamics snapshots of the field, the field log, the binding sedimentation, the authoritative operations and the event routes are exactly identical; when a direct write is mixed into the read-out path the differential test fails and the reading is ⊥ (ReaderImplementationInvalid) (IDyn-M14\_actual\_differential\_pass, IDyn-M14\_failure\_special\_open). Construction.

V47 Process-level non-interference: under the same session and the same running plan, interspersing read-outs of qualification, snapshots, degree and action between events leaves the core states of the field and of the probability layer exactly identical to those of the copy without interspersing (W9). Construction.

### 4.9 Verdict routing

V48 Undecided under the current misjudgment bounds: when the probability side crosses the boundary, Det\_I is ⊥ (ProbabilityOpen, J13); when SourceDrive crosses the boundary it is ⊥ (IntentAtomRepOpen, J15); when all three sites are at rest the dynamics verdict is ⊥ (CarrierNegOpen, J22); on exit from qualification within the segment it is ⊥ (QualExitOpen, J25); when qualification coverage is missing or the qualification at some point is ⊥, while the other items of the legal segment have no 0, it is ⊥ (QualCoverOpen, J27, J28, J30; for the case in which another item is 0 see V27); when the certificate segment is unreadable it is ⊥ (SegmentUnreadable, J29); under evidence splicing it is ⊥ (EvidenceSplice, J21). Interface check.

V49 Conditionalized signing: when the probability negative conditions are explicitly assumed closed, the probability side crossing the boundary signs Det\_I = 0 (J14), and exit from qualification within the segment signs OutDom (QualExit) (J26); when the carrier and probability negative conditions are both explicitly assumed closed, all three sites at rest signs 0 (J23); in-segment direct writing signs 0 (L2, J20), a path that carries no condition. Interface check. These hypotheses are switches of the tests and do not mean that the corresponding conditions have been closed.

V50 The entry of the dynamics verdict takes the starting point of the same object: the entry qualification is read from the qualification reading of the unified running certificate at the starting point, not from the final state, and is not borrowed from another object (J24 to J26). Interface check.

Among the inputs of verdict routing, L1 to L6 and the starting-point qualification are taken from the unified running certificate; whether the three sites are in motion is still supplied by the tests as input to the verdict function. So the legal-segment part of the dynamics verdict is read out from the certificate, while the three-site part and the verdict logic are interface checks; it is not the case that the four verdicts are signed automatically from raw running records for an arbitrary declared segment.

### 4.10 Limit instances

V51 Constructed soft ends: zero-rate dimension (V11); switching without complete action (V19); single-site loss while another site is in motion (V29, LIM8); same at both ends but changed in between: generation gate 1 → 0.2 → 1, the relations at the two ends being identical, both adjacent steps signing RateDyn, qualification persisting (LIM13); site migration: in the comparison family one member has only source drive in motion and another has only the probability organization in motion, and both have dynamics (LIM14). Construction.

V52 Re-formation after exit from qualification: on the same object the source-drive segment first loses Law-formation and then, through refreshing, undergoes renewed Law-formation; qualification goes 1 → 0 → 1, the exit coming first and the re-formation after, each with its own moment (LIM16). Construction. It supports “Re-formation Is Not Re-entry into the Original Segment” of The Limit Form of Intention: the original segment is QualExit, and the later segment requires re-declaration.

V53 Structural compatibility: no manifestation (LIM3); evidence splicing is not an object boundary (LIM12); knowing only boundary values does not sign an endpoint (LIM15, a sequence of positive rates tending to zero without deriving the zero endpoint); current switching does not imply a cross-time type (V36). These show only that the read-out kernel structurally does not read the corresponding quantity, or that verdict discipline does not make the corresponding inference; they are not actual constructions in a system containing that quantity.

Not constructed: approachable endpoints; this chapter constructs no endpoint that can only be approached and not attained. For a legal segment with running advancing while all three sites are at rest, see V28.

### 4.11 L5 probe

V54 On the same object with qualification holding, the contamination is varied class by class: evidence from a foreign run, from a foreign anchor, from both, or with a mismatched evidence key all make L5 ⊥ while the other five items are 1; in-segment direct writing, anchor change, change of duty identity, change of coverage rule, change of continuity granularity, and exit from qualification in mid-segment followed by recovery fall respectively on L2, L3, L3, L6, L6, L4, while L5 remains 1; all of L1 to L6 are taken from the same certificate segment; among the twelve stress instances and the probes above, no world was found in which L1, L2, L3, L4, L6 are all 1 while L5 is independently 0 at the object layer (stress l5). Finite construction and search within the stress domain.

4.12 Matrix of positive and negative cases

Qualification holds: Intent = 1.  
Location reading open: P\_I = ⊥, Intent = ⊥.  
Sole carrier of a dimension unavailable: Probability = 0, Intent = 0.  
Generation of both dimensions fully closed: zero class, Probability = 0, Intent = 0.  
Zero rate of a single dimension: Probability = 1, Intent = 1.  
Source-drive segment loses Law-formation: SourceDrive = 0, Intent = 0.  
Source-drive registration as a complete external selector: SrcDomainBind = 0.  
One dimension corresponding to two Desire-Gate duty classes: GC2 = 0.  
Desire-Gate duty class without a source direction: GC4 = 0.  
Post-gate candidate as effect output: that gate's TuneAvail = 0.  
Complete organizational event: IntentAct = 1.  
Externally specified dimension: A2 = 0, IntentAct = 0.  
Stopping at the gate: A4 = 0, IntentAct = 0, Z\_T explicitly missing.  
From gate X to gate A: IntentGateSwitch = 1.  
Same-gate continuation: S3 = 0.  
External gate change: S2 = 0.  
Gate change while stopping at the gate: IntentGateSwitch = 1.  
Source-drive solo, carrier solo, probability solo: each of the three sites is 1 on its own.  
In-segment direct writing: L2 = 0.  
Forged Intention operator: L2 = 0, IDyn-M3 fails.  
Change of duty identity within the segment: L3 = 0.  
Foreign evidence: L5 = ⊥.  
Change of continuity granularity within the segment: L6 = 0.  
Exit from qualification in mid-segment followed by recovery: L4 = 0, current verdict ⊥ (QualExitOpen).  
Left-bound positive unit: Start is not evaluated within this declaration.  
Stop command issued without negative coverage: Stop is not signed.  
Source drive, no source drive, source drive: Stop and re-onset after stopping.  
Illegal relation object: PD-M9 fails.  
Make-before-break of the bearer: qualification persisting, ProfileNotApplicable, DimStructDyn.  
Direction rising while the carrier falls: incomparable.

None of these results is obtained by directly rewriting Intent, IntentAct, IntentGateSwitch, IntentDyn, Degree or Rel\_ρ.

## Section Five　Construction Theorems

5.1 Theorem One　Theorem of the Construction of the Intention Operating Substrate

There exists a family of finite, executable Sedimentation-Field running systems Spec\_IB^ref = Augment(F\_ref, A\_Prob^rnd, A\_I) (Appendix A) which, taking as starting point the intention-side structure placed by A\_I and the location declared in advance, and without directly writing into any reading of Intention qualification, action, switching or dynamics:

(1) derive from actual running history and frozen registration Probability, the four sub-items of SourceDrive^M, GC1 to GC5 of GateCorrespondence^M and the gate-by-gate readings of TuneInterface^M, and combine them by ∧₃ with IntentLoc^M, taken from the declaration made in advance, into qualification; and there exist instances giving Intent = 1, giving Intent = ⊥ when the location reading is open, and negative instances in which Probability, SourceDrive^M (through decay of the source-drive segment), SrcDomainBind^M, GC2, GC4 and the gate-by-gate TuneAvail^M are 0;

(2) actually construct and distinguish on the same substrate: complete action and the missing A4 layer, external selection not signing action, A2 being read out on the same probability event from current occurrence and duty conformance; Intention Desire-Gate switching, same-gate continuation, externally forced gate swap and switching without complete action;

(3) have only one unified running certificate in the composite running system, L1, L2, L3, L4, L6 all being read out from the same certificate segment, and L5 auditing the actual load-bearing evidence on the same segment and taking only 1 or ⊥; and actually construct its negative instances: in-segment direct writing, tail direct writing, anchor change or change of duty identity within the segment, change of continuity granularity or coverage rule within the segment, and exit from qualification in mid-segment followed by recovery;

(4) actually construct dynamics worlds in which each of the three sites goes solo, as well as the Stop and re-onset after stopping of source drive and of action derived from positive occurrence intervals, continuity granularity and negative coverage, a left-bound positive unit not signing Start, a command by itself not signing Stop, and re-onset after stopping requiring the whole-segment qualification persistence of the same certificate segment; and construct the process-type witnesses required by Chapter 11: source drive already continuing while complete action first appears, Stop and re-onset after a break at the gate layer and at the tuning layer, re-onset of source drive after stopping, direction rising while the carrier falls, profile not applicable, and legal replacement of bearers;

(5) derive the load-bearing materials of IDyn-M1 to IDyn-M13, including PD-M1 to PD-M13, from the same certificate segment and the same idyn\_certificate; PD-M9 to PD-M12 rebuild and validate the relation object, the formal dimension organization, the binding organization and the rate-bearing organization; the Probability-Dynamics reading is derived as legal segment ∧₃ (ActDyn ∨₃ RateDyn ∨₃ QualStructDyn), each branch passing only through its own dependency gate, and positive witnesses coming only from legal steps recorded by the certificate;

(6) keep all readers out of the causal update of the field, which is tested by differential runs;

(7) ensure that the solvability conditions and verdict routing of the IA-M, IGS-M and IDyn-M families satisfy, on actual events and certificate records, the discipline “one missing item falls back to ⊥, not 0”, with the dynamics verdict signing neither 1 nor 0 when the legal segment has no 0 but has a ⊥; the verdicts give ⊥ under the current misjudgment bounds, and give the corresponding 0 or OutDom under explicit conditional assumptions.

Proof: the construction is given by Section Three, and each step is executable code in Appendix A. (1) By V1 to V11: the five intention-side edges sediment into Law through actual pulses, the two probability dimensions obtain Law-bearing and common binding through formation pulses, the sub-items of qualification are computed from the sedimentary state and frozen registration, and three-valued conjunction lets ⊥ persist. (2) By V12 to V14, V16, V17, V19, V20. (3) By the construction parts of V21, V22, V24, V26, V27. (4) By V29 to V34 and V37 to V39. (5) By the actually derived parts of V21, V40, V45. (6) By V46, V47. (7) By the interface parts of V3, V18, V23, V25, V27, by V41 to V45 and V48 to V50, with the strength of interface check. All regressions pass (Section Eight). □

The strength is distinguished item by item: (1) to (6) are construction, limited to the finite frozen domain of this reference specification, the location-reading-open item of (1) being interface check (V3); (7) is interface check, part of its input being given by declaration or by the tests.

5.2 Proposition One　The Constructed Part of the Qualification Hard Bound
