On this substrate the following parts of the qualification boundary have actually been constructed: the collapse of the dimensional base on the probability side (V4); the zero class on the probability side (V5); the exit of SourceDrive through decay (V6); the composite exit of SourceDrive and GateCorrespondence (V10); and the structural negative instances, under changes of frozen registration, in which SrcDomainBind, GC2, GC4 and the gate-by-gate TuneAvail are each 0 (V7 to V9). The physical boundary crossing of InIntentDomain, the separate boundary crossing of GateCorrespondence and TuneInterface through sedimentation decay, and the general degeneration family of Deg\_G beyond a finite relation domain have not been constructed. So the claim that “all four items of the qualification hard bound have been constructed in the general sense” does not hold.

5.3 Proposition Two　The Limited Conclusion of the L5 Probe

Within the finite construction and the stress domain of V54 in Section Four, no world was found in which L5 fails independently at the object layer; all constructed L5 anomalies are mismatches of evidence identity and fall to ⊥ (EvidenceSplice). This is not a proof that an independent object-layer negative boundary of L5 does not exist. According to the interface of The Limit Form of Intention, when the construction layer finds only evidence mismatch, the upstream is not upgraded on that account.

### 5.4 Handing back

Handed back to The Limit Form of Intention: zero-rate dimension, switching without complete action, single-site loss, the two ends being the same, site migration, and re-formation after exit from qualification all have constructed instances; no manifestation and the like reach only structural compatibility; all three sites at rest on a non-zero-width legal segment without running advance has been constructed, while all three sites at rest with running advance and approachable endpoints have not been constructed; the result of probing the L5 open item is as in Proposition Two.

Handed back to The Determination Form of Intention: the undecided cases under the current misjudgment bounds (ProbabilityOpen, IntentAtomRepOpen, CarrierNegOpen, QualExitOpen, EvidenceSplice, and the subdivision labels falling under QualificationOpen and DeclarationInvalid) appear on instances, and signing under conditionalized assumptions agrees with the signing rules of that chapter (except for the case in which L4 is ⊥ and another item is 0; see V27); the entry of the dynamics verdict is taken from the qualification reading of the unified running certificate at the starting point of the same object. That chapter requires the operating substrate to provide all the legal interfaces needed by the four verdicts; in this chapter whether the three sites are in motion is still fed into the dynamics verdict by the tests, so this chapter does not claim to implement the determiner of that chapter completely.

Handed back to The Inversion Form of Intention: what this chapter gives is an internal interface. The readable internal states are the full-state digests recorded by the unified running certificate, the probability-layer snapshots, the point-by-point qualification readings, the duty-identity and protocol snapshots, the event records and the gate-bearing records; the legal interventions are the legal operation set of the unified running certificate (pulses, Avail, Freeze, Thaw, IdleStep, GenGate, CarrierLink, SourceStart, SourceStop and the various events). This chapter establishes no external output model (there is no manifestation, see LIM3; effect output appears only as a registration kind of tuning candidates), nor does it construct interventions able to separate candidate internal worlds; the external observation interface and the separating interventions required by H\_Π of that chapter are not constructed in this chapter.

Handed back to the several Mathematical Forms: the run and branch identity, events and evidence keys, authoritative operation logs, the unified history of state writing, the coverage of qualification change, duty-identity snapshots, protocol freezing and modification history, positive and negative units of source drive and action, carrier-candidate registration and point-by-point bearing, the common domain of comparison of degree, the object-level materials of Probability-Dynamics, and the source-chain segments and gate-bearing records recorded according to the level actually reached, which Chapter 11 and others require the operating substrate to provide, all have counterparts in the reference implementation; the coverage certificate consists of a part derived from running records and a part given by frozen finite registration, and there is no general automatic exhaustion of all upstream coverage domains.

## Section Six　Strength Declaration

The strength of Theorem One and Proposition One is construction: they give at least one family of real, executable running systems in which the running objects used by the Intention Group-Form have corresponding instances. They are not a Reachability Form: they do not prove that every target state can be reached from every initial state. They are not a Realization Form: they do not assert that Intention in nature always runs by this specific algorithm, nor that this is a production-grade engineering implementation.

Nor are they a generative construction: the intention-side nodes and edges are placed by A\_I, the location is given by declaration in advance, and the Law-formation of the five intention-side edges takes place on a dedicated structure already placed; this chapter does not prove that Intention qualification can first hold in a base Sedimentation-Field without these structures, nor that after qualification holds source drive can occur without an external SourceStart command. These two items belong to Chapter 21.

The following must be limited truthfully:

Location: IntentLoc is given by declaration in advance, and InIntentDomain has no independent physical model; when the location is open, three-valued propagation extends to Intent.

Qualification: GC1 to GC5 are realized on a finite relation domain of two dimensions and two gates, closed by declaration, and have several structural negative instances; they do not exhaust the general degeneration family of GateCorrespondence. The negative instances of SrcDomainBind, GC2, GC4 and the gate-by-gate TuneAvail are obtained by changes of frozen registration, and do not arise naturally from sedimentation decay.

A2: CurrentProbabilityOccurrence^M and ProbabilityDutyConformance^M are derived on the same probability event; duty conformance is read in this construction as “that dimension belongs to some non-empty group of MinSuff\_ρ before the event, and the evaluation is bound to the same run and running moment”; this is one instance, in the reference specification, of the current rate-bearing certificate interface of The Mathematical Form of Probability-Action, not a general implementation of that chapter's certificate.

Coverage: the coverage certificate consists of a part derived from running records and a part given by frozen finite registration, with the source marked; there is no general automatic exhaustion of all upstream coverage domains.

Unified running certificate: what it proves is that, in this reference system, all state writing that occurs through the legal operation interface has been recorded and that the state quantities covered by the digest receive no other writing within the segment; it does not prove that quantities not covered by the digest have not changed, nor is it a proof of write completeness for arbitrary implementations. The formation process before the certificate is established, and the preparations some tests make outside the certificate (such as emptying the degree window or freezing sedimentation), lie within no declared segment.

L3 and L6: L3 compares duty-identity snapshots, L6 compares protocol snapshots and the frozen state; the fields listed in the snapshots are exactly the identity domain and protocol domain frozen by this construction.

L5: reaches only “not found within the finite construction and the stress domain.”

Cross-time classification: the evaluation unit is the single event, δ\_src = δ\_act = 1; a unit confirming no source drive is a current actual event that records a complete route not passing through the source-drive segment. This is a granularity choice of the reference specification.

Carrier: only the main carrier of A has a degree reading; the other candidates are read only for bearing, not for degree.

Tuning: what actually runs in this chapter is the actual traversal of the post-gate tuning segment as a path in the field, and it is this that A4 and TuneInterface^M read; the process in which the Tuning-Field commands the tuning Law to break the holding of the Experience-Field and appearance occurs is not modeled (there is no manifestation; see LIM3 in V53).

Probability-Dynamics: PD-M1 to PD-M13 are derived item by item within the declared segments of this reference construction, PD-M9 to PD-M12 performing object reconstruction and legality checks; the complete-family check of MinSuff depends on the reference choice “the generating factors are exactly the formal dimensions.” The evaluation points are taken as the points on the unified running certificate, and no action is in progress at a state point, so when a single operation is taken as the step, the action branch can give only Idle, NA or ⊥, not Start, Continue or Stop; exposing the onset and end of action requires finer evaluation granularity, which this chapter has not constructed. In the dyn\_compare built into the probability layer, the binding item compares amounts of sedimentation and is not used for the binding branch of this chapter. This is not a general complete implementation of The Mathematical Form of Probability-Dynamics, nor a unified solution for all modes of representation.

Verdicts: L1 to L6 are read out from the unified running certificate; whether the three sites are in motion is supplied by the tests as input to the verdict function; it may not be written as “the four verdicts are signed automatically from raw running records for an arbitrary declared segment.”

Limits: the items of V53 reach only structural compatibility; the legal segment of V28 is construction, but there is no running advance within the segment, and the three sites being at rest is given by declaration.

Inheritance: F\_ref is the executable semantic reconstruction of the appendix of the sealed Law-Dynamics operating substrate, not a byte-level copy of the original fldb\_v1\_12.py; A\_Prob^rnd is the probability-side reference increment, not the reference implementation in the appendix of The Construction Form of the Probability Operating Substrate, nor is it claimed on this basis that the adapter gap registered by that chapter has been closed.

It may not be claimed here, beyond the boundary, that: Intention has been generated by the base Sedimentation-Field on its own, or source drive can occur after qualification holds without an external start command; all natural Intention runs by this algorithm; this substrate is the only ontological realization of Intention; a general automatic determination algorithm for arbitrary physical systems has been given; the unclosed negative-representation conditions of Intention (H2 ∧ H3^red, H^-\_M, H\_ρRep^-, H\_PAct-Rep and the reverse representation of the atoms newly added in this group form) have been closed by the construction; an independent object-layer negative boundary of L5 does not exist; finite regression equals general proof; byte-level inheritance from the original source has been completed.

These choices of the reference implementation are all choices of implementation, not ontology: the topology of nodes and edges, the numbers of formation passes, the Law-formation threshold, the binding coefficient, the form of the generation gate and carrier link, the specific contents of frozen registration, the discretization of events, the evaluation unit and the continuity granularity.

## Section Seven　Breach Method

The appearance of any of the following breaches the corresponding conclusion:

K-IB1　The constructed object fails the condition group: some verification item of Section Four marked as construction does not hold under the code of Appendix A.  
K-IB2　Direct writing: some Intention reading is in fact obtained by direct writing, not computed from the sedimentary state, frozen registration and event records.  
K-IB3　Construction step not executable: some step of Appendix A cannot be executed according to Section Three.  
K-IB4　Causal inversion: invoking a reader changes subsequent actual running, or some intention-side reading enters the update of the field or the probability layer.  
K-IB5　Dangling load-bearing: some Intention reading can change when only the reader or a label is altered while running history is exactly the same.  
K-IB6　Cross-object or cross-segment evidence stitching: material taken from another object, another run, another protocol or another certificate segment is stitched together with the records of this segment to sign a verdict or dynamics.  
K-IB7　False action: an event with an externally specified dimension, an external gate change or a missing layer signs complete action; or the total reading of Probability-Action replaces A2.  
K-IB8　False switching: same-gate continuation or an externally forced gate swap signs Intention Desire-Gate switching, or a missing old-gate exit record is signed as 0.  
K-IB9　False qualification: probability qualification or Intention qualification is signed under the all-zero relation; the qualification reading replaces the duty structure that has formed a Law with current availability; Intent is signed as 0 when some sub-item of qualification is ⊥.  
K-IB10　False dynamics: bearer succession is signed as dynamics of degree of the same bearer; direction rising while the carrier falls is combined into a total strength; current switching is signed as a cross-time source-drive type; the two ends being the same is signed as having no dynamics; a session command is signed as the Stop of source drive; a left-bound positive unit is signed as Start; re-onset after stopping is signed although qualification has not persisted through the whole segment; the Start of Probability-Action is preset by code rather than read out from the points and steps recorded by the certificate; an illegal relation object supports a positive rate witness; a change in the amount of sedimentation is signed as binding-structure dynamics.  
K-IB11　Dual-track audit: the legal segment and Probability-Dynamics or the IDyn interface each sign legality using different running evidence; or the dynamics verdict signs 1 or 0 when the legal segment has no 0 but has a ⊥; or some exit able to sign 1 or 0 bypasses its solvability gate or legal-segment gate.  
K-IB12　Cross-layer operation: an intention-side operation changes the carrier state of the probability layer or the running layer.  
K-IB13　Overstepping in strength: writing an item of interface check or structural compatibility as construction; writing the formation of qualification on a placed structure as the generation of Intention; writing signing under a conditionalized assumption as the condition having been closed; writing a finite search as a theorem of non-existence; writing semantic reconstruction as byte-level inheritance.

Does not constitute a breach: an item marked interface check or structural compatibility holds within its declared strength; ⊥ under the current misjudgment bounds; items not constructed remaining not constructed.

## Section Eight　Regression Tests

The reference implementation consists of ten programs in all, with the following order of dependency:

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;fldb\_work → probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd → probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v2 → intent\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v14 (final reference core) → determination matrix, IA and IGS interface matrix, IDyn interface matrix, limit matrix, process-type witnesses; stress and L5 probe depend in addition on the determination matrix

Each program first runs the upstream programs it depends on; if the upstream does not pass, the tests of this program are not run. Each program resolves its dependencies relative to its own directory and does not depend on any fixed external path.

Running commands (the ten files placed in the same directory, invoked by each file's actual path from any working directory):

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 fldb\_work.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v2.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 intent\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v14.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 intent\_judge\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 intent\_interface\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 intent\_idyn\_interface\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 intent\_limit\_matrix\_rnd\_v14.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 intent\_process\_witness\_rnd\_v14.py  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 intent\_stress\_l5\_rnd\_v14.py

Results (Appendix B):

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;fldb\_work: native fourteen groups all PASS, \[FLDB\_V1\_12\_TOTAL\] PASS  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd: 3 seeds, 36 items, all passed  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;probability\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v2: 3 seeds, 27 items, all passed  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;intent\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v14: core 3 seeds 51 items, unified certificate and Probability-Dynamics 5 seeds 115 items, all passed  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;determination matrix: 5 seeds, 190 items, all passed  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;IA and IGS interface matrix: 5 seeds, 140 items, all passed  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;IDyn interface matrix: 5 seeds, 120 items, all passed  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;limit matrix: 5 seeds, 85 items, all passed  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;process-type witnesses: 5 seeds, 65 items, all passed, open gaps empty  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;stress and L5 probe: 12 stress instances, 84 items, all passed, number of independent L5 object failures found 0

There are 913 explicit downstream checks in all, plus the native fourteen-group regression of the running layer. The return codes of all ten programs are 0.

The load-bearing chain of this chapter is governed solely by the ten files listed in Appendix A.

## Section Nine　Interfaces

Upstream

The General Form of Intention, The Necessity Form of Intention, The Mathematical Form of Intention: the four-item structure of qualification, two-way closure, P\_I^M, SourceDrive^M, GC1 to GC5, TuneInterface^M and three-valued conjunction.  
The General Form of Intention-Action, The Necessity Form of Intention-Action, The Mathematical Form of Intention-Action: the four layers of action, current occurrence and duty conformance of A2, the same chain, IA-M1 to IA-M12.  
The General Form of Intention Desire-Gate Switching, The Necessity Form of Intention Desire-Gate Switching, The Mathematical Form of Intention Desire-Gate Switching: the four items of switching and co-binding, IGS-M1 to IGS-M10.  
The General Form of Intention-Dynamics, The Necessity Form of Intention-Dynamics, The Mathematical Form of Intention-Dynamics: legal segment L1 to L6, the three sites, cross-time classification, the requirements on process-type witnesses, IDyn-M1 to IDyn-M14.  
The Inversion Form of Intention: the division of labor between internal interface and external observation; the read-outs of this chapter are an internal interface, not external observation.  
The Determination Form of Intention: the signing rules, entry rules and misjudgment bounds of the verdicts.  
The Limit Form of Intention: soft-limit families, the hard bound, the applicability boundary and the construction probe of the L5 open item.  
The Construction Form of the Law-Dynamics Operating Substrate, The Construction Form of the Probability Operating Substrate, The Mathematical Form of Probability-Dynamics: the causal chain of the running layer and the probability layer, the running realization, and PD-M1 to PD-M13.

Hand-down

The Reachability Form of Intention: this chapter gives instances that can be read as targets, together with read-out interfaces; whether they can be reached from a given initial state is not studied.  
The Source Form of Intention: this chapter gives the process by which the five intention-side edges sediment into Law through actual traversal on a dedicated structure already placed; the source conditions of the first formation of the Intention structure are not studied.  
The Construction Form of the Intention Generation Substrate (Chapter 21): this chapter gives the operating substrate of an existing Intention structure and its read-out interfaces: the unified running certificate, legal-segment readings, and the derivation of qualification, action, switching, cross-time classification and Probability-Dynamics. After Chapter 21 has constructed in a base Sedimentation-Field the complete history of Intention qualification first holding, it may use these read-out interfaces on the same field to test the subsequent actual running; this chapter provides none of the following: an initial state without Intention, endogenous formation, the moment of first holding, autonomous source drive without an external start command, and deletion tests; for all of these Chapter 21 must give actual running evidence and counterexamples separately. Interface compatibility is not actual generation.  
The Stability Form of Intention: this chapter gives cross-time events, certificates and the derived cross-time classification; persistence, first failure and recovery are not studied.

To-do

After the sealed Law-Dynamics source is obtained, redo the increments of the probability layer and the intention layer on the sealed Spec\_LDB, check that they likewise satisfy the interfaces of 3.1, and run all regressions unchanged.  
Constructions to be supplied: a physical model of InIntentDomain; the separate boundary crossing and general degeneration family of GateCorrespondence and TuneInterface through sedimentation decay; all three sites at rest with running advance; automatic input to the verdicts, from the unified running certificate and actual derivation, of whether the three sites are in motion, so that the dynamics verdict is signed end to end for a declared segment; general enumeration of upstream coverage domains by the coverage certificate; the external output model and the legal interventions separating candidates required by The Inversion Form of Intention. In addition: when L4 is ⊥ and L1, L2, L3, L6 include a 0, change the dynamics verdict to judge ⊥ first (QualExitOpen) according to 7.2 of The Determination Form of Intention, and rerun the regressions (V27, J31).

No negative-representation condition or ontological bridge is claimed, on the strength of the reference implementation, to be closed in general: H2 ∧ H3^red, H^-\_M, H\_ρRep^-, H\_PAct-Rep, H\_LawRead and the reverse representation of the atoms newly added in this group form all remain in the status declared upstream and propagate along the actual paths of the upstream. The “condition closed” switches in the determination matrix are only hypotheses within the tests.

## Appendix A　Reference Implementation Source Code

This chapter is self-contained: from this form-text alone, as a single file, all the source code can be extracted, the model run and the results checked, without depending on any other file. The only environment required is Python 3 and its standard library; no third-party packages need to be installed, and no network is needed. Appendix A contains the source code, Appendix B the running commands and complete outputs, and Appendix C the correspondence table of files and check counts and the reproducer. Reproduce by either of two methods:

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Method One (automatic): save the content between the “文件开始” and “文件结束” lines in Appendix C as reproduce\_p16.py, then save this form-text as a text file (UTF-8), and run  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;python3 reproduce\_p16.py \<path to this form-text file\>  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;The reproducer automatically extracts the ten source files, checks SHA-256, runs them in order of dependency, compares the output of each program with Appendix B verbatim, and finally prints a line beginning “复现结论：”.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Method Two (manual): save the content between the “文件开始” and “文件结束” lines of each file in Appendix A under its original file name in one directory (UTF-8, keeping one newline at the end), check SHA-256, run them in turn with the commands of Appendix B, and compare the outputs with Appendix B.

The criteria of the two methods are the same: all ten programs exit with return code 0, the SHA-256 of all ten source files agrees with this appendix, and all ten outputs are verbatim identical to Appendix B (for two kinds of printing difference see the notes to Appendix B).

The ten files are reproduced in full in order of dependency. Each file begins at the line starting “==== 文件开始” and ends at the line starting “==== 文件结束”; between the two lines is the original source code, without any alteration. The final reference core is intent\_on\_fldb\_rnd\_v14.py; the other intention-side files are acceptance matrices reading the same core.

# fldb_v1_12.py
# Reconstructed from the sealed PDF appendix for executable verification.
import math, random, copy, itertools
from collections import defaultdict, deque

class FLB:
    def __init__(self, nodes, medium, alpha=0.05, beta=0.25, g_max=20.0,
                 lam_d=0.01, lam_g=0.01, theta=1.0, W=30, n_omega=3,
                 kappa0=0.05, w_g=1.0, w_d=0.02, s_weak=0.4, T=0.15, seed=0):
        self.nodes=nodes; self.E=list(medium); self.out=defaultdict(list)
        for e in self.E: self.out[e[0]].append(e)
        self.d={n:0.0 for n in nodes}; self.g={e:0.0 for e in self.E}
        self.rep={e:deque() for e in self.E}; self.Om={e:0.0 for e in self.E}
        self.th={e:theta for e in self.E}; self.Avail={e:1 for e in self.E}
        self.alpha,self.beta,self.g_max,self.lam_d,self.lam_g=alpha,beta,g_max,lam_d,lam_g
        self.W,self.n_omega,self.k0,self.w_g,self.w_d,self.s_weak,self.T=W,n_omega,kappa0,w_g,w_d,s_weak,T
        self.t_run=0; self.t_sed=0; self.SED=True
        self.rng=random.Random(seed); self.log=[]; self.macro=None
        self.mask={e:1 for e in self.E}
    def Decay(self):
        self.t_sed+=1
        for n in self.nodes: self.d[n]=max(0.0,self.d[n]-self.lam_d)
        for e in self.E:
            self.g[e]=max(0.0,self.g[e]-self.lam_g)
            while self.rep[e] and self.t_sed-self.rep[e][0]>self.W: self.rep[e].popleft()
            self.Om[e]=self._omega(e)
    def Trace(self,e,p): return {'e':e,'p':p}
    def Sediment(self,tau):
        e,p=tau['e'],tau['p']
        self.d[e[0]]+=self.alpha*p; self.d[e[1]]+=self.alpha*p
        self.g[e]=min(self.g_max,self.g[e]+self.beta*p*(1-self.g[e]/self.g_max))
        self.rep[e].append(self.t_sed); self.Om[e]=self._omega(e)
    def kappa(self,e):
        if not self.Avail[e]: return -1e9
        return self.k0+self.w_g*self.mask[e]*self.g[e]*(1.0 if self.S(e) else self.s_weak)+self.w_d*self.d[e[1]]
    def Continuation(self,q):
        cand=[e for e in self.out[q] if self.Avail[e]]
        if not cand: return None
        ks=[self.kappa(e)/self.T for e in cand]; m=max(ks); ws=[math.exp(k-m) for k in ks]
        r=self.rng.random()*sum(ws)
        for e,w in zip(cand,ws):
            r-=w
            if r<=0: return e
        return cand[-1]
    def probs(self,q):
        cand=[e for e in self.out[q] if self.Avail[e]]
        ks=[self.kappa(e)/self.T for e in cand]; m=max(ks); ws=[math.exp(k-m) for k in ks]; z=sum(ws)
        return {e[1]:w/z for e,w in zip(cand,ws)}
    def S(self,e): return self.g[e]>=self.th[e]
    def Deposited(self,e): return self.g[e]>0
    def Groove(self,e): return self.S(e) and len(self.rep[e])>=2
    def Gamma(self): return {e for e in self.E if self.Groove(e)}
    def _omega(self,e):
        n=len(self.rep[e]); return 0.0 if (n<self.n_omega or not self.S(e)) else min(1.0,n/(2*self.n_omega))
    def Freeze(self): self.SED=False
    def Thaw(self): self.SED=True
    def SetAvail(self,e,v): self.Avail[e]=v
    def IdleStep(self,n=1):
        for _ in range(n): self.Decay()
    def SetMask(self,e,v): self.mask[e]=v
    def pulse(self,entry,p=1.0,path=None,maxhop=6):
        self.t_run+=1
        if self.SED: self.Decay()
        q=entry; route=[q]; inst=[]
        for h in range(maxhop):
            if path is not None:
                if h>=len(path)-1: break
                e=(path[h],path[h+1])
            else: e=self.Continuation(q)
            if e is None: break
            if not self.Deposited(e): inst.append(e)
            if self.SED: self.Sediment(self.Trace(e,p))
            q=e[1]; route.append(q)
            if not self.out[q]: break
        self.log.append((self.t_run,entry,tuple(route),self.macro))
        return route,inst
    def _closure(self,start,edges):
        adj=defaultdict(list)
        for i,j in edges: adj[i].append(j)
        seen={start}; st=[start]
        while st:
            x=st.pop()
            for y in adj[x]:
                if y not in seen: seen.add(y); st.append(y)
        return seen
    def CarrierRealizes_inst(self,R,duty):
        a=duty['anchor']
        if a not in R or not self.Deposited(a): return False
        dep=[e for e in R if self.Deposited(e)]
        return a[0] in self._closure(duty['ante'],dep) and duty['read'] in self._closure(a[1],dep)
    def Established_inst(self,R,duty):
        return set(R)<=set(self.E) and all(self.Deposited(e) for e in R) and self.CarrierRealizes_inst(R,duty)
    def MediumReach_inst(self,entry,Q):
        seen=self._closure(entry,[e for e in self.E if self.Avail[e]])
        return any(self.Avail[e] and e[0] in seen for e in Q)
    def RolePath_inst(self,R,duty):
        a=duty['anchor']
        if a not in R or not (self.Deposited(a) and self.mask[a]): return False
        open_edges=[e for e in R if self.Deposited(e) and self.mask[e]]
        return a[0] in self._closure(duty['ante'],open_edges) and duty['read'] in self._closure(a[1],open_edges)

def OmegaSupport_pre(F,entry,Q):
    return F.MediumReach_inst(entry,Q) and any(F.Om[e]>0 for e in Q)

def hit_prob(F,entry,target,n=2000):
    G=copy.deepcopy(F); G.Freeze(); c=0
    for _ in range(n): c += target in G.pulse(entry)[0]
    return c/n

def DynamicRole_inst(F,R,duty,control_R=None,n=2000,delta=0.15):
    if not F.RolePath_inst(R,duty): return False, {}
    base=hit_prob(F,duty['ante'],duty['read'],n)
    L=copy.deepcopy(F)
    for e in R: L.SetMask(e,0)
    pm=hit_prob(L,duty['ante'],duty['read'],n)
    inv=(L.g==F.g and L.Om==F.Om and {e:len(L.rep[e]) for e in L.E}=={e:len(F.rep[e]) for e in F.E})
    pc=None
    if control_R:
        C=copy.deepcopy(F)
        for e in control_R: C.SetMask(e,0)
        pc=hit_prob(C,duty['ante'],duty['read'],n)
    ok=inv and (base-pm)>delta and (pc is None or abs(base-pc)<=delta)
    return ok,dict(P_hat_base=base,P_hat_Rmask=pm,P_hat_ctrlmask=pc,invariants=inv)

# ---------- G6 decision core: ljudge.py v1.0 logic, kernel routed through F.kappa ----------
# Reconstructed from the sealed appendix.
MAXHOP = 6


def kernel(F, q, avail=None, mask=None):
    """K_y at position q: distribution over out-edges, from state only (exact).
    Uses F.kappa so that whatever the subclass actually runs is what gets judged.
    """
    avail = avail or F.Avail
    cand = [e for e in F.out[q] if avail[e]]
    if not cand: return {}
    ks = [F.kappa(e)/F.T for e in cand]
    m = max(ks); ws = [math.exp(k-m) for k in ks]; z = sum(ws)
    return {e: w/z for e, w in zip(cand, ws)}

def eval_paths(F, entry, edges_allowed=None, maxhop=MAXHOP):
    """Exhaustive Eval(Ctx): all routes with exact mu. No Monte Carlo."""
    out = []
    def rec(q, route, prob, hops):
        K = kernel(F, q)
        if edges_allowed is not None:
            K = {e: p for e, p in K.items() if e in edges_allowed}
            z = sum(K.values())
            if z == 0:
                out.append((tuple(route), prob, 'dead')); return
            K = {e: p/z for e, p in K.items()}  # PR1 restricted re-normalisation inside P
        if not K or hops >= maxhop:
            out.append((tuple(route), prob, 'end')); return
        for e, p in K.items(): rec(e[1], route+[e[1]], prob*p, hops+1)
    rec(entry, [entry], 1.0, 0)
    return out

def mu_read(F, entry, target, edges_allowed=None):
    return sum(p for r, p, _ in eval_paths(F, entry, edges_allowed) if target in r)

def actargs_at(F, q, eps=0.01):
    """Minimal mu-sufficient groups over per-edge state fixings at position q."""
    cand = [e for e in F.out[q] if F.Avail[e]]
    if not cand: return set(), []
    base = {e: F.g[e] for e in cand}
    def dist(gvals):
        for e in cand: F.g[e] = gvals[e]
        try:
            ks = [F.kappa(e)/F.T for e in cand]
        finally:
            for e in cand: F.g[e] = base[e]
        m = max(ks); ws = [math.exp(k-m) for k in ks]; z = sum(ws)
        return [w/z for w in ws]
    def tv(p, qq): return 0.5*sum(abs(a-b) for a, b in zip(p, qq))
    def sufficient(C):
        freevars = [e for e in cand if e not in C]
        if not freevars: return True
        grids = [[0.0, F.g_max] for _ in freevars]
        dists = []
        for combo in itertools.product(*grids):
            gv = dict(base); gv.update({e: v for e, v in zip(freevars, combo)})
            for e in C: gv[e] = base[e]
            dists.append(dist(gv))
        return max(tv(a, b) for a in dists for b in dists) <= eps
    minimal = []
    for r in range(len(cand)+1):
        for C in itertools.combinations(cand, r):
            if sufficient(set(C)) and not any(set(M) <= set(C) for M in minimal):
                minimal.append(C)
    return set(u for M in minimal for u in M), minimal

def duty(ante, anchor, read): return dict(ante=ante, anchor=anchor, read=read)

def _judge_P(F, L, P, R, pi0, eps):
    """CR / CNI / Dyn / OmegaSupport on one implementation P subset R."""
    ante, anchor, read = L['ante'], L['anchor'], L['read']; d = {}
    m_res = mu_read(F, ante, read, edges_allowed=set(P))
    d['P'] = [f"{e[0]}->{e[1]}" for e in sorted(P)]
    d['CR'] = dict(anchor_in_P=anchor in P, mu_restricted=round(m_res, 4), pi0=pi0)
    if not (anchor in P and m_res >= pi0): return 0, d
    assert all(F.g[e] > 0 for e in P), "CNI reduction premise violated"
    d['CNI'] = dict(by_reduction_lemma=True, Q_subset_Estab=True)
    aa, minimal = actargs_at(F, anchor[0], eps)
    adj = defaultdict(list)
    for (i, j) in P:
        if F.g[(i, j)] > 0 and F.mask[(i, j)]: adj[i].append(j)
    seen = {anchor[1]}; st = [anchor[1]]
    while st:
        x = st.pop()
        for y2 in adj[x]:
            if y2 not in seen: seen.add(y2); st.append(y2)
    rolepath = (anchor in aa) and (read in seen or read == anchor[1])
    d['Dyn'] = dict(ActArg_at_anchor_tail=[f"{e[0]}->{e[1]}" for e in sorted(aa)],
                    minimal_groups=[[f"{e[0]}->{e[1]}" for e in M] for M in minimal],
                    anchor_in_ActArg=anchor in aa, rolepath=rolepath)
    if not rolepath: return 2, d
    seenA = F._closure(ante, [e for e in F.E if F.Avail[e]])
    reach = any(F.Avail[e] and e[0] in seenA for e in P)
    bear = [e for e in P if e == anchor or (anchor[1] == e[0] or e[0] in seen)]
    omega_ok = all(F.Om[e] > 0 for e in bear)
    d['OmegaSupport'] = dict(Reach=reach, Bear=[f"{e[0]}->{e[1]}" for e in bear],
                             Omega={f"{e[0]}->{e[1]}":round(F.Om[e], 2) for e in bear})
    if not reach: return 3, dict(d, fail='Reach')
    if not omega_ok: return 3, dict(d, fail='OmegaOK')
    return 4, d

def judge(F, L, R, I, pi0=0.9, eps=0.01, verbose=False):
    """Decision domain: single-duty, single-entry I={Ante}, measure-type candidate."""
    T = {}
    if list(I) != [L['ante']]:
        T['fail_at'] = 'bot (outside decision domain: single duty, single entry I={Ante})'
        return None, T
    F = copy.deepcopy(F); F.Freeze()
    T['Y'] = {e: (round(F.g[e], 2), int(F.S(e)), round(F.Om[e], 2), F.Avail[e], F.mask[e])
              for e in F.E if F.g[e] > 0 or e in R}
    cc = len(R) > 0 and all(e in F.E for e in R) and all(F.g[e] > 0 for e in R)
    T['CC'] = cc
    if not cc: return _out('CC', T, verbose)
    anchor = L['anchor']; best = (-1, None)
    for r in range(1, len(R)+1):
        for Pt in itertools.combinations(R, r):
            if anchor not in Pt: continue
            depth, detail = _judge_P(F, L, set(Pt), R, pi0, eps)
            if depth > best[0]: best = (depth, detail)
            if depth == 4: break
        if best[0] == 4: break
    depth, detail = best
    for k in ('P', 'CR', 'CNI', 'Dyn', 'OmegaSupport'):
        if detail and k in detail: T[k] = detail[k]
    if depth == 4:
        T['Bind'] = dict(by_collapse_lemma=True, value=True)
        T['verdict'] = 'R in S'
        return _out(None, T, verbose)
    fail = ['CR', 'CNI', 'Dyn',
            ('OmegaSupport(Reach)' if detail and detail.get('fail') == 'Reach' else 'OmegaSupport(OmegaOK)')][depth if depth < 3 else 3]
    return _out(fail, T, verbose)

def _out(fail, T, verbose):
    T['fail_at'] = fail
    if verbose:
        print("PASS R in S" if fail is None else f"FAIL @ {fail}")
        for k, v in T.items():
            if k != 'fail_at': print(f" {k} = {v}")
    return (fail is None), T

def judge_G6(F, R, L, I, pi0=0.9, eps=0.01, verbose=False):
    return judge(F, L, R, I, pi0, eps, verbose)

def corr_M(F,a,b):
    ev=defaultdict(lambda:[False,False])
    for _,_,r,m in F.log:
        if m is None: continue
        if a in r: ev[m][0]=True
        if b in r: ev[m][1]=True
    both=sum(1 for x,y in ev.values() if x and y); either=sum(1 for x,y in ev.values() if x or y)
    return both/either if either else 0.0

MED=[('a1','m1'),('a1','m2'),('a2','m1'),
('a2','m2'),('m1','c'),('m1','x'),('m2','x'),
('m2','c'),('u','y'),('u','z'),('y','w'),('z','w'),
('y','z')]
NODES=['a1','a2','u','m1','m2','c','x','y','z','w']



# conservative extension topology. These edges are unavailable when ld_enabled=False.
EXTRA_NODES=['r','o']
EXTRA_EDGES=[('c','r'),('r','a1'),('c','o')]


class FLDB(FLB):
    """One unified conservative extension of FLB.
    The same d/g/rep/Om/Avail/Mask/t_run/t_sed state carries both the sealed
    FLB regression duties and the law-dynamics witnesses. No second field model is embedded here.
    """
    def __init__(self, nodes, medium, *args, ld_enabled=False,
                 coupling_enabled=True, zscore=0.30,
                 alpha_c=.10, mu_c=.055, eta_c=.085, nu_c=.10,
                 zmin=.08, dt_c=.05, degree_window=12, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(nodes, medium, *args, **kwargs)
        self.ld_enabled=ld_enabled
        self.coupling_enabled=coupling_enabled
        self.zscore=zscore
        self.alpha_c,self.mu_c,self.eta_c,self.nu_c=alpha_c,mu_c,eta_c,nu_c
        self.zmin,self.dt_c=zmin,dt_c
        self.pathdyn={'A':[1.0,.70], 'X':[1.0,.70]}  # [Omega_c, Z]
        self.degree_trace=deque(maxlen=degree_window)
        for e in EXTRA_EDGES:
            if e in self.Avail:
                self.Avail[e]=1 if ld_enabled else 0

    @staticmethod
    def _branch_for_edge(e):
        if e in (('a1','m1'),('m1','c')): return 'A'
        if e in (('a1','m2'),('m2','x')): return 'X'
        return None

    def kappa(self,e):
        base=super().kappa(e)
        if base < -1e8 or not (self.ld_enabled and self.coupling_enabled):
            return base
        k=self._branch_for_edge(e)
        if k is None:
            return base
        oc,z=self.pathdyn[k]
        bear=self.g[e]/self.g_max if self.g_max>0 else 0.0
        return base + self.mask[e]*bear*self.zscore*(oc/max(z,self.zmin))

    def _update_pathdyn(self,active):
        if not self.ld_enabled:
            return
        for k,(oc,z) in list(self.pathdyn.items()):
            a=1.0 if k==active else 0.0
            oc=max(.05,oc); z=max(self.zmin,z)
            doc=self.alpha_c*a*oc/z - self.mu_c*z/oc
            dz=self.eta_c*z/oc - self.nu_c*a*oc
            self.pathdyn[k]=[
                max(.05,oc+self.dt_c*doc),
                max(self.zmin,z+self.dt_c*dz)
            ]

    def pulse(self,entry,p=1.0,path=None,maxhop=12):
        route,inst=super().pulse(entry,p,path,maxhop)
        active=None
        for e in zip(route,route[1:]):
            if e==('a1','m1'): active='A'; break
            if e==('a1','m2'): active='X'; break
        if active is not None:
            self._update_pathdyn(active)
        entry_edge=self.PathMap('L1','R1')['carrier_edges'][0]
        N_t=0; R_t=0
        for e in zip(route,route[1:]):
            if e==entry_edge:
                if N_t==0: N_t=1
                else: R_t+=1
        a_flow=sum(1 for e in zip(route,route[1:]) if e==('a1','m1'))
        x_flow=sum(1 for e in zip(route,route[1:]) if e==('a1','m2'))
        self.degree_trace.append((N_t,R_t,a_flow,x_flow,tuple(route)))
        return route,inst

    def PathMap(self,law_id='L1',carrier_id='R1'):
        table={
            ('L1','R1'): dict(branch='A', depth_nodes=('m1','c'),
                              carrier_edges=(('a1','m1'),('m1','c')))
        }
        return table[(law_id,carrier_id)]

    def LawCertificateG6(self):
        return judge(self, L1, R1, ['a1'])

    def LawA(self):
        ok,_=self.LawCertificateG6()
        return ok

    def DegreeA(self):
        pm=self.PathMap('L1','R1')
        dn=pm['depth_nodes']
        delta=sum(self.d[n] for n in dn)/len(dn)
        W=list(self.degree_trace)
        sumN=sum(x[0] for x in W); sumR=sum(x[1] for x in W)
        reentry=(sumR/sumN) if sumN else None
        z=self.pathdyn[pm['branch']][1]
        af=sum(x[2] for x in W); xf=sum(x[3] for x in W)
        share=af/(af+xf) if (af+xf) else None
        return (delta,reentry,z,share)

    def exec_eval_segment(self,ops):
        d0=self.DegreeA(); r0=self.t_run; s0=self.t_sed
        law_trace=[('start',self.t_run,self.t_sed,self.LawA())]
        op_trace=[]
        for op in ops:
            kind=op[0]
            r_before=self.t_run; s_before=self.t_sed
            if kind=='pulse':
                kwargs=op[1] if len(op)>1 else {}
                route,_=self.pulse('a1',**kwargs)
                op_trace.append(('pulse',r_before,s_before,tuple(route)))
            elif kind=='idle':
                n=op[1] if len(op)>1 else 1
                self.IdleStep(n)
                op_trace.append(('idle',r_before,s_before,n))
            else:
                raise ValueError(kind)
            law_trace.append((kind,self.t_run,self.t_sed,self.LawA()))
        zeta=tuple(t[1] for t in op_trace if t[0]=='pulse')
        d1=self.DegreeA()
        return dict(D0=d0,D1=d1,r0=r0,r1=self.t_run,s0=s0,s1=self.t_sed,
                    r_span=self.t_run-r0,zeta=zeta,ops=tuple(op_trace),
                    law_trace=tuple(law_trace),legal=all(x[3] for x in law_trace))

    def ActionA(self,route):
        es=set(zip(route,route[1:]))
        return int(set(R1)<=es)

    def dyn_snapshot(self):
        return dict(
            t_run=self.t_run,t_sed=self.t_sed,SED=self.SED,
            d=tuple((n,round(self.d[n],12)) for n in self.nodes),
            g=tuple((e,round(self.g[e],12)) for e in self.E),
            rep=tuple((e,tuple(self.rep[e])) for e in self.E),
            Om=tuple((e,round(self.Om[e],12)) for e in self.E),
            Avail=tuple((e,self.Avail[e]) for e in self.E),
            mask=tuple((e,self.mask[e]) for e in self.E),
            pdyn=tuple((k,round(v[0],12),round(v[1],12)) for k,v in sorted(self.pathdyn.items())),
            routes=tuple(x[2] for x in self.log),
        )

def fresh_cls(cls,seed=0):
    if cls is FLB: return FLB(NODES,MED,seed=seed)
    return FLDB(NODES+EXTRA_NODES,MED+EXTRA_EDGES,seed=seed,ld_enabled=False)

R1=[('a1','m1'),('m1','c')]; Q1=[('m1','c')]
L1=dict(ante='a1',anchor=('m1','c'),read='c')
Rcorr=[('y','z')]
Lcorr=dict(ante='y',anchor=('y','z'),read='z')

def _probs(F,q): return {k:round(v,3) for k,v in F.probs(q).items()}

def _lb1_field(cls,seed=1):
    F=fresh_cls(cls,seed)
    for k in range(60):
        F.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m1','c']); F.pulse('a2',1.0,path=['a2','m2','x'])
    return F

def run_suite(cls):
    def test_preserve_C1_C7():
        out={}; F=fresh_cls(cls,7); F.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m1','c'])
        out['C1_C2_trace_to_sediment']=F.g[('a1','m1')]>0 and F.g[('m1','c')]>0 and F.d['m1']>0
        k_before=F.kappa(('m1','c')); F.pulse('u',1.0,path=['u','y','w'])
        out['C3_persist_into_next_kappa']=F.g[('m1','c')]>0 and abs(F.kappa(('m1','c'))-k_before)<0.02
        def hist(h1):
            H=fresh_cls(cls,8)
            for _ in range(30):
                if h1:
                    H.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m1']); H.pulse('a2',1.0,path=['a2','m2'])
                else:
                    H.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m2']); H.pulse('a2',1.0,path=['a2','m1'])
            return H
        H1,H2=hist(True),hist(False)
        same_d=all(abs(H1.d[n]-H2.d[n])<0.02 for n in NODES)
        diff_g=H1.probs('a1')['m1']>0.9 and H2.probs('a1')['m1']<0.1
        out['C4_relation_in_g_not_d']=same_d and diff_g
        Fm=copy.deepcopy(H1); Fm.SetMask(('a1','m1'),0); Fm.SetMask(('a2','m2'),0)
        out['C5_history_governs']=H1.probs('a1')['m1']>0.9 and abs(Fm.probs('a1')['m1']-0.5)<0.05
        D=copy.deepcopy(H1); g0=D.g[('a1','m1')]; D.IdleStep(50); out['C6_decay']=D.g[('a1','m1')]<g0
        r,_=H1.pulse('a1'); out['C7_endogenous_transition']=len(r)>=2 and r[0]=='a1'
        return all(v for k,v in out.items() if k.startswith('C')),out

    def test_LB1():
        F=_lb1_field(cls); e=('m1','c')
        out=dict(N_total=F.t_run,t_sed=F.t_sed,g_m1c=round(F.g[e],2),rep=len(F.rep[e]),Om=F.Om[e],
                 Groove=F.Groove(e),Est=F.Established_inst(R1,L1),Reach=F.MediumReach_inst('a1',Q1),
                 RolePath=F.RolePath_inst(R1,L1),OmegaSupport_pre=OmegaSupport_pre(F,'a1',Q1),
                 P_hat_c_a1=hit_prob(F,'a1','c'),g_a1m2=F.g[('a1','m2')])
        ok=out['Groove'] and out['Est'] and out['Reach'] and out['RolePath'] and out['OmegaSupport_pre'] and out['P_hat_c_a1']>0.9 and out['g_a1m2']==0
        return ok,out

    def test_LB2():
        H=fresh_cls(cls,2); H.pulse('a1',20.0,path=['a1','m1','c']); H.Freeze(); e=('m1','c')
        probe=[tuple(H.pulse('a1')[0]) for _ in range(3)]
        H2=fresh_cls(cls,2)
        for _ in range(20): H2.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m1','c'])
        dyn,dd=DynamicRole_inst(H,R1,L1,control_R=[('a2','m2'),('m2','x')])
        out=dict(g=round(H.g[e],2),rep=len(H.rep[e]),Om=H.Om[e],Groove=H.Groove(e),Est=H.Established_inst(R1,L1),
                 RolePath=H.RolePath_inst(R1,L1),probe=probe,DynamicRole_inst=dyn,**dd,
                 OmegaSupport_pre=OmegaSupport_pre(H,'a1',Q1),ctrl_g=round(H2.g[e],2),ctrl_Om=H2.Om[e],ctrl_Groove=H2.Groove(e))
        ok=out['Om']==0 and not out['Groove'] and out['Est'] and dyn and not out['OmegaSupport_pre'] and all(p==('a1','m1','c') for p in probe) and out['ctrl_Om']>0
        return ok,out

    def test_LB3():
        F=_lb1_field(cls); F.Freeze(); L=copy.deepcopy(F); L.SetMask(('a1','m1'),0); L.SetMask(('m1','c'),0)
        C=copy.deepcopy(F); C.SetMask(('a2','m2'),0); C.SetMask(('m2','x'),0)
        dyn,dd=DynamicRole_inst(F,R1,L1,control_R=[('a2','m2'),('m2','x')])
        out=dict(R_mask_g_unchanged=L.g==F.g,
                 R_mask_rep_unchanged={e:len(L.rep[e]) for e in L.E}=={e:len(F.rep[e]) for e in F.E},
                 R_mask_Om_unchanged=L.Om==F.Om,RolePath_after_mask=L.RolePath_inst(R1,L1),DynamicRole_inst=dyn)
        ok=dyn and out['R_mask_g_unchanged'] and out['R_mask_rep_unchanged'] and out['R_mask_Om_unchanged'] and not out['RolePath_after_mask']
        return ok,out

    def test_LB4():
        K=fresh_cls(cls,3)
        for k in range(60):
            K.macro=k; K.pulse('u',1.0,path=['u','y','w']); K.pulse('u',1.0,path=['u','z','w'])
        K.macro=None; K.Freeze()
        K3=copy.deepcopy(K); K3.SetMask(('y','w'),0); K3.SetMask(('y','z'),0)
        K2=copy.deepcopy(K); K2.SetMask(('u','z'),0)
        K4=copy.deepcopy(K); K4.SetMask(('u','y'),0)
        dyn,_=DynamicRole_inst(K,Rcorr,Lcorr)
        out=dict(Corr_M=corr_M(K,'y','z'),Reach_y_z=K.MediumReach_inst('y',Rcorr),g_yz=K.g[('y','z')],
                 RolePath_Lcorr=K.RolePath_inst(Rcorr,Lcorr),Est_Lcorr=K.Established_inst(Rcorr,Lcorr),
                 DynamicRole_inst_Lcorr=dyn,u=_probs(K,'u'),u_after_y_mask=_probs(K3,'u'),u_after_uz_mask=_probs(K2,'u'))
        ok=out['Corr_M']==1.0 and out['Reach_y_z'] and not out['RolePath_Lcorr'] and out['u']==out['u_after_y_mask'] and out['u_after_uz_mask'].get('z',1)==0
        return ok,out

    def test_LB5():
        F=_lb1_field(cls); e=('m1','c')
        H=fresh_cls(cls,2); H.pulse('a1',20.0,path=['a1','m1','c'])
        Wk=fresh_cls(cls,5)
        for _ in range(4): Wk.pulse('a1',0.2,path=['a1','m1','c'])
        W0=fresh_cls(cls,5); W0.th={x:0.0 for x in W0.E}
        for _ in range(4): W0.pulse('a1',0.2,path=['a1','m1','c'])
        out=dict(rep_LB1=len(F.rep[e]),Om_LB1=F.Om[e],rep_strong=len(H.rep[e]),Om_strong=H.Om[e],S_strong=H.S(e),
                 weak_g=round(Wk.g[e],2),weak_rep=len(Wk.rep[e]),weak_S=Wk.S(e),weak_Groove=Wk.Groove(e),weak_Om=Wk.Om[e],
                 theta0_Groove=W0.Groove(e),theta0_Om=W0.Om[e])
        ok=out['Om_LB1']>0 and out['Om_strong']==0 and out['S_strong'] and out['weak_rep']>=Wk.n_omega and not out['weak_S'] and out['weak_Om']==0 and not out['weak_Groove'] and out['theta0_Om']>0
        return ok,out

    def test_LB6():
        J=_lb1_field(cls); before=J.MediumReach_inst('a1',Q1); J.SetAvail(('a1','m1'),0)
        out=dict(Reach_before=before,Reach_after=J.MediumReach_inst('a1',Q1),Est_after=J.Established_inst(R1,L1),Om_after=J.Om[('m1','c')],g_after=round(J.g[('a1','m1')],2))
        ok=before and not out['Reach_after'] and out['Est_after'] and out['Om_after']>0
        return ok,out

    def test_LB7():
        J=_lb1_field(cls); J.SetAvail(('a1','m1'),0)
        out=dict(Est=J.Established_inst(R1,L1),Om_m1c=J.Om[('m1','c')],Reach=J.MediumReach_inst('a1',Q1),
                 OmegaSupport_pre=OmegaSupport_pre(J,'a1',Q1),P_hat_c_a1=hit_prob(J,'a1','c'))
        ok=out['Est'] and out['Om_m1c']>0 and not out['Reach'] and not out['OmegaSupport_pre'] and out['P_hat_c_a1']==0
        return ok,out

    def test_LB8():
        Z=_lb1_field(cls); Z.Freeze(); g0=dict(Z.g); d0=dict(Z.d); O0=dict(Z.Om); r0={e:len(Z.rep[e]) for e in Z.E}; ts=Z.t_sed
        for _ in range(500): Z.pulse('a1')
        out=dict(t_run=Z.t_run,t_sed=(ts,Z.t_sed),g_unchanged=g0==Z.g,d_unchanged=d0==Z.d,
                 rep_unchanged=r0=={e:len(Z.rep[e]) for e in Z.E},Om_unchanged=O0==Z.Om,
                 P_c_a1=hit_prob(Z,'a1','c'),from_m1=tuple(Z.pulse('m1')[0]))
        ok=out['g_unchanged'] and out['d_unchanged'] and out['rep_unchanged'] and out['Om_unchanged'] and out['t_sed'][0]==out['t_sed'][1] and out['P_c_a1']>0.9
        return ok,out

    def test_LB9():
        D=_lb1_field(cls); e=('m1','c'); g0=round(D.g[e],2); r0=len(D.rep[e]); O0=D.Om[e]; G0=D.Groove(e); ts=D.t_sed; tr=D.t_run; old=D.Gamma(); D.IdleStep(400)
        out=dict(t_run=(tr,D.t_run),t_sed=(ts,D.t_sed),g=(g0,round(D.g[e],2)),rep=(r0,len(D.rep[e])),Om=(O0,D.Om[e]),Groove=(G0,D.Groove(e)),
                 old_grooves_rep_all_zero=all(len(D.rep[x])==0 for x in old),unused_edges_rep_zero=all(len(D.rep[x])==0 for x in D.E if D.g[x]==0))
        ok=D.g[e]<g0 and len(D.rep[e])==0 and D.Om[e]==0 and not D.Groove(e) and out['old_grooves_rep_all_zero'] and out['unused_edges_rep_zero'] and out['t_run'][0]==out['t_run'][1]
        return ok,out

    tests=[test_preserve_C1_C7,test_LB1,test_LB2,test_LB3,test_LB4,test_LB5,test_LB6,test_LB7,test_LB8,test_LB9]
    results=[]
    for t in tests:
        ok,out=t(); results.append((t.__name__,bool(ok),out))
    return results

# Unified FLDB v1.12 acceptance suite -- every new witness uses FLDB itself.

def cmp_degree(a,b,e=1e-9):
    if None in a or None in b:return 'NA'
    aa=(a[0],a[1],-a[2],a[3]); bb=(b[0],b[1],-b[2],b[3])
    ge=all(bb[i]>=aa[i]-e for i in range(4)); gt=any(bb[i]>aa[i]+e for i in range(4))
    le=all(aa[i]>=bb[i]-e for i in range(4)); lt=any(aa[i]>bb[i]+e for i in range(4))
    if ge and gt:return 'Rise'
    if le and lt:return 'Fall'
    if not ge and not le:return 'Migration'
    return 'Equal'

def new_operational(seed=0,coupling=True):
    return FLDB(NODES+EXTRA_NODES,MED+EXTRA_EDGES,seed=seed,
                ld_enabled=True,coupling_enabled=coupling,zscore=.30)

def _set_loop(F,on=True):
    for e in EXTRA_EDGES:
        F.SetAvail(e,1 if on else 0)

def form_A(F,na=60,nx=0):
    _set_loop(F,False)
    for _ in range(na): F.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m1','c'])
    for _ in range(nx): F.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m2','x'])
    _set_loop(F,True)
    return F

def test_g6_five_cases():
    def lb1(seed=1):
        F=FLB(NODES,MED,seed=seed)
        for _ in range(60):
            F.pulse('a1',1.0,path=['a1','m1','c']); F.pulse('a2',1.0,path=['a2','m2','x'])
        return F
    out={}
    v,T=judge(lb1(),L1,R1,['a1']); out['positive']=(v,T.get('fail_at'))
    H=FLB(NODES,MED,seed=2); H.pulse('a1',20.0,path=['a1','m1','c'])
    v2,T2=judge(H,L1,R1,['a1']); out['strong_impulse']=(v2,T2.get('fail_at'))
    K=FLB(NODES,MED,seed=3)
    for _ in range(60):
        K.pulse('u',1.0,path=['u','y','w']); K.pulse('u',1.0,path=['u','z','w'])
    v3,T3=judge(K,duty('y',('y','z'),'z'),[('y','z')],['y']); out['pure_corr']=(v3,T3.get('fail_at'))
    J=lb1(); J.SetAvail(('a1','m1'),0)
    v4,T4=judge(J,L1,R1,['a1']); out['abandoned']=(v4,T4.get('fail_at'))
    W=FLB(NODES,MED,seed=5)
    for _ in range(4): W.pulse('a1',0.2,path=['a1','m1','c'])
    v5,T5=judge(W,L1,R1,['a1']); out['weak_trace']=(v5,T5.get('fail_at'))
    v6,T6=judge(lb1(),L1,R1,['a1','a2']); out['domain_guard']=(v6,T6.get('fail_at'))
    ok=(out['positive']==(True,None)
        and out['strong_impulse'][1]=='OmegaSupport(OmegaOK)'
        and out['pure_corr'][1]=='CC'
        and out['abandoned'][1]=='CR'
        and out['weak_trace'][1]=='OmegaSupport(OmegaOK)'
        and out['domain_guard'][0] is None)
    return ok,out

def test_law_state_sensitivity():
    F=form_A(new_operational(0),60,10); F.Freeze()
    g0={e:F.g[e] for e in F.E}; rep0={e:len(F.rep[e]) for e in F.E}
    pd0={k:tuple(v) for k,v in F.pathdyn.items()}
    v0,_=F.LawCertificateG6()
    kap0={e:F.kappa(e) for e in F.out['m1'] if F.Avail[e]}
    for _ in range(40): F.pulse('a1')
    sed_same=(all(abs(F.g[e]-g0[e])<1e-12 for e in F.E)
              and all(len(F.rep[e])==rep0[e] for e in F.E))
    pd_moved=any(tuple(F.pathdyn[k])!=pd0[k] for k in pd0)
    kap1={e:F.kappa(e) for e in F.out['m1'] if F.Avail[e]}
    kappa_moved=any(abs(kap1[e]-kap0[e])>1e-12 for e in kap0)
    v1,_=F.LawCertificateG6()
    out=dict(sediment_unchanged=sed_same,pathdyn_moved=pd_moved,
             kappa_moved=kappa_moved,verdict_before=v0,verdict_after=v1,
             no_cache=not hasattr(F,'_law_cache'))
    ok=(sed_same and pd_moved and kappa_moved and out['no_cache'])
    return ok,out

def test_regression_full():
    a=run_suite(FLB); b=run_suite(FLDB)
    ok=(len(a)==len(b) and all(x[0]==y[0] and x[1] and y[1] and x[2]==y[2] for x,y in zip(a,b)))
    return ok,dict(old=[(n,o) for n,_,o in a],new=[(n,o) for n,_,o in b])

def test_six_events_unified():
    out={}
    A=form_A(new_operational(101),60,0)
    r0,_=A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x']); a0=A.ActionA(r0)
    r1,_=A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); a1=A.ActionA(r1)
    r2,_=A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); a2=A.ActionA(r2)
    r3,_=A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x']); a3=A.ActionA(r3)
    out['Start']=(a0,a1)==(0,1) and A.LawA()
    out['Continue']=(a1,a2)==(1,1) and A.LawA()
    out['Stop']=(a2,a3)==(1,0) and A.LawA()
    out['act_routes']=(r0,r1,r2,r3)
    R=form_A(new_operational(0),60,10); prev=R.DegreeA(); rise=None
    for i in range(80):
        route,_=R.pulse('a1'); cur=R.DegreeA(); typ=cmp_degree(prev,cur)
        if typ=='Rise' and R.LawA(): rise=(i,prev,cur,route); break
        prev=cur
    out['Rise']=rise is not None; out['rise_witness']=rise
    F=form_A(new_operational(0),60,5); before=F.DegreeA(); law0=F.LawA()
    F.IdleStep(1)
    route,_=F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x']); after=F.DegreeA(); law1=F.LawA()
    fall=(cmp_degree(before,after)=='Fall' and law0 and law1)
    out['Fall']=fall; out['fall_witness']=(before,after,route,law0,law1)
    M=form_A(new_operational(0),60,0); M.degree_trace.clear()
    M.SetAvail(('c','r'),1); M.SetAvail(('r','a1'),1); M.SetAvail(('c','o'),0)
    route1,_=M.pulse('a1'); d1=M.DegreeA(); law1=M.LawA()
    M.SetAvail(('c','r'),0); M.SetAvail(('c','o'),1)
    route2,_=M.pulse('a1'); d2=M.DegreeA(); law2=M.LawA()
    out['Migration']=(cmp_degree(d1,d2)=='Migration' and law1 and law2)
    out['migration_witness']=(d1,d2,route1,route2,law1,law2)
    out['true_loop']=(M.g[('c','r')]>0 and M.g[('r','a1')]>0)
    ok=all(out[k] for k in ['Start','Continue','Stop','Rise','Fall','Migration','true_loop'])
    return ok,out

def test_reader_off_unified():
    base=form_A(new_operational(7),60,10)
    A=copy.deepcopy(base); B=copy.deepcopy(base)
    for _ in range(80):
        A.pulse('a1'); _=A.DegreeA(); _=A.LawA()
        B.pulse('a1')
    ok=A.dyn_snapshot()==B.dyn_snapshot()
    return ok,dict(equal=ok)

def test_dynamics_off_unified():
    WITNESS_SEED=43
    base=form_A(new_operational(0),60,60)
    ON=copy.deepcopy(base); OFF=copy.deepcopy(base)
    OFF.coupling_enabled=False
    def _pq(F,q):
        cand=[e for e in F.out[q] if F.Avail[e]]
        ks=[F.kappa(e)/F.T for e in cand]
        mx=max(ks); ws=[math.exp(k-mx) for k in ks]; z=sum(ws)
        return cand,[w/z for w in ws]
    cand,p_on=_pq(ON,'a1'); _,p_off=_pq(OFF,'a1')
    lo,hi=sorted([p_on[0],p_off[0]])
    u=random.Random(WITNESS_SEED).random()
    in_interval=(lo<u<hi)
    ON.rng=random.Random(WITNESS_SEED); OFF.rng=random.Random(WITNESS_SEED)
    first=None
    for i in range(80):
        ro,_=ON.pulse('a1'); rf,_=OFF.pulse('a1')
        if first is None and ro!=rf: first=(i,ro,rf)
    gd=sum(abs(ON.g[e]-OFF.g[e]) for e in ON.E)
    dd=sum(abs(ON.d[n]-OFF.d[n]) for n in ON.nodes)
    out=dict(witness_seed=WITNESS_SEED,position='a1',edges=[f"{e[0]}->{e[1]}" for e in cand],
             P_on=[round(x,6) for x in p_on],P_off=[round(x,6) for x in p_off],
             separating_interval=(round(lo,6),round(hi,6)),u=round(u,6),u_in_interval=in_interval,
             prob_differs=(p_on!=p_off),same_initial_state=True,same_seed=True,same_protocol=True,
             only_coupling_differs=True,first_divergence=first,sum_abs_g=gd,sum_abs_d=dd)
    ok=(out['prob_differs'] and in_interval and first is not None and gd>0 and dd>0)
    return ok,out

def test_multiseed_50_unified():
    fail=[]
    for seed in range(50):
        R=form_A(new_operational(seed),60,10); prev=R.DegreeA(); rise=False
        for _ in range(80):
            R.pulse('a1'); cur=R.DegreeA()
            if cmp_degree(prev,cur)=='Rise' and R.LawA(): rise=True; break
            prev=cur
        F=form_A(new_operational(seed),60,5); b=F.DegreeA(); l0=F.LawA()
        F.IdleStep(1); F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x']); a=F.DegreeA()
        fall=(cmp_degree(b,a)=='Fall' and l0 and F.LawA())
        M=form_A(new_operational(seed),60,0); M.degree_trace.clear()
        M.SetAvail(('c','r'),1); M.SetAvail(('r','a1'),1); M.SetAvail(('c','o'),0)
        M.pulse('a1'); d1=M.DegreeA(); l1=M.LawA()
        M.SetAvail(('c','r'),0); M.SetAvail(('c','o'),1)
        M.pulse('a1'); d2=M.DegreeA(); l2=M.LawA()
        migration=(cmp_degree(d1,d2)=='Migration' and l1 and l2)
        if not (rise and fall and migration): fail.append((seed,rise,fall,migration))
    return (not fail),dict(passed=50-len(fail),failures=fail)

def test_segment_exec_unified():
    F=form_A(new_operational(13),60,10)
    seg=F.exec_eval_segment([
        ('pulse', {'path':['a1','m1','c']}),
        ('pulse', {'path':['a1','m2','x']}),
        ('pulse', {'path':['a1','m1','c']}),
    ])
    ok=(seg['r_span']>=2 and len([x for x in seg['ops'] if x[0]=='pulse'])>=2
        and seg['legal'] and seg['D0'] is not None and seg['D1'] is not None)
    return ok,seg

def test_pathmap_unified():
    F=form_A(new_operational(17),60,0)
    pm=F.PathMap('L1','R1'); d=F.DegreeA(); expected=(F.d['m1']+F.d['c'])/2.0
    ok=(pm['branch']=='A' and tuple(pm['carrier_edges'])==tuple(R1) and abs(d[0]-expected)<1e-12)
    return ok,dict(pathmap=pm,delta=d[0],expected_delta=expected)

def test_act_legal_segments():
    A=form_A(new_operational(101),60,0)
    out={}; acts=[]; segs=[]
    for path in (['a1','m2','x'],['a1','m1','c'],['a1','m1','c'],['a1','m2','x']):
        seg=A.exec_eval_segment([('pulse',{'path':path})])
        segs.append(seg); acts.append(A.ActionA(list(seg['ops'][0][3])))
    out['acts']=tuple(acts)
    out['Start']=dict(pair=(acts[0],acts[1]),legal=segs[0]['legal'] and segs[1]['legal'],
                      law_trace_ok=all(x[3] for x in segs[0]['law_trace']+segs[1]['law_trace']),
                      zeta=(segs[0]['zeta'],segs[1]['zeta']))
    out['Continue']=dict(pair=(acts[1],acts[2]),legal=segs[1]['legal'] and segs[2]['legal'],
                      law_trace_ok=all(x[3] for x in segs[1]['law_trace']+segs[2]['law_trace']),
                      zeta=(segs[1]['zeta'],segs[2]['zeta']))
    out['Stop']=dict(pair=(acts[2],acts[3]),legal=segs[2]['legal'] and segs[3]['legal'],
                      law_trace_ok=all(x[3] for x in segs[2]['law_trace']+segs[3]['law_trace']),
                      zeta=(segs[2]['zeta'],segs[3]['zeta']))
    ok=(acts==[0,1,1,0] and out['Start']['legal'] and out['Continue']['legal'] and out['Stop']['legal']
        and out['Start']['law_trace_ok'] and out['Continue']['law_trace_ok'] and out['Stop']['law_trace_ok'])
    return ok,out

def test_z_sep_multiseed(nseed=20):
    import copy as _copy
    rows=[]
    for seed in range(nseed):
        B=form_A(new_operational(seed),60,0); B.degree_trace.clear()
        A=_copy.deepcopy(B); C=_copy.deepcopy(B); A.Freeze(); C.Freeze()
        A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x'])
        C.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x']); C.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c'])
        x,y=A.DegreeA(),C.DegreeA()
        rows.append((round(x[2]-y[2],18),
                     abs(x[0]-y[0])<=1e-12 and abs(x[1]-y[1])<=1e-12 and abs(x[3]-y[3])<=1e-12,
                     A.LawA() and C.LawA()))
    vals={r[0] for r in rows}
    ok=(len(vals)==1 and all(r[1] and r[2] for r in rows) and abs(rows[0][0])>1e-12)
    return ok,dict(seeds=nseed,distinct_dZ=sorted(vals),others_zero_all=all(r[1] for r in rows),law_all=all(r[2] for r in rows))

def test_legal_segments_unified():
    out={}
    R=form_A(new_operational(0),60,10); rise=None
    for i in range(80):
        seg=R.exec_eval_segment([('pulse', {})])
        if cmp_degree(seg['D0'],seg['D1'])=='Rise' and seg['legal']:
            rise=(i,seg); break
    out['RiseLegal']=rise is not None
    F=form_A(new_operational(0),60,5)
    fallseg=F.exec_eval_segment([('idle',1),('pulse',{'path':['a1','m2','x']})])
    out['FallLegal']=(cmp_degree(fallseg['D0'],fallseg['D1'])=='Fall' and fallseg['legal'])
    M=form_A(new_operational(0),60,0); M.degree_trace.clear()
    M.SetAvail(('c','r'),1); M.SetAvail(('r','a1'),1); M.SetAvail(('c','o'),0)
    seg1=M.exec_eval_segment([('pulse', {})])
    M.SetAvail(('c','r'),0); M.SetAvail(('c','o'),1)
    seg2=M.exec_eval_segment([('pulse', {})])
    out['MigrationLegal']=(cmp_degree(seg1['D1'],seg2['D1'])=='Migration' and seg1['legal'] and seg2['legal'])
    ok=all(out.values())
    return ok,dict(flags=out,rise=rise,fall=fallseg,migration=(seg1,seg2))

def test_degree_sep_unified():
    import copy as _copy
    tol=1e-12; out={}
    B=form_A(new_operational(1),60,0); B.degree_trace.clear(); B.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c'])
    A=_copy.deepcopy(B); C=_copy.deepcopy(B); A.IdleStep(1); C.IdleStep(5)
    out['delta']=(A.DegreeA(),C.DegreeA(),A.LawA() and C.LawA())
    B=form_A(new_operational(2),60,0); B.degree_trace.clear(); A=_copy.deepcopy(B); C=_copy.deepcopy(B); A.Freeze(); C.Freeze()
    A.SetAvail(('c','r'),1); A.SetAvail(('r','a1'),1); A.SetAvail(('c','o'),0)
    C.SetAvail(('c','r'),0); C.SetAvail(('c','o'),1)
    A.pulse('a1'); C.pulse('a1'); out['reentry']=(A.DegreeA(),C.DegreeA(),A.LawA() and C.LawA())
    B=form_A(new_operational(4),60,0); B.degree_trace.clear(); A=_copy.deepcopy(B); C=_copy.deepcopy(B); A.Freeze(); C.Freeze()
    A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x'])
    C.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x']); C.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c'])
    out['Z']=(A.DegreeA(),C.DegreeA(),A.LawA() and C.LawA())
    B=form_A(new_operational(3),60,0); B.degree_trace.clear(); A=_copy.deepcopy(B); C=_copy.deepcopy(B); A.Freeze(); C.Freeze()
    A.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c','r','a1','m1','c','o'])
    C.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c','r','a1','m1','c','r','a1','m2','x'])
    out['FlowShare']=(A.DegreeA(),C.DegreeA(),A.LawA() and C.LawA())
    targets={'delta':0,'reentry':1,'Z':2,'FlowShare':3}; detail={}; allok=True
    for name,(x,y,law) in out.items():
        dif=[abs(x[i]-y[i]) for i in range(4)]
        t=targets[name]; others=[i for i in range(4) if i!=t]
        ok=law and dif[t]>tol and all(dif[i]<=tol for i in others)
        detail[name]=dict(A=x,B=y,diff=dif,law=law,target=t,ok=ok)
        allok &= ok
    return allok,detail

def test_same_class_subject():
    import inspect,sys
    mod=sys.modules[__name__]
    classes=[n for n,o in vars(mod).items() if inspect.isclass(o)]
    forbidden=[n for n in classes if n=='F']
    return (not forbidden),dict(classes=classes,forbidden=forbidden)

def run_all():
    tests=[
        ('same_class_subject',test_same_class_subject),
        ('regression_full',test_regression_full),
        ('law_state_sensitivity',test_law_state_sensitivity),
        ('g6_crosscheck',test_g6_five_cases),
        ('pathmap_unified',test_pathmap_unified),
        ('segment_exec_unified',test_segment_exec_unified),
        ('act_legal_segments',test_act_legal_segments),
        ('legal_segments_unified',test_legal_segments_unified),
        ('z_sep_multiseed',test_z_sep_multiseed),
        ('degree_sep_unified',test_degree_sep_unified),
        ('six_events_unified',test_six_events_unified),
        ('reader_off_unified',test_reader_off_unified),
        ('dynamics_off_unified',test_dynamics_off_unified),
        ('multiseed_50_unified',test_multiseed_50_unified),
    ]
    total=True
    for name,fn in tests:
        ok,out=fn(); total &= bool(ok)
        print(f'[{name}]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
        print(out)
    print('[FLDB_V1_12_TOTAL]', 'PASS' if total else 'FAIL')
    return total

if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(0 if run_all() else 1)
