~~~python
        I=self.I
        em=None
        if isinstance(rv,dict) and rv.get('event_id') is not None:
            em=dict(event_id=rv.get('event_id'),evidence_key=rv.get('evidence_key'),event_key=rv.get('event_key'),
                    event_run_id=rv.get('run_id'),event_branch_id=rv.get('branch_id'),event_protocol=rv.get('protocol'),
                    route=tuple(rv.get('route',())),kind=rv.get('kind'))
        return dict(seq=self.seq,op=op,pre=pre,post=post,t_run=I.F.t_run,t_sed=I.F.t_sed,
                    run_id=I.run_id,branch_id=I.branch_id,protocol=I.protocol,
                    identity=self._identity_snapshot(),protocol_snapshot=self._protocol_snapshot(),
                    qual=copy.deepcopy(I.qualification()),psnap=self._prob_snapshot(),event=em,
                    act=self._event_act(rv),authoritative=True)
    def _prob_snapshot(self):
        # Probability-side state at this evaluation point (pure read; never writes state).
        P=self.I.P; s=P.snapshot()
        s=dict(s); s['BindStruct']=tuple(sorted(d for d,v in P.bind.items() if v>=P.bind_thr))
        return copy.deepcopy(s)
    def _event_act(self,rv):
        # In-step probability-action unit reading a_u of this operation: 1 when the operation is an
        # event whose same w_rho has current occurrence and duty conformance; 0 when the operation is
        # not an event, or an event without probability occurrence; OPEN when unreadable.
        if not (isinstance(rv,dict) and rv.get('event_id') is not None): return 0
        pe=rv.get('p_event')
        if pe is None: return 0
        occ=self.I.CurrentProbabilityOccurrenceM(pe)
        if occ is False: return 0
        if occ is None: return None
        duty=self.I.ProbabilityDutyConformanceM(pe)
        return 1 if duty is True else (0 if duty is False else None)
    def _call(self,op,fn,*a,**k):
        if op not in self.LEGAL: raise ValueError(('IllegalControllerOp',op))
        pre=self.state_digest(); rv=fn(*a,**k); post=self.state_digest(); self.seq+=1
        self.entries.append(self._rec(op,pre,post,rv)); return rv
    def source_start(self,*a,**k): return self._call('SourceStart',self.I.source_start,*a,**k)
    def source_stop(self,*a,**k): return self._call('SourceStop',self.I.source_stop,*a,**k)
    def gen_gate(self,*a,**k): return self._call('GenGate',self.I.P.do_gen_gate,*a,**k)
    def carrier_link(self,*a,**k): return self._call('CarrierLink',self.I.P.do_carrier_link,*a,**k)
    def intent_avail(self,e,v,*a,**k):
        if tuple(e) not in I_EDGES: raise KeyError(('NotIntentEdge',e))
        return self._call('IntentAvail',self.I.do_avail,e,v,*a,**k)
    def run_avail(self,e,v): return self._call('RunAvail',self.I.F.SetAvail,e,v)
    def org_event(self,*a,**k): return self._call('OrgEvent',self.I.org_event,*a,**k)
    def org_gate_event(self,*a,**k): return self._call('OrgGateEvent',self.I.org_gate_event,*a,**k)
    def external_event(self,*a,**k): return self._call('ExternalEvent',self.I.external_event,*a,**k)
    def external_gate_event(self,*a,**k): return self._call('ExternalGateEvent',self.I.external_gate_event,*a,**k)
    def source_only_event(self,*a,**k): return self._call('SourceOnlyEvent',self.I.source_only_event,*a,**k)
    def freeze(self): return self._call('Freeze',self.I.F.Freeze)
    def thaw(self): return self._call('Thaw',self.I.F.Thaw)
    def idle(self,n=1): return self._call('IdleStep',self.I.F.IdleStep,n)
    def pulse(self,*a,**k): return self._call('Pulse',self.I.F.pulse,*a,**k)
    def point(self):
        return dict(index=len(self.entries)-1,digest=self.state_digest(),t_run=self.I.F.t_run,t_sed=self.I.F.t_sed)
    def span(self,a,b):
        i,j=a.get('index'),b.get('index'); E=self.entries
        readable=isinstance(i,int) and isinstance(j,int) and 0<=i<j<len(E)
        if not readable:
            return dict(readable=False,L1=OPEN,L2=OPEN,L4=OPEN,entries=[],quals=[],events=[])
        seg=E[i+1:j+1]
        chain=(a.get('digest')==E[i]['post'] and all(E[k]['pre']==E[k-1]['post'] for k in range(i+1,j+1)) and b.get('digest')==E[j]['post'])
        legal=all(e.get('op') in self.LEGAL and e.get('authoritative') is True for e in seg)
        same_run=all(e.get('run_id')==self.I.run_id and e.get('branch_id')==self.I.branch_id for e in seg)
        mono=all(E[k]['t_run']>=E[k-1]['t_run'] and E[k]['t_sed']>=E[k-1]['t_sed'] for k in range(i+1,j+1))
        L1=bool(j>i and a.get('digest')==E[i]['post'] and same_run and mono)
        logs=bool(self.I.P.audit_authoritative_ops() and self.I.audit_intent_ops())
        L2=bool(chain and legal and logs)
        qs=[E[k].get('qual') for k in range(i,j+1)]
        cover=all(isinstance(q,dict) and 'Intent' in q for q in qs)
        L4=(ONE if cover and all(q['Intent'] is True for q in qs) else ZERO if cover and any(q['Intent'] is False for q in qs) else OPEN)
        ids=[E[k].get('identity') for k in range(i,j+1)]
        id_cover=all(isinstance(x,dict) for x in ids)
        L3=ONE if id_cover and all(x==ids[0] for x in ids) else ZERO if id_cover else OPEN
        ps=[E[k].get('protocol_snapshot') for k in range(i,j+1)]
        pcover=all(isinstance(x,dict) for x in ps)
        def frozen(p):
            if not p: return False
            return (p.get('idyn',{}).get('status')=='frozen' and
                    all(r.get('status')=='frozen' for r in p.get('intent_history',())) and
                    all(r.get('status')=='frozen' for r in p.get('prob_history',())) and
                    all(r.get('status')=='frozen' for r in p.get('idyn_history',())))
        L6=ONE if pcover and all(x==ps[0] for x in ps) and all(frozen(x) for x in ps) else ZERO if pcover else OPEN
        evmeta=[e['event'] for e in seg if e.get('event') is not None]
        links=[E[k]['pre']==E[k-1]['post'] for k in range(i+1,j+1)]
        psnaps=[copy.deepcopy(E[k].get('psnap')) for k in range(i,j+1)]
        return dict(readable=True,L1=ONE if L1 else ZERO,L2=ONE if L2 else ZERO,L3=L3,L4=L4,L6=L6,links=links,psnaps=psnaps,
                    chain=chain,legal=legal,logs=logs,qual_cover=cover,identity_cover=id_cover,protocol_cover=pcover,
                    entries=copy.deepcopy(seg),quals=copy.deepcopy(qs),events=copy.deepcopy(evmeta),
                    start_qual=copy.deepcopy(qs[0]) if qs else None)
    def close_gap(self): return self.state_digest()!=self.entries[-1]['post']

ONE=1; ZERO=0

def event_covered_by_span(e,span):
    return any(x.get('event_id')==e.get('event_id') and x.get('evidence_key')==e.get('evidence_key') and
               x.get('event_run_id')==e.get('run_id') and x.get('event_branch_id')==e.get('branch_id') and
               x.get('event_protocol')==e.get('protocol') and tuple(x.get('route',()))==tuple(e.get('route',()))
               for x in span.get('events',[]))

def legal_segment_certificate(I,audit,a,b,evidence_events=None):
    """The sole L1-L6 certificate used by judgement, stress and IDyn.
    L1/L2/L3/L4/L6 come from RuntimeAuditV14.span; L5 audits every actually
    bearing evidence record against the same run/branch/anchor/protocol and span event key.
    """
    span=audit.span(a,b)
    if not span.get('readable'):
        return dict(L1=OPEN,L2=OPEN,L3=OPEN,L4=OPEN,L5=OPEN,L6=OPEN,start_qual=None,L5_reason='EvidenceOpen',span=span)
    evs=list(evidence_events or [])
    foreign=False; missing=False
    for e in evs:
        if not isinstance(e,dict) or e.get('evidence_key') is None:
            missing=True; continue
        if (e.get('run_id')!=I.run_id or e.get('branch_id')!=I.branch_id or e.get('anchor')!=I.anchor or e.get('protocol')!=I.protocol):
            foreign=True; continue
        # Event-bearing evidence must be in the same declared runtime span.
        if e.get('event_id') is not None and not event_covered_by_span(e,span): foreign=True
    L5=OPEN if (foreign or missing) else ONE
    return dict(L1=span.get('L1'),L2=span.get('L2'),L3=span.get('L3'),L4=span.get('L4'),L5=L5,L6=span.get('L6'),
                start_qual=span.get('start_qual'),qual_states=span.get('quals'),span=span,
                L5_reason='EvidenceSplice' if foreign else 'EvidenceOpen' if missing else None)

LEGAL_KEYS=('L1','L2','L3','L4','L5','L6')

def legal_gate(audit):
    """Three-valued Read_LegalIDynSeg (sealed IDyn definition 11) with the L4 applicability route.

    Returns (kind, reason) with kind in {'ONE','ZERO','OUT','OPEN'}:
      - all six items OPEN (unreadable / zero-width span)        -> OPEN  SegmentUnreadable
      - L4 = 0 (qualification exits)                             -> OUT   QualExit (applicability boundary,
                                                                        routed by the judge, never IntentDyn=0)
      - otherwise any of L1, L2, L3, L6 = 0  (zero absorption)   -> ZERO  first such item
      - otherwise any item OPEN                                  -> OPEN  QualCoverOpen / EvidenceSplice /
                                                                        EvidenceOpen / Lk Open
      - all six = 1                                              -> ONE
    L5 never takes 0.  Every function that can sign IntentDyn 1 or 0 must pass this gate first."""
    vals={k:audit.get(k) for k in LEGAL_KEYS}
    if all(v is OPEN for v in vals.values()): return ('OPEN','SegmentUnreadable')
    if vals['L4']==ZERO: return ('OUT','QualExit')
    for k in ('L1','L2','L3','L6'):
        if vals[k]==ZERO: return ('ZERO',k)
    if vals['L4'] is OPEN: return ('OPEN','QualCoverOpen')
    if vals['L5'] is OPEN: return ('OPEN',audit.get('L5_reason') or 'EvidenceSplice')
    for k in ('L1','L2','L3','L6'):
        if vals[k] is OPEN: return ('OPEN',f'{k}Open')
    if all(v==ONE for v in vals.values()): return ('ONE',None)
    return ('OPEN','LegalSegOpen')

def _t3(v):
    if v is None: return None
    return 1 if v else 0

def _and3(*vals):
    """Three-valued conjunction: any 0 gives 0; all 1 gives 1; otherwise OPEN."""
    vs=[_t3(v) for v in vals]
    if any(v==0 for v in vs): return 0
    if all(v==1 for v in vs): return 1
    return None

def _or3(vals):
    """Three-valued disjunction: any 1 gives 1; all 0 gives 0; otherwise OPEN."""
    vs=[_t3(v) for v in vals]
    if any(v==1 for v in vs): return 1
    if vs and all(v==0 for v in vs): return 0
    return None

def pdyn_objects(I):
    """Object validators for the probability-side snapshot (sealed PDyn definitions 3.12-3.15 and
    Probability-mathematics definitions 3.18, 3.26, 9.1)."""
    import math
    def fin(x):
        try: return math.isfinite(float(x))
        except Exception: return False
    def rel_obj(s):
        r=s.get('Rel'); V=s.get('V')
        if not isinstance(r,(tuple,list)) or not isinstance(V,(tuple,list)): return None
        try: rr=tuple((str(d),float(w)) for d,w in r)
        except Exception: return None
        if tuple(d for d,_ in rr)!=tuple(str(v) for v in V): return None
        # legal generation weights are nonnegative and finite (definition 3.18)
        if any((not fin(w)) or w<0 for _,w in rr): return None
        z=sum(w for _,w in rr)
        # zero class [0] is a legal relation object; a nonzero class is held in normalized representation
        if z>0 and abs(z-1.0)>1e-7: return None
        return rr
    def dim_org_obj(s):
        V=tuple(s.get('V',())); org=s.get('DimOrg')
        if not isinstance(org,(tuple,list)): return None
        try: org=tuple((str(cid),str(d),str(role)) for cid,d,role in org)
        except Exception: return None
        if len(set(org))!=len(org): return None
        dims={d for _,d,_ in org}
        if not set(V).issubset(dims) or not dims.issubset(set(I.P.dims)): return None
        for cid,d,role in org:
            C=I.P.carrier_candidates.get(cid)
            if C is None or C['dim']!=d or C['role']!=role: return None
        return org
    def bind_obj(s):
        b=s.get('Bind')
        if not isinstance(b,(tuple,list)): return None
        try: b=tuple((str(d),float(v)) for d,v in b)
        except Exception: return None
        ds=[d for d,_ in b]
        # one entry per formal dimension (a function on the formal dims), values nonnegative finite
        if len(ds)!=len(set(ds)) or set(ds)!={str(d) for d in I.P.dims}: return None
        if any((not fin(v)) or v<0 for _,v in b): return None
        st=s.get('BindStruct')
        return (b, tuple(st) if isinstance(st,(tuple,list)) else None)
    def bear_org_obj(s):
        ms=s.get('MinSuff'); org=dim_org_obj(s); rel=rel_obj(s)
        if not isinstance(ms,(tuple,list)) or org is None or rel is None: return None
        try: ms=tuple(tuple(sorted(str(d) for d in G)) for G in ms)
        except Exception: return None
        if not ms or len(set(ms))!=len(ms): return None
        # antichain: inclusion-minimal family (definition 3.26)
        sets=[set(G) for G in ms]
        if any(i!=j and sets[i]<=sets[j] for i in range(len(sets)) for j in range(len(sets))): return None
        V=set(str(d) for d in s.get('V',()))
        zero=all(w==0.0 for _,w in rel)
        support=tuple(sorted(d for d,w in rel if w>0))
        if zero:
            # zero class: the empty set is the only minimal sufficient group, MinSuff = {{}} (9.1)
            if ms!=((),): return None
            return (ms,())
        if any(len(G)==0 for G in ms): return None
        if any(not set(G).issubset(V) for G in ms): return None
        # complete family in this reference (generation factors are the formal dims): {support(Rel)}
        if ms!=(support,): return None
        roles={d:tuple(sorted((cid,role) for cid,dd,role in org if dd==d)) for d in V}
        if any(any(not roles.get(d) for d in G) for G in ms): return None
        return (ms,tuple((G,tuple((d,roles.get(d,())) for d in G)) for G in ms))
    return rel_obj,dim_org_obj,bind_obj,bear_org_obj

def pdyn_solver(I, events, span):
    """PD-M1..PD-M13 and Read_{N_PDyn} for this finite reference specification (v14.5).

    Evaluation points are the RuntimeAudit points of the declared span; every step is one recorded
    operation.  Read_{N_PDyn} = LegalSeg AND3 DynActual (definition 3.18); DynActual = OR3 of the
    action, rate and qualification-structure branches; each branch reads only through its own
    dependency gate and SamePDynBind; an illegal object makes only the branches that depend on it OPEN.
    """
    events=list(events); cond={k:True for k in PD_KEYS}; steps=[]
    rel_obj,dim_org_obj,bind_obj,bear_org_obj=pdyn_objects(I)
    readable=bool(span.get('readable'))
    pts=list(span.get('psnaps') or []) if readable else []
    seg=list(span.get('entries') or []) if readable else []
    links=list(span.get('links') or []) if readable else []
    ev_snaps=[s for e in events for s in ((e.get('p_event') or {}).get('pre'),(e.get('p_event') or {}).get('post')) if isinstance(s,dict)]
    cond['PD-M1']=bool(I.idyn_protocol.get('status')=='frozen' and readable)
    cond['PD-M2']=bool(readable and span.get('L1')==ONE and all(_event_actual(I,e) for e in events))
    cond['PD-M3']=bool(readable and span.get('L2')==ONE and all(event_covered_by_span(e,span) for e in events))
    cond['PD-M4']=bool(I.P.protocol_history and all(r.get('status')=='frozen' for r in I.P.protocol_history))
    cond['PD-M5']=bool(readable and len(pts)==len(seg)+1 and all(isinstance(p,dict) for p in pts) and
                       all(e.get('event_id') is not None and (e.get('p_event') or {}).get('t_run') is not None for e in events))
    cond['PD-M6']=all(isinstance(p,dict) and isinstance(p.get('Probability'),bool) for p in pts+ev_snaps)
    cond['PD-M7']=all(e.get('p_event') is not None and I.CurrentProbabilityOccurrenceM(e['p_event']) is not None for e in events)
    cond['PD-M8']=bool(all((e.get('p_event') or {}).get('kind') in {'org','external'} and e.get('evidence_key') is not None for e in events)
                       and all(x.get('act',None) in (0,1) for x in seg))
    obj_pts=pts+ev_snaps
    cond['PD-M9']=all(rel_obj(p) is not None for p in obj_pts)
    cond['PD-M10']=all(dim_org_obj(p) is not None for p in obj_pts)
    cond['PD-M11']=all(bind_obj(p) is not None for p in obj_pts)
    cond['PD-M12']=all(bear_org_obj(p) is not None for p in obj_pts)
    cond['PD-M13']=all((e.get('p_event') or {}).get('run_id')==I.P.run_id and (e.get('p_event') or {}).get('branch_id')==I.P.branch_id and
                       (e.get('p_event') or {}).get('protocol')==I.P.protocol and e.get('run_id')==I.run_id and e.get('branch_id')==I.branch_id and
                       e.get('anchor')==I.anchor and e.get('evidence_key') is not None for e in events)
    # Read_{LegalSeg} of the probability segment: explicit trajectory / update failure is 0; missing material is OPEN.
    if readable and (span.get('L1')==ZERO or span.get('L2')==ZERO): legal=0
    elif all(cond[k] for k in ('PD-M1','PD-M2','PD-M3','PD-M4','PD-M5')): legal=1
    else: legal=None
    # Every bearing event must be one of the recorded steps of this span.
    ev_ok=all(event_covered_by_span(e,span) for e in events) and cond['PD-M13']
    for k,x in enumerate(seg):
        s0=pts[k] if k<len(pts) else None; s1=pts[k+1] if k+1<len(pts) else None
        K=(I.anchor,I.P.run_id,I.branch_id,'PDynRef-14.5',k,k+1,I.protocol)
        bind=bool(ev_ok and k<len(links) and links[k] and x.get('run_id')==I.run_id and x.get('branch_id')==I.branch_id and
                  x.get('protocol')==I.protocol and x.get('authoritative') is True and isinstance(s0,dict) and isinstance(s1,dict))
        if not bind:
            steps.append(dict(K=K,op=x.get('op'),SamePDynBind=False,Q_Act=None,Q_Rate=None,Q_Dim=None,Q_Bind=None,Q_BearStruct=None)); continue
        P0,P1=s0.get('Probability'),s1.get('Probability')
        contact=(True if (P0 is True or P1 is True) else (False if (P0 is False and P1 is False) else None))
        # Action branch (definition 3.11).  A state point records no action in progress, so A_k = A_{k+1} = 0
        # are recorded readings; the in-step unit a_u is the operation's own action reading.
        a_u=x.get('act',None)
        if P0 is False or P1 is False: step_act='NA'
        elif P0 is not True or P1 is not True: step_act=None
        elif a_u==0: step_act='Idle'
        elif a_u==1: step_act=None   # 0 -> 0 endpoints with an in-step action: grain too coarse, OPEN
        else: step_act=None
        q_act=(1 if step_act in ('Start','Continue','Stop') else 0 if step_act in ('Idle','NA') else None)
        r0,r1=rel_obj(s0),rel_obj(s1)
        same_rel=(I.P._same_rel(r0,r1) if (r0 is not None and r1 is not None) else None)
        q_rate=(None if same_rel is None or contact is None else (1 if (contact and not same_rel) else 0))
        d0,d1=dim_org_obj(s0),dim_org_obj(s1)
        q_dim=(None if d0 is None or d1 is None or contact is None else
               (1 if (contact and (tuple(s0.get('V',()))!=tuple(s1.get('V',())) or d0!=d1)) else 0))
        b0,b1=bind_obj(s0),bind_obj(s1)
        # BindDiff compares the formal bind structure, never the sediment quantities (definition 3.14).
        q_bind=(None if b0 is None or b1 is None or b0[1] is None or b1[1] is None or contact is None else
                (1 if (contact and b0[1]!=b1[1]) else 0))
        o0,o1=bear_org_obj(s0),bear_org_obj(s1)
        if o0 is None or o1 is None or contact is None or same_rel is None: q_bear=None
        else: q_bear=(1 if (contact and same_rel and o0[1]!=o1[1]) else 0)
        steps.append(dict(K=K,op=x.get('op'),SamePDynBind=True,Contact=contact,StepAct=step_act,a_u=a_u,
                          Q_Act=q_act,Q_Rate=q_rate,Q_Dim=q_dim,Q_Bind=q_bind,Q_BearStruct=q_bear,SameRel=same_rel))
    # Branch-local solvability (section 5.2): a missing condition makes only the items that depend on it OPEN.
    dep={'Q_Act':('PD-M6','PD-M7','PD-M8'),'Q_Rate':('PD-M9',),'Q_Dim':('PD-M10',),'Q_Bind':('PD-M11',),
         'Q_BearStruct':('PD-M9','PD-M12')}
    for st in steps:
        for q,ks in dep.items():
            if not all(cond[k] for k in ks): st[q]=None
    act=_or3([st['Q_Act'] for st in steps]) if steps else None
    rate=_or3([st['Q_Rate'] for st in steps]) if steps else None
    qual=_or3([v for st in steps for v in (st['Q_Dim'],st['Q_Bind'],st['Q_BearStruct'])]) if steps else None
    dyn=_or3([act,rate,qual])
    read=_and3(legal,dyn)
    return dict(M11=all(cond.values()),conditions=cond,steps=steps,LegalSeg=legal,
                ActDyn=act,RateDyn=rate,QualStructDyn=qual,DynActual=dyn,Read=read)

def idyn_certificate(I,audit,a,b,events,endpoints):
    legal=legal_segment_certificate(I,audit,a,b,events)
    span=legal['span']
    src=source_window_solver(I,events,span)
    car=carrier_solver(I,endpoints)
    pd=pdyn_solver(I,events,span)
    proto=I.idyn_protocol
    conditions={}
    conditions['IDyn-M1']=bool(legal.get('start_qual') and legal['start_qual'].get('Intent') is True and proto.get('status')=='frozen')
    conditions['IDyn-M2']=bool(span.get('readable') and span.get('L1')==ONE)
    conditions['IDyn-M3']=bool(span.get('readable') and span.get('L2')==ONE)
    conditions['IDyn-M4']=bool(span.get('readable') and span.get('qual_cover') and all(q.get('Intent') is not None for q in span.get('quals',[])))
    conditions['IDyn-M5']=bool(legal.get('L5')==ONE)
    conditions['IDyn-M6']=bool(legal.get('L6')==ONE)
    conditions['IDyn-M7']=bool(proto.get('status')=='frozen' and proto.get('unit_rule')=='event-units-closed' and proto.get('delta_src')==1 and proto.get('delta_act')==1)
    conditions['IDyn-M8']=bool(src['M8'])
    conditions['IDyn-M9']=bool(car['M9'])
    conditions['IDyn-M10']=bool(car['M10'])
    conditions['IDyn-M11']=bool(pd['M11'])
    conditions['IDyn-M12']=bool(src['M12'])
    cov_items={
      'legal_span_readable':span.get('readable') is True,
      'M8_units':src['M8'],'M9_registry_bear':car['M9'],'M10_degree_domain':car['M10'],'M11_PD':pd['M11'],'M12_windows':src['M12'],
      'M3_same_runtime_certificate':conditions['IDyn-M3'],'M4_same_runtime_certificate':conditions['IDyn-M4'],
      'M5_evidence_same_run':conditions['IDyn-M5'],'M6_protocol_frozen':conditions['IDyn-M6'],
      'event_membership':all(event_covered_by_span(e,span) for e in events),
      'candidate_domain_frozen':tuple(I.P.candidate_registry_frozen)==tuple(car['registry'])
    }
    conditions['IDyn-M13']=all(cov_items.values())
    # M14 is an implementation-level differential certificate and is intentionally supplied by
    # reader_noninterference_certificate(); never by a declaration flag.
    return dict(M1=conditions['IDyn-M1'],M2=conditions['IDyn-M2'],M3=conditions['IDyn-M3'],M4=conditions['IDyn-M4'],
                M5=conditions['IDyn-M5'],M6=conditions['IDyn-M6'],M7=conditions['IDyn-M7'],M8=conditions['IDyn-M8'],
                M9=conditions['IDyn-M9'],M10=conditions['IDyn-M10'],M11=conditions['IDyn-M11'],M12=conditions['IDyn-M12'],M13=conditions['IDyn-M13'],
                conditions=conditions,legal=legal,span=span,source=src,carrier=car,pdyn=pd,coverage=cov_items)


def run_integrated_tests(seeds=range(5)):
    failures=[]; checks=0
    def ck(seed,name,c,d=None):
        nonlocal checks
        checks+=1
        if not c: failures.append((seed,name,d))
    for seed in seeds:
        I=build(seed); A=RuntimeAuditV14(I)
        A.source_start(); A.gen_gate('A',1.0); A.gen_gate('X',0.0)
        W0=copy.deepcopy(I); p0=A.point(); e=A.org_event(); p1=A.point(); W1=copy.deepcopy(I)
        c=idyn_certificate(I,A,p0,p1,[e],[{'world':W0},{'world':W1}])
        ck(seed,'shared_cert_positive',all(c[k] for k in ('M3','M4','M8','M9','M10','M11','M12','M13')),c)
        ck(seed,'PD_M3_same_span_positive',c['pdyn']['conditions']['PD-M3'] and c['span']['L2']==1,c)
        ck(seed,'event_identity_ledger_bound',event_covered_by_span(e,c['span']),c['span']['events'])
        J=build(seed+1000); B=RuntimeAuditV14(J); B.source_start(); B.gen_gate('A',1.0); B.gen_gate('X',0.0)
        X0=copy.deepcopy(J); q0=B.point(); J.F.d['m1']+=1e-9; e2=B.org_event(); q1=B.point(); X1=copy.deepcopy(J)
        bad=idyn_certificate(J,B,q0,q1,[e2],[{'world':X0},{'world':X1}])
        ck(seed,'bypass_breaks_IDynM3_and_PDM3',not bad['M3'] and not bad['pdyn']['conditions']['PD-M3'],bad)
        K=build(seed+2000); C=RuntimeAuditV14(K); C.source_start(); C.gen_gate('A',1.0); C.gen_gate('X',0.0)
        Y0=copy.deepcopy(K); r0=C.point(); e3=C.org_event(); r1=C.point(); Y1=copy.deepcopy(K); C.entries[r1['index']]['qual']=None
        qc=idyn_certificate(K,C,r0,r1,[e3],[{'world':Y0},{'world':Y1}])
        ck(seed,'qual_cover_loss_hits_M4_not_M3',qc['M3'] and not qc['M4'] and qc['span']['L4'] is None,qc)
        L=build(seed+3000); D=RuntimeAuditV14(L); D.source_start(); D.gen_gate('A',0.9)
        Z0=copy.deepcopy(L); s0=D.point(); e4=D.org_event(); s1=D.point(); Z1=copy.deepcopy(L); L.P.authoritative_ops[-1]['authoritative']=False
        lc=idyn_certificate(L,D,s0,s1,[e4],[{'world':Z0},{'world':Z1}])
        ck(seed,'prob_log_corruption_breaks_shared_M3',not lc['M3'] and not lc['pdyn']['conditions']['PD-M3'],lc)
        foreign=copy.deepcopy(e); foreign['p_event']['run_id']='foreign-prob-run'; pf=pdyn_solver(I,[foreign],c['span'])
        ck(seed,'foreign_probability_bind_rejected',not pf['M11'] and pf['conditions']['PD-M13'] is False,pf)
        Q=build(seed+4000); E=RuntimeAuditV14(Q); t0=E.point(); E.gen_gate('A',0.0); E.gen_gate('X',0.0); t1=E.point(); sp=E.span(t0,t1)
        ck(seed,'qual_exit_covered_L4_zero',sp['qual_cover'] and sp['L4']==0 and sp['L2']==1,sp)
        T=build(seed+5000); F=RuntimeAuditV14(T); F.source_only_event(); T.F.g[('m1','c')]+=1e-12
        ck(seed,'trailing_bypass_close_gap',F.close_gap(),F.point())
        # v14.2 regression: intent-side Avail must not cross into lower probability carrier edges.
        U=build(seed+6000); before=U.F.Avail[('a1','m1')]; n=len(U.intent_ops)
        try:
            U.do_avail(('a1','m1'),0); refused=False
        except Exception:
            refused=True
        ck(seed,'intent_avail_refuses_lower_carrier',refused and U.F.Avail[('a1','m1')]==before and len(U.intent_ops)==n,(refused,U.F.Avail[('a1','m1')],len(U.intent_ops)))
        # v14.2 regression: a forged intent operator invalidates the unified L2 audit.
        V=build(seed+7000); G=RuntimeAuditV14(V); v0=G.point(); G.source_start(); G.source_only_event(); v1=G.point()
        forged=dict(V.intent_ops[-1]); forged.update(operator='ForgedOp',op_id=999); V.intent_ops.append(forged)
        vl=legal_segment_certificate(V,G,v0,v1,[])
        ck(seed,'forged_intent_operator_breaks_L2',vl['L2']==0,(vl['L2'],V.audit_intent_ops()))
        # The same forged operator must propagate through the IDyn certificate to M3.
        H=build(seed+8000); K=RuntimeAuditV14(H); K.source_start(); h0=K.point(); he=K.org_event(); h1=K.point(); HW0=copy.deepcopy(H); HW1=copy.deepcopy(H)
        forged=dict(H.intent_ops[-1]); forged.update(operator='ForgedOp',op_id=999); H.intent_ops.append(forged)
        hc=idyn_certificate(H,K,h0,h1,[he],[{'world':HW0},{'world':HW1}])
        ck(seed,'forged_intent_operator_breaks_IDynM3',not hc['M3'],hc['M3'])
        # ===== v14.5: PDyn read gate (D3), object legality (D4-D6), action branch (D7) =====
        # D3 three-valued tables
        T3=(1,0,None)
        tbl_and=all(_and3(x,y)==(0 if 0 in (x,y) else 1 if (x,y)==(1,1) else None) for x in T3 for y in T3)
        tbl_or=all(_or3([x,y])==(1 if 1 in (x,y) else 0 if (x,y)==(0,0) else None) for x in T3 for y in T3)
        ck(seed,'D3_three_valued_tables',tbl_and and tbl_or,(tbl_and,tbl_or))
        # D3(a): legal segment 1, rate branch illegal at one point, an independent legal positive
        # (formal dimension organisation changes through a make-before-break carrier handoff) -> 1.
        HM=build(seed+9000); [HM.P.formation_candidate('A_alt',p=2.0) for _ in range(5)]
        AH=RuntimeAuditV14(HM); h0=AH.point(); AH.carrier_link('A_alt',1); AH.carrier_link('A_primary',0); h1=AH.point(); hs=AH.span(h0,h1)
        hs_bad=copy.deepcopy(hs); hs_bad['psnaps'][0]['Rel']=(('A',-0.5),('X',1.5))
        hp=pdyn_solver(HM,[],hs_bad)
        ck(seed,'D3a_illegal_rate_branch_does_not_block_legal_positive',
           hp['LegalSeg']==1 and hp['steps'][0]['Q_Rate'] is None and hp['QualStructDyn']==1 and hp['Read']==1 and not hp['conditions']['PD-M9'],
           (hp['LegalSeg'],[(st['op'],st['Q_Rate'],st['Q_Dim']) for st in hp['steps']],hp['Read']))
        # D3(b): legal segment 1, rate branch illegal, no other branch has a positive -> never 1.
        # (Steps are legal no-op wiring operations, so no branch moves; a source pulse would itself move the rate
        # through decay, which is a genuine RateDyn and therefore not used here.)
        NB=build(seed+9100); AN=RuntimeAuditV14(NB); n0=AN.point(); AN.gen_gate('A',NB.P.gen_gate['A']); AN.gen_gate('X',NB.P.gen_gate['X']); n1=AN.point(); ns=AN.span(n0,n1)
        ns_bad=copy.deepcopy(ns); ns_bad['psnaps'][1]['Rel']=(('A',float('nan')),('X',1.0))
        np_=pdyn_solver(NB,[],ns_bad); np_ok=pdyn_solver(NB,[],ns)
        ck(seed,'D3b_illegal_rate_without_other_positive_not_one',
           np_['LegalSeg']==1 and np_['Read'] is None and np_ok['Read']==0 and np_ok['DynActual']==0,(np_['Read'],np_ok['Read'],np_ok['DynActual']))
        # D3(c): LegalSeg OPEN (probability protocol history no longer frozen) and DynActual fully 0 -> 0, not OPEN.
        LO=build(seed+9200); AL=RuntimeAuditV14(LO); l0=AL.point(); AL.gen_gate('A',LO.P.gen_gate['A']); l1=AL.point(); ls=AL.span(l0,l1)
        LO.P.protocol_history.append(dict(version='ProbRND-2-revised',status='revised',seq=1)); lp=pdyn_solver(LO,[],ls)
        ck(seed,'D3c_open_legal_and_zero_dyn_is_zero',lp['LegalSeg'] is None and lp['DynActual']==0 and lp['Read']==0,(lp['LegalSeg'],lp['DynActual'],lp['Read']))
        # D3(d): LegalSeg 0 (bypass write inside the span) with a structural positive elsewhere -> 0.
        LZ=build(seed+9300); [LZ.P.formation_candidate('A_alt',p=2.0) for _ in range(5)]
        AZ=RuntimeAuditV14(LZ); z0=AZ.point(); LZ.F.d['m1']+=0.01; AZ.carrier_link('A_alt',1); AZ.carrier_link('A_primary',0); z1=AZ.point()
        zp=pdyn_solver(LZ,[],AZ.span(z0,z1))
        ck(seed,'D3d_zero_legal_segment_gives_zero',zp['LegalSeg']==0 and zp['Read']==0,(zp['LegalSeg'],zp['DynActual'],zp['Read']))
        # D3(e): a recorded GenGate step changes Rel on a legal segment: the rate branch signs 1 and so does the read.
        RG=build(seed+9600); AR=RuntimeAuditV14(RG); g0=AR.point(); AR.gen_gate('A',0.4); g1=AR.point(); rp=pdyn_solver(RG,[],AR.span(g0,g1))
        ck(seed,'D3e_rate_positive_from_recorded_step',rp['LegalSeg']==1 and rp['RateDyn']==1 and rp['Read']==1,(rp['LegalSeg'],rp['RateDyn'],rp['Read']))
        # D7(b): the former V40 positive came from a preset Start; on a single organisation-event span the action
        # branch is now OPEN (grain), no other branch moves, so the probability read is OPEN, not 1.
        ck(seed,'D7b_single_org_event_read_open',c['pdyn']['ActDyn'] is None and c['pdyn']['Read'] is None and c['pdyn']['LegalSeg']==1,
           (c['pdyn']['ActDyn'],c['pdyn']['RateDyn'],c['pdyn']['QualStructDyn'],c['pdyn']['Read']))
        # D4 Rel object: negative and non-finite weights are illegal.
        robj,dobj,bobj,oobj=pdyn_objects(I); base=copy.deepcopy(c['span']['psnaps'][0])
        neg=dict(base,Rel=(('A',-0.5),('X',1.5))); nan=dict(base,Rel=(('A',float('nan')),('X',1.0))); inf=dict(base,Rel=(('A',float('inf')),('X',1.0)))
        ck(seed,'D4_rel_nonnegative_finite',robj(base) is not None and robj(neg) is None and robj(nan) is None and robj(inf) is None,(robj(base),robj(neg),robj(nan)))
        # D5 Bind object: one entry per formal dim, nonnegative finite; sediment quantities are not structure.
        dup=dict(base,Bind=tuple(base['Bind'])+tuple(base['Bind'][:1])); bneg=dict(base,Bind=tuple((d,-1.0) for d,_ in base['Bind']))
        org_steps=[st for st in c['pdyn']['steps'] if st['op']=='OrgEvent']
        ck(seed,'D5_bind_unique_finite_structure_not_quantity',
           bobj(base) is not None and bobj(dup) is None and bobj(bneg) is None and org_steps and org_steps[0]['Q_Bind']==0,
           (bobj(dup),bobj(bneg),[st['Q_Bind'] for st in org_steps]))
        # D6 MinSuff: zero-class special case, inclusion-minimality (antichain), no empty group beside a nonzero Rel, complete family.
        eg=dict(base,MinSuff=((),)+tuple(base['MinSuff'])); ac=dict(base,MinSuff=(('A',),('A','X'))); dupg=dict(base,MinSuff=tuple(base['MinSuff'])*2)
        wrongfam=dict(base,MinSuff=(('X',),))
        Z0=build(seed+9400); AZ0=RuntimeAuditV14(Z0); AZ0.gen_gate('A',0.0); AZ0.gen_gate('X',0.0); zsnap=AZ0.entries[-1]['psnap']
        ck(seed,'D6_minsuff_zero_class_antichain_complete',
           oobj(base) is not None and oobj(eg) is None and oobj(ac) is None and oobj(dupg) is None and oobj(wrongfam) is None and
           oobj(zsnap) is not None and oobj(dict(zsnap,MinSuff=(('A',),))) is None,
           (oobj(eg),oobj(ac),oobj(dupg),oobj(wrongfam),zsnap.get('MinSuff')))
        # D7 action branch: a state point records no action in progress (A_k = A_{k+1} = 0, recorded, not inserted);
        # an in-step action between two 0 endpoints is OPEN (grain too coarse), an event without probability
        # occurrence is Idle, and an endpoint with Probability 0 is NA.  No Start is preset.
        AC=build(seed+9500); AA=RuntimeAuditV14(AC); a0=AA.point(); AA.gen_gate('A',1.0); AA.gen_gate('X',0.0); ea=AA.org_event(); ex=AA.external_event('A')
        AA.run_avail(('a1','m1'),0); a1=AA.point(); ap=pdyn_solver(AC,[],AA.span(a0,a1))
        sa={st['op']:st for st in ap['steps']}
        ck(seed,'D7_action_branch_from_recorded_points',
           sa['OrgEvent']['a_u']==1 and sa['OrgEvent']['StepAct'] is None and sa['OrgEvent']['Q_Act'] is None and
           sa['ExternalEvent']['a_u']==0 and sa['ExternalEvent']['StepAct']=='Idle' and sa['ExternalEvent']['Q_Act']==0 and
           sa['RunAvail']['StepAct']=='NA' and sa['RunAvail']['Q_Act']==0 and all(st['StepAct']!='Start' for st in ap['steps']),
           [(st['op'],st['a_u'],st['StepAct'],st['Q_Act']) for st in ap['steps']])
    return dict(seeds=len(list(seeds)),checks=checks,failures=failures,TOTAL_PASS=not failures)

if __name__=='__main__':
    cp=subprocess.run([sys.executable,PROB_PATH],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=240)
    up=(cp.returncode==0 and '[UPSTREAM_FULL] PASS' in cp.stdout and '"TOTAL_PASS": true' in cp.stdout)
    print('[UPSTREAM+PROB_FULL]', 'PASS' if up else 'FAIL')
    if not up:
        print(cp.stdout[-12000:]); print(cp.stderr[-4000:]); raise SystemExit(2)
    r1=run_tests(range(3)); print(json.dumps(r1,indent=2,default=str))
    r2=run_integrated_tests(range(5)); print('[INTEGRATED_IDYN_CERT]',json.dumps(r2,indent=2,default=str))
    ok=bool(r1['TOTAL_PASS'] and r2['TOTAL_PASS'])
    print('[INTENT_IB_FINAL_CORE]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
    raise SystemExit(0 if ok else 1)
~~~

~~~python
import importlib.util, sys, subprocess, copy, json, hashlib
from pathlib import Path

HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
INTENT_PATH=str(HERE/'intent_on_fldb_rnd_v14.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ir',INTENT_PATH)
ir=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=ir; spec.loader.exec_module(ir)

ONE=1; ZERO=0; OPEN=None


def erode_intent_edges(I, targets, max_cycles=20):
    targets=set(targets)
    for _ in range(max_cycles):
        if all(not I.edge_structural(e) for e in targets):
            return True
        I.P.formation_pulse('A'); I.P.formation_pulse('X')
        for e in [I.source_edge,I.gate_link['A'],I.gate_link['X'],I.tune['A'],I.tune['X']]:
            if e in targets: continue
            I.F.pulse(e[0],path=[e[0],e[1]])
    return all(not I.edge_structural(e) for e in targets)

def verdict(v,reason=None,**extra):
    d={'verdict':v,'reason':reason}; d.update(extra); return d

def det_intent_core(q, bridges_closed=False):
    if q['Intent']:
        return verdict(ONE,'PositiveCore',core=q)
    # Current sealed judgement status: probability negative path is conditional/open;
    # new intent atoms have no proven negative representation.
    if not q['P']:
        if bridges_closed:
            return verdict(ZERO,'ProbabilityNegativeClosed',core=q)
        return verdict(OPEN,'ProbabilityOpen',core=q)
    return verdict(OPEN,'IntentAtomRepOpen',core=q)

def det_intent(I, bridges_closed=False):
    return det_intent_core(I.qualification(),bridges_closed=bridges_closed)

def det_action(I,ev,pact_rep_closed=False):
    di=det_intent(I)
    if di['verdict'] is ZERO: return verdict('OutDom','IntentDomainFail')
    if di['verdict'] is OPEN: return verdict(OPEN,'IntentDomainOpen')
    # v14.4: the IA-M1..M12 solvability gate (including the coverage certificate) precedes any 1/0.
    r3=I.A_read3(ev)
    if r3.get('reason') is not None: return verdict(OPEN,r3['reason'],core=r3)
    r=r3
    if r['Read']==1: return verdict(ONE,'N_IAct',core=r)
    # Only an explicit False is a structural failure layer; an OPEN layer keeps the verdict OPEN.
    if r['A1'] is False: return verdict(ZERO,'A1',core=r)
    if r['A2'] is False:
        # If this is a real current org event whose probability duty cannot be closed, preserve open.
        if ev.get('kind')=='org' and not pact_rep_closed: return verdict(OPEN,'PActRepOpen',core=r)
        return verdict(ZERO,'A2-Occurrence',core=r)
    if r['A3'] is False: return verdict(ZERO,'A3',core=r)
    if r['A4'] is False: return verdict(ZERO,'A4',core=r)
    return verdict(OPEN,'CoverageOpen',core=r)


def det_switch(I,prev,cur,exit_coverage=True,pact_rep_closed=False):
    di=det_intent(I)
    if di['verdict'] is ZERO: return verdict('OutDom','IntentDomainFail')
    if di['verdict'] is OPEN: return verdict(OPEN,'IntentDomainOpen')
    # Missing old-gate exit record is representation-open, not S4 structural zero.
    if cur.get('old_gate_exit') is None or not exit_coverage:
        return verdict(OPEN,'GateExitOpen')
    # v14.4: the IGS-M1..M10 solvability gate precedes any 1/0.
    r3=I.switch_read3(prev,cur)
    if r3.get('reason') is not None: return verdict(OPEN,r3['reason'],core=r3)
    r=r3
    if r['Read']==1: return verdict(ONE,'N_IGS',core=r)
    if r['S1'] is False: return verdict(ZERO,'S1',core=r)
    if r['S2'] is not True:
        # S2's A2-PDuty subpath can be open; an OPEN layer is never a structural zero.
        ar=I.A_read(cur)
        if ar['A1'] and ar['A2'] is not True and cur.get('kind')=='org' and not pact_rep_closed:
            return verdict(OPEN,'PActRepOpen',core=r)
        if r['S2'] is None or any(ar[k] is None for k in ('A1','A2','A3')): return verdict(OPEN,'S2Open',core=r)
        return verdict(ZERO,'S2',core=r)
    if r['S3'] is False: return verdict(ZERO,'S3',core=r)
    if r['S4'] is False: return verdict(ZERO,'S4',core=r)
    if r['B_same'] is False: return verdict(ZERO,'B_same',core=r)
    return verdict(OPEN,'CoverageOpen',core=r)

def det_dyn_from_audit(I,audit,source_dyn=False,carrier_dyn=False,prob_dyn=False,
                       probability_negative_closed=False,carrier_negative_closed=False,
                       source_negative_closed=True,qual_failure_kind=None):
    # Entry judgement must be made from the same declared object's start state, not
    # from the object's current endpoint and never from a proxy object.
    # An unreadable certificate span carries no legal-segment material at all.
    if all(audit.get(k) is OPEN for k in ir.LEGAL_KEYS):
        return verdict(OPEN,'SegmentUnreadable',audit=audit)
    start_core=audit.get('start_qual')
    di=det_intent_core(start_core,bridges_closed=probability_negative_closed) if start_core is not None else det_intent(I,bridges_closed=probability_negative_closed)
    if di['verdict'] is ZERO: return verdict('OutDom','StartDomainFail',audit=audit)
    if di['verdict'] is OPEN: return verdict(OPEN,'StartDomainOpen',audit=audit)
    # Shared three-valued legal-segment gate (zero absorption; OPEN blocks both 1 and 0).
    kind,reason=ir.legal_gate(audit)
    if kind=='OUT':
        # L4 applicability boundary, never an IntentDyn=0 route.
        if qual_failure_kind=='Probability' and probability_negative_closed:
            return verdict('OutDom','QualExit',audit=audit)
        return verdict(OPEN,'QualExitOpen',audit=audit)
    if kind=='ZERO': return verdict(ZERO,reason,audit=audit)
    if kind=='OPEN': return verdict(OPEN,reason,audit=audit)
    sites=(source_dyn,carrier_dyn,prob_dyn)
    if any(v is True for v in sites): return verdict(ONE,'DynActual',audit=audit,sites=sites)
    if any(v is None for v in sites): return verdict(OPEN,'DynSiteOpen',audit=audit,sites=sites)
    # Three zeros require the negative paths for carrier/probability to be closed.
    if not carrier_negative_closed: return verdict(OPEN,'CarrierNegOpen',audit=audit,sites=sites)
    if not probability_negative_closed: return verdict(OPEN,'PDynNegOpen',audit=audit,sites=sites)
    if not source_negative_closed: return verdict(OPEN,'SourceOpen',audit=audit,sites=sites)
    return verdict(ZERO,'DynActual',audit=audit,sites=sites)


def legal_segment_audit(I,audit,start,end,evidence_events=None):
    return ir.legal_segment_certificate(I,audit,start,end,evidence_events or [])

def run_matrix(seeds=range(5)):
    failures=[]; checks=0; observations=[]
    def ck(seed,name,cond,detail=None):
        nonlocal checks
        checks+=1
        if not cond: failures.append((seed,name,detail))
    for seed in seeds:
        I=ir.build(seed)
        di=det_intent(I); ck(seed,'J1_intent_positive',di['verdict']==1,di)
        e=I.org_event(); da=det_action(I,e); ck(seed,'J2_action_positive',da['verdict']==1,da)

        W=ir.build(seed+1001); ev=W.org_gate_event(force_dim='A')
        ar=W.A_read(ev); ck(seed,'J3_A4_direct_zero',ar['A1'] and ar['A2'] and ar['A3'] and not ar['A4'],ar)
        da4=det_action(W,ev); ck(seed,'J4_action_A4_zero',da4['verdict']==0 and da4['reason']=='A4',da4)

        S=ir.build(seed+2000)
        S.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); S.P.do_gen_gate('X',1.0); e1=S.org_event()
        S.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); S.P.do_gen_gate('X',0.0); e2=S.org_event()
        sw=S.switch_read(e1,e2); ck(seed,'J5_switch_positive',sw['Read']==1,sw)
        e3=S.org_event(); same=S.switch_read(e2,e3); ck(seed,'J6_same_gate_zero',same['Read']==0 and not same['S3'],same)
        e2m=copy.deepcopy(e2); e2m['old_gate_exit']=None
        swm=S.switch_read(e1,e2m); ck(seed,'J7_missing_exit_not_positive',swm['Read']==0 and not swm['S4'],swm)

        # Every legal-segment test below uses the sole RuntimeAuditV14 span certificate.
        D=ir.build(seed+3000); AD=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(D); p0=AD.point(); sev=AD.source_only_event(); p1=AD.point()
        la=legal_segment_audit(D,AD,p0,p1,[sev])

        AZ2.entries[z0['index']+2]['qual']=None; laz=legal_segment_audit(QZ,AZ2,z0,z1,[])

        # v14.4 D1: every other legal-segment item OPEN also blocks both the positive and the conditional zero.

        observations.append({'seed':seed,'positive_action':da,'switch':sw,'L2':la2,'L3':la3,'L5':la5,'L6':la6,'qual_mid_exit':laq})
    return {'seeds':len(list(seeds)),'checks':checks,'failures':failures,'TOTAL_PASS':not failures,'observations':observations[:1]}

def _jsonable(x):
    if isinstance(x,dict): return {str(k):_jsonable(v) for k,v in x.items()}
    if isinstance(x,(list,tuple,set)): return [_jsonable(v) for v in x]
    return x

if __name__=='__main__':
    cp=subprocess.run([sys.executable,INTENT_PATH],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=300)
    ok=(cp.returncode==0 and '[UPSTREAM+PROB_FULL] PASS' in cp.stdout and '"TOTAL_PASS": true' in cp.stdout)
    print('[FULL_INHERITED_MODEL]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
    if not ok:
        print(cp.stdout[-12000:]); print(cp.stderr[-4000:]); raise SystemExit(2)
    r=run_matrix(range(5)); print(json.dumps(_jsonable(r),indent=2,default=str))
    raise SystemExit(0 if r['TOTAL_PASS'] else 1)
~~~

~~~python
import importlib.util,sys,subprocess,json,copy
from pathlib import Path
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PATH=str(HERE/'intent_on_fldb_rnd_v14.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ir2',PATH); ir=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=ir; spec.loader.exec_module(ir)

OPEN=None

def run(seeds=range(5)):
    failures=[]; checks=0; obs=[]
    def ck(seed,name,cond,detail=None):
        nonlocal checks; checks+=1
        if not cond: failures.append((seed,name,detail))
    for seed in seeds:
        I=ir.build(seed)
        ev=I.org_event()
        c=I.ia_conditions(ev)
        ck(seed,'IA_all_12_positive',len(c)==12 and all(c.values()),c)
        r=I.A_read3(ev); ck(seed,'IA_positive_read',r.get('Read')==1,r)
        # Each solvability condition missing must yield OPEN, never structural 0.
        for k in sorted(c):
            rr=I.A_read3(ev,{k:False})
            ck(seed,f'{k}_missing_to_open',rr.get('Read') is None and rr.get('reason')==k,rr)
        # Object-zero with all M conditions present remains zero (A4 truncated world).
        W=ir.build(seed+1000); tev=W.org_gate_event(force_dim='A')
        tc=W.ia_conditions(tev); tr=W.A_read3(tev)
        ck(seed,'IA_zero_not_open_when_all_M',all(tc.values()) and tr.get('Read')==0 and tr.get('A4') is False,(tc,tr))

        # IGS positive pair and all ten M conditions.
        S=ir.build(seed+2000); S.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); S.P.do_gen_gate('X',1.0); p=S.org_event(); S.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); S.P.do_gen_gate('X',0.0); q=S.org_event()
        gc=S.igs_conditions(p,q); gr=S.switch_read3(p,q)
        ck(seed,'IGS_all_10_positive',len(gc)==10 and all(gc.values()),gc)
        ck(seed,'IGS_positive_read',gr.get('Read')==1,gr)
        for k in sorted(gc):
            rr=S.switch_read3(p,q,{k:False})
            ck(seed,f'{k}_missing_to_open',rr.get('Read') is None and rr.get('reason')==k,rr)
        # Structural no-switch (same gate) with all M present must be 0, not OPEN.
        q2=S.org_event(); gc2=S.igs_conditions(q,q2); gr2=S.switch_read3(q,q2)
        ck(seed,'IGS_structural_zero_with_all_M',all(gc2.values()) and gr2.get('Read')==0 and gr2.get('S3') is False,(gc2,gr2))

        obs.append({'seed':seed,'IA':c,'IGS':gc,'IAread':r,'IGSread':gr})
    return dict(seeds=len(list(seeds)),checks=checks,failures=failures,TOTAL_PASS=not failures,observations=obs[:1])

if __name__=='__main__':
    cp=subprocess.run([sys.executable,PATH],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=300)
    ok=(cp.returncode==0 and '[UPSTREAM+PROB_FULL] PASS' in cp.stdout and '"TOTAL_PASS": true' in cp.stdout)
    print('[FULL_INHERITED_MODEL]', 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')
    if not ok:
        print(cp.stdout[-12000:]); print(cp.stderr[-4000:]); raise SystemExit(2)
    r=run(range(5)); print(json.dumps(r,indent=2,default=str)); raise SystemExit(0 if r['TOTAL_PASS'] else 1)
~~~

~~~python
import importlib.util,sys,subprocess,json,copy
from pathlib import Path
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PATH=str(HERE/'intent_on_fldb_rnd_v14.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ir14',PATH); ir=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=ir; spec.loader.exec_module(ir)
OPEN=None

KEYS=tuple(f'IDyn-M{i}' for i in range(1,14))

def build_cert(seed):
    I=ir.build(seed); A=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(I)
    A.source_start(); A.gen_gate('A',1.0); A.gen_gate('X',0.0)
    W0=copy.deepcopy(I); p0=A.point(); e=A.org_event(); p1=A.point(); W1=copy.deepcopy(I)
    c=ir.idyn_certificate(I,A,p0,p1,[e],[{'world':W0},{'world':W1}])
    return I,A,p0,p1,e,W0,W1,c

def reader_noninterference_world(seed,interfere=False):
    A=ir.build(seed); B=ir.build(seed)
    for X,readers in ((A,False),(B,True)):
        X.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.7)
        if readers:
            for _ in range(5): X.qualification(); X.current_package(); X.P.snapshot(); X.P.Rel(); X.P.minsuff(); X.F.DegreeA()
            if interfere: X.F.d['m1'] += 1e-6
        X.source_only_event()
    def phys(X):
        return (X.F.dyn_snapshot(),tuple(X.F.log),tuple(sorted(X.P.bind.items())),
                tuple((r['operator'],r['dim'],r['before'],r['after']) for r in X.P.authoritative_ops),
                tuple((e.get('kind'),tuple(e.get('route',()))) for e in X.events))
    return phys(A)==phys(B)

def idyn_interface_read(cert,m14,source_dyn=False,carrier_dyn=False,prob_dyn=False):
    c=dict(cert['conditions']); c['IDyn-M14']=bool(m14)
    if not m14: return {'Read':OPEN,'reason':'ReaderImplementationInvalid','conditions':c}
    miss=[k for k in KEYS if not c.get(k)]
    if miss: return {'Read':OPEN,'reason':miss[0],'conditions':c}
    # The solvability gate alone never signs 1/0: the shared legal-segment gate must also pass.
    kind,reason=ir.legal_gate(cert['legal'])
    if kind=='OUT': return {'Read':OPEN,'reason':'QualExitOpen','conditions':c}
    if kind=='ZERO': return {'Read':0,'reason':reason,'conditions':c}
    if kind=='OPEN': return {'Read':OPEN,'reason':reason,'conditions':c}
    return {'Read':1 if any((source_dyn,carrier_dyn,prob_dyn)) else 0,'reason':None,'conditions':c}

def run(seeds=range(5)):
    failures=[]; checks=0; obs=[]
    def ck(seed,name,c,d=None):
        nonlocal checks; checks+=1
        if not c: failures.append((seed,name,d))
    for seed in seeds:
        I,A,p0,p1,e,W0,W1,c=build_cert(seed); ni=reader_noninterference_world(seed+10000)
        ck(seed,'IDyn_all_14_positive',all(c['conditions'].get(k) for k in KEYS) and ni,c)
        r=idyn_interface_read(c,ni,source_dyn=True); ck(seed,'IDyn_positive_gate',r['Read']==1,r)

        # M1: declared location is unknown -> three-valued qualification cannot enter the solver domain.
        M=ir.build(seed+100); M.intent_loc_declared=None; AM=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(M); a0=AM.point(); me=AM.source_only_event(); a1=AM.point(); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,AM,a0,a1,[me],[{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)},{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M1_actual_location_open',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M1'] and M.qualification()['Intent'] is None,mc)
        # M2: runtime identity changes midspan.
        M=ir.build(seed+200); AM=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(M); a0=AM.point(); AM.source_only_event(); a1=AM.point(); AM.entries[a1['index']]['run_id']='foreign-run'; mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,AM,a0,a1,[],[{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)},{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M2_actual_trajectory_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M2'],mc)
        # M3: unlogged state write in the declared span.
        M=ir.build(seed+300); AM=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(M); a0=AM.point(); M.F.d['m1']+=1e-9; me=AM.source_only_event(); a1=AM.point(); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,AM,a0,a1,[me],[{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)},{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M3_actual_write_audit_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M3'],mc)
        # M4: remove a qualification record from the sole ledger.
        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+400); A2.entries[q1['index']]['qual']=None; mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M4_actual_qual_cover_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M4'],mc)
        # M5: bearing evidence from another run.
        foreign=copy.deepcopy(e); foreign['run_id']='foreign'; mc=ir.idyn_certificate(I,A,p0,p1,[foreign],[{'world':W0},{'world':W1}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M5_actual_evidence_bind_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M5'],mc)
        # M6: post-start protocol drift.
        M=ir.build(seed+600); AM=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(M); a0=AM.point(); M.idyn_protocol['coverage_rule']='posthoc'; AM.source_only_event(); a1=AM.point(); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,AM,a0,a1,[],[{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)},{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M6_actual_protocol_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M6'],mc)
        # M7: frozen grain is not the declared unit grain.
        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+700); M.idyn_protocol['delta_src']=2; mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M7_actual_grain_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M7'],mc)
        # M8: destroy the actual source-chain representation on the recorded event.
        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+800); me['chains']=None; mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M8_actual_source_read_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M8'],mc)
        # M9: frozen candidate registry no longer matches the discovered candidate domain.
        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+900); M.P.candidate_registry_frozen=tuple(); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M9_actual_carrier_registry_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M9'],mc)
        # M10: legal carrier handoff removes A_primary from one endpoint, leaving no same-carrier Degree domain.
        M=ir.build(seed+1000); [M.P.formation_candidate('A_alt',p=2.0) for _ in range(5)]; AM=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(M); AM.source_start(); AM.gen_gate('A',1.0); AM.gen_gate('X',0.0); Wb=copy.deepcopy(M); q0=AM.point(); AM.carrier_link('A_alt',1); AM.carrier_link('A_primary',0); me=AM.org_event(); q1=AM.point(); We=copy.deepcopy(M); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,AM,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':Wb},{'world':We}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M10_actual_degree_domain_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M10'],mc)
        # M11: malformed Rel object defeats PD-M9 reconstruction, not merely a missing-key toggle.
        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+1100); me['p_event']['pre']['Rel']=(('A',2.0),('X',2.0)); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])
        ck(seed,'IDyn-M11_actual_PD_solver_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M11'] and not mc['pdyn']['conditions']['PD-M9'],mc)
        # PD-M10/11/12 each require a reconstructible formal object, not key presence.
        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+1110); me['p_event']['pre']['DimOrg']=(('ghost','A','ProbDir:A'),('X_primary','X','ProbDir:X')); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])
        ck(seed,'PD-M10_invalid_dimension_org_rejected',not mc['pdyn']['conditions']['PD-M10'] and not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M11'],mc)
        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+1120); me['p_event']['pre']['Bind']=(('A',1.0),); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])

        M,A2,q0,q1,me,MW0,MW1,mc=build_cert(seed+1130); me['p_event']['pre']['MinSuff']=(('A','Y'),); mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,A2,q0,q1,[me],[{'world':MW0},{'world':MW1}])
        ck(seed,'PD-M12_invalid_rate_bear_org_rejected',not mc['pdyn']['conditions']['PD-M12'] and not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M11'],mc)

        ck(seed,'IDyn-M13_actual_coverage_fail',not mc['conditions']['IDyn-M13'],mc)

        ck(seed,'IDyn-M14_failure_special_open',not ni_bad and rr['Read'] is None and rr['reason']=='ReaderImplementationInvalid',rr)
        # v14.4: a qualification point that is OPEN inside the span fails M4 and the read stays OPEN.

        mc=ir.idyn_certificate(M,AM,a0,a1,[me],[{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)},{'world':copy.deepcopy(M)}]); mr=idyn_interface_read(mc,ni,source_dyn=True)

    if isinstance(x,(list,tuple,set)): return [_jsonable(v) for v in x]
~~~

~~~python
import importlib.util,sys,subprocess,json,copy
from pathlib import Path
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PATH=str(HERE/'intent_on_fldb_rnd_v14.py')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ir3',PATH); ir=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=ir; spec.loader.exec_module(ir)
m=ir.pr.m


def erode_intent_edges(I, targets, max_cycles=20):
    targets=set(targets)
    for _ in range(max_cycles):
        if all(not I.edge_structural(e) for e in targets): return True
        I.P.formation_pulse('A'); I.P.formation_pulse('X')
        for e in [I.source_edge,I.gate_link['A'],I.gate_link['X'],I.tune['A'],I.tune['X']]:
            if e in targets: continue
            I.F.pulse(e[0],path=[e[0],e[1]])
    return all(not I.edge_structural(e) for e in targets)

def reform_intent_edge(I, edge, max_cycles=20):
    for _ in range(max_cycles):
        if I.edge_structural(edge): return True
        I.P.formation_pulse('A'); I.P.formation_pulse('X')
        I.F.pulse(edge[0],path=[edge[0],edge[1]])
        for e in [I.source_edge,I.gate_link['A'],I.gate_link['X'],I.tune['A'],I.tune['X']]:
            if e==edge: continue
            I.F.pulse(e[0],path=[e[0],e[1]])
    return I.edge_structural(edge)

def carrier_only(I):
    I.F.degree_trace.clear(); I.F.Freeze()
    for _ in range(6):
        I.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m1','c']); I.F.pulse('a1',path=['a1','m2','x'])
    p0=I.P.snapshot(); d0=I.F.DegreeA(); I.F.pulse('u',path=['u','y','w']); p1=I.P.snapshot(); d1=I.F.DegreeA()
    pd=I.P.dyn_compare(p0,p1,False,False)
    return dict(source=False,carrier=(m.cmp_degree(d0,d1) in ('Rise','Fall','Migration')),
                prob=bool(pd['ActDyn'] or pd['RateDyn'] or pd['DimStructDyn'] or pd['BindStructDyn'] or pd['RateBearingStructDyn']),
                degree=(d0,d1),pd=pd)

def prob_only(I, value=0.2):
    d0=I.F.DegreeA(); p0=I.P.snapshot(); I.P.do_gen_gate('A',value); p1=I.P.snapshot(); d1=I.F.DegreeA(); pd=I.P.dyn_compare(p0,p1,False,False)
    return dict(source=False,carrier=(m.cmp_degree(d0,d1) in ('Rise','Fall','Migration')),
                prob=bool(pd['ActDyn'] or pd['RateDyn'] or pd['DimStructDyn'] or pd['BindStructDyn'] or pd['RateBearingStructDyn']),pd=pd,rel=(p0['Rel'],p1['Rel']))

def run(seeds=range(5)):
    failures=[]; checks=0; obs=[]
    def ck(seed,name,cond,detail=None):
        nonlocal checks; checks+=1
        if not cond: failures.append((seed,name,detail))
    for seed in seeds:
        # 1 Probability hard boundary, other three qualification atoms held.
        I=ir.build(seed); q0=I.qualification(); AI=ir.RuntimeAuditV14(I); AI.run_avail(('a1','m1'),0); q1=I.qualification()
        ck(seed,'LIM1_probability_boundary',q0['Intent'] and (not q1['P']) and q1['SourceDrive'] and q1['GateCorrespondence'] and q1['TuneInterface'] and not q1['Intent'],(q0,q1))

        # 2 SourceDrive hard boundary while Probability remains.
        S=ir.build(seed+100); s0=S.qualification(); assert erode_intent_edges(S,[S.source_edge]); s1=S.qualification()
        ck(seed,'LIM2_source_boundary',s0['Intent'] and s1['P'] and not s1['SourceDrive'] and s1['GateCorrespondence'] and s1['TuneInterface'] and not s1['Intent'],(s0,s1))

        # 3 no manifestation is compatible: manifestation is not an input to the qualification kernel at all.
        M=ir.build(seed+200); manifest_state=0; mq=M.qualification()
        ck(seed,'LIM3_no_manifestation_compatible',manifest_state==0 and mq['Intent'] and not hasattr(M,'manifestation'),mq)

        # 4 exact zero current rate for one formal dimension while qualification remains.
        Z=ir.build(seed+300); Z.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); zq=Z.qualification(); rel=dict(Z.P.Rel())
        ck(seed,'LIM4_zero_rate_dim_compatible',zq['Intent'] and abs(rel.get('A',1.0))<1e-15 and set(Z.P.V())=={'A','X'},(zq,rel,Z.P.minsuff()))

        # 5 action A4 boundary: A1-A3 real, no tune occurrence; qualification remains.
        A=ir.build(seed+400); ae=A.org_gate_event(force_dim='A'); ar=A.A_read(ae); aq=A.qualification()
        ck(seed,'LIM5_A4_boundary',aq['Intent'] and ar['A1'] and ar['A2'] and ar['A3'] and not ar['A4'] and ar['Read']==0,(aq,ar,ae['route']))

        # 6 switch without full action: post state has S1-S4+bind but A4=0.
        G=ir.build(seed+500); G.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); G.P.do_gen_gate('X',1.0); g0=G.org_event(); G.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); G.P.do_gen_gate('X',0.0); g1=G.org_gate_event(force_dim='A')
        sw=G.switch_read(g0,g1); ga=G.A_read(g1)
        ck(seed,'LIM6_switch_without_full_action',G.qualification()['Intent'] and sw['Read']==1 and ga['A1'] and ga['A2'] and ga['A3'] and not ga['A4'],(sw,ga,g0['gate_after'],g1['gate_after']))

        # 7 external forced gate relocation: physical gate change, no intent source chain, so S2 fails.
        E=ir.build(seed+600); E.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.0); E.P.do_gen_gate('X',1.0); e0=E.org_event(); e1=E.external_gate_event('A'); esw=E.switch_read(e0,e1)
        ck(seed,'LIM7_external_gate_not_IGS',E.qualification()['Intent'] and esw['S1'] and (not esw['S2']) and esw['S3'] and esw['S4'] and esw['Read']==0,(esw,e0['route'],e1['route']))

        # 8 source site absent while carrier site moves: IntentDyn remains positive by disjunction.
        C=ir.build(seed+700); co=carrier_only(C)
        ck(seed,'LIM8_single_site_loss_not_dyn_exit',(not co['source']) and co['carrier'] and (not co['prob']),co)

        # 9 all three sites quiet in a legal qualified state: object-level DynActual=0.
        N=ir.build(seed+800); p0=N.P.snapshot(); d0=N.F.DegreeA(); p1=N.P.snapshot(); d1=N.F.DegreeA(); pd=N.P.dyn_compare(p0,p1,False,False)
        dyn0=(False, m.cmp_degree(d0,d1)!='Equal', bool(pd['ActDyn'] or pd['RateDyn'] or pd['DimStructDyn'] or pd['BindStructDyn'] or pd['RateBearingStructDyn']))
        ck(seed,'LIM9_all_sites_quiet',N.qualification()['Intent'] and dyn0==(False,False,False),(dyn0,pd,d0,d1))

        # 10 outside write: actual state mutation outside allowed operation history.
        W=ir.build(seed+900); before=W.F.d['m1']; tr=W.F.t_run; ts=W.F.t_sed; W.F.d['m1']+=0.111; after=W.F.d['m1']
        ck(seed,'LIM10_outside_write_L2',before!=after and W.F.t_run==tr and W.F.t_sed==ts,(before,after,tr,ts))

        # 11 qualification exits inside a declared segment; object applicability ends, not IntentDyn=0.
        Q=ir.build(seed+1000); qpre=Q.qualification(); assert erode_intent_edges(Q,[Q.source_edge]); qpost=Q.qualification()
        ck(seed,'LIM11_qual_exit_L4_boundary',qpre['Intent'] and not qpost['Intent'] and qpost['P'],(qpre,qpost,Q.F.t_run,Q.F.t_sed))

        # 12 evidence splice remains epistemic/formal, not an object-level dynamics boundary.
        # Object segment here is carrier-only positive; separately mark a foreign evidence record.
        X=ir.build(seed+1100); xo=carrier_only(X); foreign={'run_id':'foreign','evidence_key':('foreign',1)}
        ck(seed,'LIM12_evidence_splice_not_object_boundary',xo['carrier'] and foreign['run_id']!=X.run_id,(xo,foreign))

        # 13 endpoint equality does not imply no dynamics: A gate 1 -> .2 -> 1; endpoints same, both adjacent steps RateDyn.
        R=ir.build(seed+1200); r0=R.P.snapshot(); R.P.do_gen_gate('A',0.2); r1=R.P.snapshot(); R.P.do_gen_gate('A',1.0); r2=R.P.snapshot()
        d01=R.P.dyn_compare(r0,r1,False,False); d12=R.P.dyn_compare(r1,r2,False,False)
        ck(seed,'LIM13_endpoint_equal_mid_dyn',R.P._same_rel(r0['Rel'],r2['Rel']) and d01['RateDyn'] and d12['RateDyn'] and R.qualification()['Intent'],(r0['Rel'],r1['Rel'],r2['Rel'],d01,d12))

        # 14 site migration across a predeclared comparison family: source-only member -> probability-only member; both remain dynamics-positive.
        F1=ir.build(seed+1300); se=F1.source_only_event(); src_member=(se['route']==('sI','a1'))
        F2=ir.build(seed+1400); po=prob_only(F2,0.2)
        ck(seed,'LIM14_site_migration_not_exit',src_member and po['prob'] and not po['source'] and not po['carrier'],(se,po))

        # 15 boundary-value-only discipline: a finite positive sequence alone does not certify endpoint attainment.
        # Construct positive rates approaching zero without evaluating the zero endpoint in this comparison family.
        B=ir.build(seed+1500); vals=[]
        for n in (1,2,3,4,5):
            B.P.do_gen_gate('A',10.0**(-n)); vals.append(dict(B.P.Rel())['A'])
        inf_est=0.0; endpoint_attained=any(v==0.0 for v in vals)
        ck(seed,'LIM15_infimum_not_attainment',all(v>0 for v in vals) and vals[-1]<vals[0] and inf_est==0.0 and not endpoint_attained,vals)

        # 16 1->0->1 requires resegmentation; restoring availability reforms qualification but does not erase prior exit.
        RF=ir.build(seed+1600); a=RF.qualification(); assert erode_intent_edges(RF,[RF.source_edge]); b=RF.qualification(); t_exit=RF.F.t_run; assert reform_intent_edge(RF,RF.source_edge); c=RF.qualification(); t_reform=RF.F.t_run
        exit_record=(a['Intent'] and not b['Intent']); reform_record=(not b['Intent'] and c['Intent'])
        ck(seed,'LIM16_reformation_requires_new_segment',exit_record and reform_record and t_exit<t_reform,(a,b,c,t_exit,t_reform))

        # 17 compound two-axis boundary: compare a separately frozen candidate with SourceDrive and GateCorrespondence both failed.
        D=ir.build(seed+1700); assert erode_intent_edges(D,[D.source_edge,D.gate_link['A']])
        dq=D.qualification(); failset={k for k in ('SourceDrive','GateCorrespondence') if not dq[k]}
        ck(seed,'LIM17_dual_axis_compound_boundary',failset=={'SourceDrive','GateCorrespondence'} and not dq['Intent'],(dq,failset))

        obs.append({'seed':seed,'zero_rate':(zq,rel),'A4':ar,'switch_no_A4':(sw,ga),'carrier_only':co,'endpoint_equal':(r0['Rel'],r1['Rel'],r2['Rel']),'reform':(a,b,c)})
    return {'seeds':len(list(seeds)),'checks':checks,'failures':failures,'TOTAL_PASS':not failures,'observations':obs[:1]}

    if isinstance(x,(list,tuple,set)): return [_jsonable(v) for v in x]

    ok=(cp.returncode==0 and '[UPSTREAM+PROB_FULL] PASS' in cp.stdout and '"TOTAL_PASS": true' in cp.stdout)
~~~
